--- FILE: boards/arm/lpc17xx_40xx/open1788/scripts/ld.script ---
     1	/****************************************************************************
     2	 * boards/arm/lpc17xx_40xx/open1788/scripts/ld.script
     3	 *
     4	 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     5	 *
     6	 * Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one or more
     7	 * contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file distributed with
     8	 * this work for additional information regarding copyright ownership.  The
     9	 * ASF licenses this file to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
    10	 * "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance with the
    11	 * License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
    12	 *
    13	 *   http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
    14	 *
    15	 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
    16	 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT
    17	 * WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.  See the
    18	 * License for the specific language governing permissions and limitations
    19	 * under the License.
    20	 *
    21	 ****************************************************************************/
    22	
    23	/* The LPC1788 has 512Kb of FLASH beginning at address 0x0000:0000 and
    24	 * 96Kb of total SRAM: 64Kb of SRAM in the CPU block beginning at address
    25	 * 0x10000000 and 32Kb of Peripheral SRAM in two banks, 8Kb at addresses
    26	 * 0x20000000 bank0 first and 8kb at 0x20002000 at bank0 second. And 16Kb
    27	 * at 0x20004000 on bank1.
    28	 *
    29	 * Here we assume that .data and .bss will all fit into the 64Kb CPU SRAM
    30	 * address range.
    31	 */
    32	
    33	MEMORY
    34	{
    35	    FLASH (rx) : ORIGIN = 0x00000000, LENGTH = 512K
    36	    SRAM (rwx) : ORIGIN = 0x10000000, LENGTH = 64K
    37	    AHBRAM8_B0A(rwx): ORIGIN = 0x20000000, LENGTH = 8K
    38	    AHBRAM8_B0B(rwx): ORIGIN = 0x20002000, LENGTH = 8K
    39	    AHBRAM16(rwx): ORIGIN = 0x20004000, LENGTH = 16K
    40	}
    41	
    42	OUTPUT_ARCH(arm)
    43	EXTERN(_vectors)
    44	ENTRY(_stext)
    45	SECTIONS
    46	{
    47	    .text : {
    48	        _stext = ABSOLUTE(.);
    49	        *(.vectors)
    50	        *(.text .text.*)
    51	        *(.fixup)
    52	        *(.gnu.warning)
    53	        *(.rodata .rodata.*)
    54	        *(.gnu.linkonce.t.*)
    55	        *(.glue_7)
    56	        *(.glue_7t)
    57	        *(.got)
    58	        *(.gcc_except_table)
    59	        *(.gnu.linkonce.r.*)
    60	        _etext = ABSOLUTE(.);
    61	    } > FLASH
    62	
    63	    .init_section : ALIGN(4) {
    64	        _sinit = ABSOLUTE(.);
    65	        KEEP(*(SORT_BY_INIT_PRIORITY(.init_array.*) SORT_BY_INIT_PRIORITY(.ctors.*)))
    66	        KEEP(*(.init_array EXCLUDE_FILE(*crtbegin.o *crtbegin?.o *crtend.o *crtend?.o) .ctors))
    67	        _einit = ABSOLUTE(.);
    68	    } > FLASH
    69	
    70	    .ARM.extab : ALIGN(4) {
    71	        *(.ARM.extab*)
    72	    } > FLASH
    73	
    74	    .ARM.exidx : ALIGN(4) {
    75	        __exidx_start = ABSOLUTE(.);
    76	        *(.ARM.exidx*)
    77	        __exidx_end = ABSOLUTE(.);
    78	    } > FLASH
    79	
    80	    _eronly = ABSOLUTE(.);
    81	
    82	    .data : ALIGN(4) {
    83	        _sdata = ABSOLUTE(.);
    84	        *(.data .data.*)
    85	        *(.gnu.linkonce.d.*)
    86	        CONSTRUCTORS
    87	        . = ALIGN(4);
    88	        _edata = ABSOLUTE(.);
    89	    } > SRAM AT > FLASH
    90	
    91	    .bss : ALIGN(4) {
    92	        _sbss = ABSOLUTE(.);
    93	        *(.bss .bss.*)
    94	        *(.gnu.linkonce.b.*)
    95	        *(COMMON)
    96	        . = ALIGN(8);
    97	        _ebss = ABSOLUTE(.);
    98	    } > SRAM
    99	
   100	    /* Stabs debugging sections */
   101	
   102	    .stab 0 : { *(.stab) }
   103	    .stabstr 0 : { *(.stabstr) }
   104	    .stab.excl 0 : { *(.stab.excl) }
   105	    .stab.exclstr 0 : { *(.stab.exclstr) }
   106	    .stab.index 0 : { *(.stab.index) }
   107	    .stab.indexstr 0 : { *(.stab.indexstr) }
   108	    .comment 0 : { *(.comment) }
   109	    .debug_abbrev 0 : { *(.debug_abbrev) }
   110	    .debug_info 0 : { *(.debug_info) }
   111	    .debug_line 0 : { *(.debug_line) }
   112	    .debug_pubnames 0 : { *(.debug_pubnames) }
   113	    .debug_aranges 0 : { *(.debug_aranges) }
   114	}

--- FILE: boards/arm/lpc17xx_40xx/open1788/include/board.h ---
     1	/****************************************************************************
     2	 * boards/arm/lpc17xx_40xx/open1788/include/board.h
     3	 *
     4	 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     5	 *
     6	 * Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one or more
     7	 * contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file distributed with
     8	 * this work for additional information regarding copyright ownership.  The
     9	 * ASF licenses this file to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
    10	 * "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance with the
    11	 * License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
    12	 *
    13	 *   http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
    14	 *
    15	 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
    16	 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT
    17	 * WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.  See the
    18	 * License for the specific language governing permissions and limitations
    19	 * under the License.
    20	 *
    21	 ****************************************************************************/
    22	
    23	#ifndef __BOARDS_ARM_LPC17XX_40XX_OPEN1788_INCLUDE_BOARD_H
    24	#define __BOARDS_ARM_LPC17XX_40XX_OPEN1788_INCLUDE_BOARD_H
    25	
    26	/****************************************************************************
    27	 * Included Files
    28	 ****************************************************************************/
    29	
    30	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    31	
    32	#include <stdbool.h>
    33	
    34	#if defined(CONFIG_ARCH_IRQBUTTONS) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ)
    35	#  include <nuttx/irq.h>
    36	#endif
    37	
    38	/****************************************************************************
    39	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    40	 ****************************************************************************/
    41	
    42	/* Clocking *****************************************************************/
    43	
    44	/* NOTE:
    45	 * The following definitions require lpc17_40_syscon.h.
    46	 * It is not included here because the including C file may not have that
    47	 * file in its include path.
    48	 */
    49	
    50	#define BOARD_XTAL_FREQUENCY       (12000000)            /* XTAL oscillator frequency */
    51	#define BOARD_OSCCLK_FREQUENCY     BOARD_XTAL_FREQUENCY  /* Main oscillator frequency */
    52	#define BOARD_RTCCLK_FREQUENCY     (32768)               /* RTC oscillator frequency */
    53	#define BOARD_INTRCOSC_FREQUENCY   (4000000)             /* Internal RC oscillator frequency */
    54	#define BOARD_WDTOSC_FREQUENCY     (500000)              /* WDT oscillator frequency */
    55	
    56	/* This is the clock setup we configure for:
    57	 *
    58	 * SYSCLK = BOARD_OSCCLK_FREQUENCY = 12MHz  -> Select Main oscillator for
    59	 *                                             source
    60	 * PLL0CLK = (10 * SYSCLK) / 1 = 120MHz -> PLL0 multiplier=10,
    61	 *                                         pre-divider=1
    62	 * CCLCK = 120MHz  -> CCLK divider = 1
    63	 */
    64	
    65	#define LPC17_40_CCLK              120000000 /* 120Mhz */
    66	#define BOARD_PCLKDIV              2         /* Peripheral clock = LPC17_40_CCLK/2 */
    67	#define BOARD_PCLK_FREQUENCY       (LPC17_40_CCLK / BOARD_PCLKDIV)
    68	
    69	/* Select the main oscillator as the frequency source.
    70	 *  SYSCLK is then the frequency of the main oscillator.
    71	 *
    72	 * If BOARD_XTAL_FREQUENCY > 15000000, then the SCS OSCRS bit (bit 4)
    73	 * should also be set in the BOARD_SCS_VALUE.
    74	 */
    75	
    76	#undef CONFIG_LPC17_40_MAINOSC
    77	#define CONFIG_LPC17_40_MAINOSC       1
    78	#define BOARD_SCS_VALUE            SYSCON_SCS_OSCEN
    79	
    80	/* Select the main oscillator and CCLK divider.
    81	 * The output of the divider is CCLK.
    82	 * The input to the divider (PLLCLK) will be determined by the PLL output.
    83	 */
    84	
    85	#define BOARD_CCLKSEL_DIVIDER      1
    86	#define BOARD_CCLKSEL_VALUE        (BOARD_CCLKSEL_DIVIDER | SYSCON_CCLKSEL_CCLKSEL)
    87	
    88	/* PLL0.  PLL0 is used to generate the CPU clock (PLLCLK).
    89	 *
    90	 *  Source clock:               Main oscillator
    91	 *  PLL0 Multiplier value (M):  10
    92	 *  PLL0 Pre-divider value (P): 1
    93	 *
    94	 *  PLL0CLK = (M * SYSCLK) = 120MHz
    95	 */
    96	
    97	#undef CONFIG_LPC17_40_PLL0
    98	#define CONFIG_LPC17_40_PLL0          1
    99	#define BOARD_CLKSRCSEL_VALUE      SYSCON_CLKSRCSEL_MAIN
   100	
   101	#define BOARD_PLL0CFG_MSEL         10
   102	#define BOARD_PLL0CFG_PSEL         1
   103	#define BOARD_PLL0CFG_VALUE \
   104	  (((BOARD_PLL0CFG_MSEL-1) << SYSCON_PLLCFG_MSEL_SHIFT) | \
   105	   ((BOARD_PLL0CFG_PSEL-1) << SYSCON_PLLCFG_PSEL_SHIFT))
   106	
   107	/* PLL1 : PLL1 is used to generate clock for the USB */
   108	
   109	#undef  CONFIG_LPC17_40_PLL1
   110	#define BOARD_PLL1CFG_MSEL        4
   111	#define BOARD_PLL1CFG_PSEL        2
   112	#define BOARD_PLL1CFG_VALUE \
   113	  (((BOARD_PLL1CFG_MSEL-1) << SYSCON_PLLCFG_MSEL_SHIFT) | \
   114	   ((BOARD_PLL1CFG_PSEL-1) << SYSCON_PLLCFG_PSEL_SHIFT))
   115	
   116	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_EMC
   117	/* EMC clock selection.
   118	 *
   119	 * The EMC clock should not be driven above 80MHz.  As a result the EMC
   120	 * uses the CPU clock divided by two.
   121	 */
   122	
   123	#  define BOARD_EMCCLKSEL_DIVIDER  2
   124	#  define BOARD_EMCCLKSEL_VALUE    SYSCON_EMCCLKSEL_CCLK_DIV2
   125	#  define LPC17_40_EMCCLK             (LPC17_40_CCLK / BOARD_EMCCLKSEL_DIVIDER)
   126	#endif
   127	
   128	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_USBHOST) || (CONFIG_LPC17_40_USBDEV)
   129	/* USB divider.  The output of the PLL is used as the USB clock
   130	 *
   131	 *  USBCLK = PLL1CLK = (SYSCLK * 4)  = 48MHz
   132	 */
   133	
   134	#  define BOARD_USBCLKSEL_DIVIDER  1
   135	#  define BOARD_USBCLKSEL_VALUE    (SYSCON_USBCLKSEL_USBDIV_DIV1 | \
   136	                                    SYSCON_USBCLKSEL_USBSEL_PLL1)
   137	#endif
   138	
   139	/* FLASH Configuration */
   140	
   141	#undef  CONFIG_LPC17_40_FLASH
   142	#define CONFIG_LPC17_40_FLASH         1
   143	
   144	/* Flash access use 6 CPU clocks - Safe for any allowed conditions */
   145	
   146	#define BOARD_FLASHCFG_VALUE       (SYSCON_FLASHCFG_TIM_5 | 0x03a)
   147	
   148	/* Ethernet configuration */
   149	
   150	#define ETH_MCFG_CLKSEL_DIV        ETH_MCFG_CLKSEL_DIV56
   151	
   152	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD
   153	/* SDIO dividers.  Note that slower clocking is required when DMA is disabled
   154	 * in order to avoid RX overrun/TX underrun errors due to delayed responses
   155	 * to service FIFOs in interrupt driven mode.
   156	 * SDCARD_CLOCK=PCLK/(2*(SDCARD_CLKDIV+1))
   157	 */
   158	
   159	#  define SDCARD_CLKDIV_INIT       74   /* 400Khz  */
   160	#  define SDCARD_INIT_CLKDIV       (SDCARD_CLKDIV_INIT)
   161	
   162	#  define SDCARD_NORMAL_CLKDIV     1    /* DMA ON:  SDCARD_CLOCK=15MHz */
   163	#define SDCARD_SLOW_CLKDIV         14   /* DMA OFF: SDCARD_CLOCK=2MHz */
   164	
   165	#  ifdef CONFIG_SDIO_DMA
   166	#    define SDCARD_MMCXFR_CLKDIV   (SDCARD_NORMAL_CLKDIV)
   167	#  else
   168	#    define SDCARD_MMCXFR_CLKDIV   (SDCARD_SLOW_CLKDIV)
   169	#  endif
   170	
   171	#  ifdef CONFIG_SDIO_DMA
   172	#    define SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV    (SDCARD_NORMAL_CLKDIV)
   173	#  else
   174	#    define SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV    (SDCARD_SLOW_CLKDIV)
   175	#  endif
   176	#endif
   177	
   178	/* Set EMC delay values:
   179	 *
   180	 * CMDDLY: Programmable delay value for EMC outputs in command delayed
   181	 *   mode.  The delay amount is roughly CMDDLY * 250 picoseconds.
   182	 * FBCLKDLY: Programmable delay value for the feedback clock that controls
   183	 *   input data sampling.  The delay amount is roughly (FBCLKDLY+1) * 250
   184	 *   picoseconds.
   185	 * CLKOUT0DLY: Programmable delay value for the CLKOUT0 output. This would
   186	 *   typically be used in clock delayed mode.  The delay amount is roughly
   187	 *  (CLKOUT0DLY+1) * 250 picoseconds.
   188	 * CLKOUT1DLY: Programmable delay value for the CLKOUT1 output. This would
   189	 *  typically be used in clock delayed mode.  The delay amount is roughly
   190	 *  (CLKOUT1DLY+1) * 250 picoseconds.
   191	 *
   192	 * Optimal for NOR: {1,1,1,1}
   193	 * Needed for NAND and SDRAM: {17,1,2,1}
   194	 */
   195	
   196	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_EMC
   197	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTNAND) || defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAM)
   198	#  define BOARD_CMDDLY             17
   199	#  define BOARD_FBCLKDLY           17
   200	#  define BOARD_CLKOUT0DLY         1
   201	#  define BOARD_CLKOUT1DLY         1
   202	#else
   203	#  define BOARD_CMDDLY             1
   204	#  define BOARD_FBCLKDLY           1
   205	#  define BOARD_CLKOUT0DLY         1
   206	#  define BOARD_CLKOUT1DLY         1
   207	#endif
   208	#endif
   209	
   210	/* LED definitions **********************************************************/
   211	
   212	/* If CONFIG_ARCH_LEDS is not defined, then the user can control the LEDs in
   213	 * any way.  The following definitions are used to access individual LEDs.
   214	 *
   215	 * LED1 -- Connected to P1[14]
   216	 * LED2 -- Connected to P0[16] (shared with UART1 RXD)
   217	 * LED3 -- Connected to P1[13]
   218	 * LED4 -- Connected to P4[27]
   219	 *
   220	 * These LEDs are connected to ground so a high output value will illuminate
   221	 * them.
   222	 */
   223	
   224	/* LED index values for use with board_userled() */
   225	
   226	#define BOARD_LED1                 0
   227	#define BOARD_LED2                 1
   228	#define BOARD_LED3                 2
   229	#define BOARD_LED4                 3
   230	#define BOARD_NLEDS                4
   231	
   232	/* LED bits for use with board_userled_all() */
   233	
   234	#define BOARD_LED1_BIT             (1 << BOARD_LED1)
   235	#define BOARD_LED2_BIT             (1 << BOARD_LED2)
   236	#define BOARD_LED3_BIT             (1 << BOARD_LED3)
   237	#define BOARD_LED4_BIT             (1 << BOARD_LED4)
   238	
   239	/* If CONFIG_ARCH_LEDs is defined, then NuttX will control the four LEDs
   240	 * on the WaveShare Open1788K.
   241	 * The following definitions describe how NuttX controls the LEDs:
   242	 */
   243	
   244	                               /* LED1 LED2 LED3 LED4                      */
   245	#define LED_STARTED         0  /*  OFF  OFF  OFF  OFF                      */
   246	#define LED_HEAPALLOCATE    1  /*  ON   OFF  OFF  OFF                      */
   247	#define LED_IRQSENABLED     2  /*  OFF   ON  OFF  OFF                      */
   248	#define LED_STACKCREATED    3  /*  ON    ON  OFF  OFF                      */
   249	#define LED_INIRQ           4  /*  LED3 glows, on while in interrupt       */
   250	#define LED_SIGNAL          4  /*  LED3 glows, on while in signal handler  */
   251	#define LED_ASSERTION       4  /*  LED3 glows, on while in assertion       */
   252	#define LED_PANIC           4  /*  LED3 Flashes at 2Hz                     */
   253	#define LED_IDLE            5  /*  LED4 glows: ON while active
   254	                                *              OFF while sleeping
   255	                                */
   256	
   257	/* Button definitions *******************************************************/
   258	
   259	/* The Open1788 supports several buttons.
   260	 * All must be pulled up by the Open1788.
   261	 * When closed, the pins will be pulled to ground.
   262	 * So the buttons will read "1" when open and "0" when closed.
   263	 * All except USER1 are capable of generating interrupts.
   264	 *
   265	 * USER1           -- Connected to P4[26]
   266	 * USER2           -- Connected to P2[22]
   267	 * USER3           -- Connected to P0[10]
   268	 *
   269	 * And a Joystick
   270	 *
   271	 * JOY_A           -- Connected to P2[25]
   272	 * JOY_B           -- Connected to P2[26]
   273	 * JOY_C           -- Connected to P2[23]
   274	 * JOY_D           -- Connected to P2[19]
   275	 * JOY_CTR         -- Connected to P0[14] (shared with SSP1 SSEL)
   276	 *
   277	 * For the interrupting buttons, interrupts are generated on both edges
   278	 * (press and release).
   279	 */
   280	
   281	#define BOARD_BUTTON_USER1         0
   282	#define BOARD_BUTTON_USER2         1
   283	#define BOARD_BUTTON_USER3         2
   284	
   285	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_A           3
   286	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_B           4
   287	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_C           5
   288	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_D           6
   289	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_CTR         7
   290	
   291	#define NUM_BUTTONS                8
   292	
   293	#define BOARD_BUTTON_USER1_BIT     (1 << BOARD_BUTTON_USER1)
   294	#define BOARD_BUTTON_USER2_BIT     (1 << BOARD_BUTTON_USER2)
   295	#define BOARD_BUTTON_USER3_BIT     (1 << BOARD_BUTTON_USER3)
   296	
   297	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_A_BIT       (1 << BOARD_JOYSTICK_A)
   298	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_B_BIT       (1 << BOARD_JOYSTICK_B)
   299	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_C_BIT       (1 << BOARD_JOYSTICK_C)
   300	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_D_BIT       (1 << BOARD_JOYSTICK_D)
   301	#define BOARD_JOYSTICK_CTR_BIT     (1 << BOARD_JOYSTICK_CTR)
   302	
   303	/* Alternate pin selections *************************************************/
   304	
   305	/* UART0:
   306	 *
   307	 * TX    --- Connected to P0[2]
   308	 * RX    --- Connected to P0[3]
   309	 */
   310	
   311	#define GPIO_UART0_TXD             GPIO_UART0_TXD_2
   312	#define GPIO_UART0_RXD             GPIO_UART0_RXD_2
   313	
   314	/* UART1:
   315	 *
   316	 * All pin options are controlled by older bridges on the bottom of the
   317	 * board.
   318	 * There are the default settings on my board as it came out of the box:
   319	 *
   320	 * RTS   --- Connected to P0[22]
   321	 * RI    --- Connected to P0[21]
   322	 * DSR   --- Connected to P0[19]
   323	 * DCD   --- Connected to P0[18]
   324	 * CTS   --- Connected to P0[17]
   325	 * DTR   --- Connected to P0[20]
   326	 * TXD   --- Connected to P0[15]
   327	 * RXD   --- Connected to P0[16] (Shared with LED2)
   328	 */
   329	
   330	#define GPIO_UART1_RTS             GPIO_UART1_RTS_2
   331	#define GPIO_UART1_RI              GPIO_UART1_RI_1
   332	#define GPIO_UART1_DSR             GPIO_UART1_DSR_1
   333	#define GPIO_UART1_DCD             GPIO_UART1_DCD_1
   334	#define GPIO_UART1_CTS             GPIO_UART1_CTS_1
   335	#define GPIO_UART1_DTR             GPIO_UART1_DTR_1
   336	#define GPIO_UART1_TXD             GPIO_UART1_TXD_1
   337	#define GPIO_UART1_RXD             GPIO_UART1_RXD_1
   338	
   339	/* MCI-SDIO:
   340	 *
   341	 * D0    --- Connected to P1[6]
   342	 * D1    --- Connected to P2[11]
   343	 * D2    --- Connected to P2[12]
   344	 * D3    --- Connected to P2[13]
   345	 * CLK   --- Connected to P1[2]
   346	 * CMD   --- Connected to P1[3]
   347	 */
   348	
   349	#define GPIO_SD_DAT0               GPIO_SD_DAT0_2
   350	#define GPIO_SD_DAT1               GPIO_SD_DAT1_1
   351	#define GPIO_SD_DAT2               GPIO_SD_DAT2_1
   352	#define GPIO_SD_DAT3               GPIO_SD_DAT3_1
   353	#define GPIO_SD_CLK                GPIO_SD_CLK_2
   354	#define GPIO_SD_CMD                GPIO_SD_CMD_2
   355	
   356	/* LCD R:
   357	 *
   358	 * VD0   --- Connected to P0[4]
   359	 * VD1   --- Connected to P0[5]
   360	 * VD2   --- Connected to P4[28]
   361	 * VD3   --- Connected to P4[29]
   362	 * VD4   --- Connected to P2[6]
   363	 * VD5   --- Connected to P2[7]
   364	 * VD6   --- Connected to P2[8]
   365	 * VD7   --- Connected to P2[9]
   366	 */
   367	
   368	#define GPIO_LCD_VD0                GPIO_LCD_VD0_1
   369	#define GPIO_LCD_VD1                GPIO_LCD_VD1_1
   370	#define GPIO_LCD_VD2                GPIO_LCD_VD2_2
   371	#define GPIO_LCD_VD3                GPIO_LCD_VD3_2
   372	#define GPIO_LCD_VD4                GPIO_LCD_VD4_1
   373	#define GPIO_LCD_VD5                GPIO_LCD_VD5_1
   374	#define GPIO_LCD_VD6                GPIO_LCD_VD6_2
   375	#define GPIO_LCD_VD7                GPIO_LCD_VD7_2
   376	
   377	/* LCD G:
   378	 *
   379	 * VD8    --- Connected to P0[6]
   380	 * VD9    --- Connected to P0[7]
   381	 * VD10   --- Connected to P1[20]
   382	 * VD11   --- Connected to P1[21]
   383	 * VD12   --- Connected to P1[22]
   384	 * VD13   --- Connected to P1[23]
   385	 * VD14   --- Connected to P1[24]
   386	 * VD15   --- Connected to P1[25]
   387	 */
   388	
   389	#define GPIO_LCD_VD8                GPIO_LCD_VD8_1
   390	#define GPIO_LCD_VD9                GPIO_LCD_VD9_1
   391	#define GPIO_LCD_VD10               GPIO_LCD_VD10_1
   392	#define GPIO_LCD_VD11               GPIO_LCD_VD11_1
   393	#define GPIO_LCD_VD12               GPIO_LCD_VD12_1
   394	#define GPIO_LCD_VD13               GPIO_LCD_VD13_1
   395	#define GPIO_LCD_VD14               GPIO_LCD_VD14_1
   396	#define GPIO_LCD_VD15               GPIO_LCD_VD15_1
   397	
   398	/* LCD B:
   399	 *
   400	 * VD16   --- Connected to P0[8]
   401	 * VD17   --- Connected to P0[9]
   402	 * VD18   --- Connected to P2[12]
   403	 * VD19   --- Connected to P2[13]
   404	 * VD20   --- Connected to P1[26]
   405	 * VD21   --- Connected to P1[27]
   406	 * VD22   --- Connected to P1[28]
   407	 * VD23   --- Connected to P1[29]
   408	 *
   409	 * DCLK   --- Connected to P2[2]
   410	 * LP     --- Connected to P2[5]
   411	 * FP     --- Connected to P2[3]
   412	 * ENAB_M --- Connected to P2[4]
   413	 * PWR    --- Connected to P2[0]
   414	 */
   415	
   416	/* XPT2046 Touchscreen:
   417	 *
   418	 * -------------- -------------------- ------------ -------------------------
   419	 * XTPT2046       Module               Module       Open1788 LED
   420	 *                Signal               Connector    Connector
   421	 * -------------- -------------------- ------------ -------------------------
   422	 * Pin 11 PENIRQ\ PENIRQ (pulled high) PORT3 Pin 1  P2.15 PENIRQ
   423	 * Pin 12 DOUT    MISO                 PORT3 Pin 4  P1.18 MISO1
   424	 *                                                  (Also USB HOST UP LED)
   425	 * Pin 13 BUSY    BUSY (pulled high)   PORT3 Pin 9  P2.14 BUSY
   426	 * Pin 14 DIN     MOSI                 PORT3 Pin 3  P0.13 MOSI1
   427	 *                                                  (Also USB Device up LED
   428	 *                                                  and SD CD pin)
   429	 * Pin 15 CS\     SSEL (pulled high)   PORT3 Pin 6  P1.8  GPIO
   430	 *                                                  (Also RMII_CRS_DV)
   431	 * Pin 16 DCLK    SCK                  PORT3 Pin 5  P1.19 SCK1
   432	 * -------------- -------------------- ------------ -------------------------
   433	 */
   434	
   435	#define GPIO_SSP1_MISO   GPIO_SSP1_MISO_3
   436	#define GPIO_SSP1_MOSI   GPIO_SSP1_MOSI_2
   437	#define GPIO_SSP1_SCK    GPIO_SSP1_SCK_2
   438	#define GPIO_SSP0_MISO   GPIO_SSP0_MISO_1
   439	#define GPIO_SSP0_MOSI   GPIO_SSP0_MOSI_1
   440	#define GPIO_SSP0_SCK    GPIO_SSP0_SCK_1
   441	#define GPIO_I2C0_SCL    GPIO_I2C0_SCL_1
   442	#define GPIO_I2C0_SDA    GPIO_I2C0_SDA_1
   443	
   444	
   445	/* EA LPC1788 UART2, UART3, CAN2 Pin Definitions */
   446	#define GPIO_UART2_TXD   GPIO_UART2_TXD_1
   447	#define GPIO_UART2_RXD   GPIO_UART2_RXD_1
   448	#define GPIO_UART3_TXD   GPIO_UART3_TXD_3
   449	#define GPIO_UART3_RXD   GPIO_UART3_RXD_3
   450	#define GPIO_CAN2_RD     GPIO_CAN2_RD_1
   451	#define GPIO_CAN2_TD     GPIO_CAN2_TD_1
   452	
   453	
   454	/* EA LPC1788 LAN8720 PHY Management Pins */
   455	#define GPIO_ENET_MDC    GPIO_ENET_MDC_1
   456	#define GPIO_ENET_MDIO   GPIO_ENET_MDIO_1
   457	
   458	#endif /* __BOARDS_ARM_LPC17XX_40XX_OPEN1788_INCLUDE_BOARD_H */

    23	#ifndef __BOARDS_ARM_LPC17XX_40XX_OPEN1788_SRC_OPEN1788_H
    24	#define __BOARDS_ARM_LPC17XX_40XX_OPEN1788_SRC_OPEN1788_H
    25	
    26	/****************************************************************************
    27	 * Included Files
    28	 ****************************************************************************/
    29	
    30	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    31	#include <nuttx/compiler.h>
    32	
    33	/****************************************************************************
    34	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    35	 ****************************************************************************/
    36	
    37	/* Open1788 GPIO Pin Definitions ********************************************/
    38	
    39	/* GPIO P2[21] connects to the Ready/Busy pin of the NAND part.
    40	 * We need to reconfigure this pin as normal GPIO input if NAND is used.
    41	 */
    42	
    43	#define GPIO_NAND_RB     (GPIO_INPUT | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN21)
    44	
    45	/* If CONFIG_ARCH_LEDS is not defined, then the user can control the LEDs
    46	 * in any way.
    47	 * The following definitions are used to access individual LEDs.
    48	 *
    49	 * LED1 -- Connected to P1[14]
    50	 * LED2 -- Connected to P0[16]
    51	 * LED3 -- Connected to P1[13]
    52	 * LED4 -- Connected to P4[27]
    53	 *
    54	 * These LEDs are connected to ground so a high output value will illuminate
    55	 * them.
    56	 */
    57	
    58	#define GPIO_LED1        (GPIO_OUTPUT | GPIO_VALUE_ONE | GPIO_PORT1 | GPIO_PIN14)
    59	#define GPIO_LED2        (GPIO_OUTPUT | GPIO_VALUE_ONE | GPIO_PORT0 | GPIO_PIN16)
    60	#define GPIO_LED3        (GPIO_OUTPUT | GPIO_VALUE_ONE | GPIO_PORT1 | GPIO_PIN13)
    61	#define GPIO_LED4        (GPIO_OUTPUT | GPIO_VALUE_ONE | GPIO_PORT4 | GPIO_PIN27)
    62	
    63	/* Button definitions *******************************************************/
    64	
    65	/* The Open1788 supports several buttons.
    66	 * All must be pulled up by the Open1788.
    67	 * When closed, the pins will be pulled to ground.
    68	 * So the buttons will read "1" when open and "0" when closed.
    69	 * All except USER1 are capable of generating interrupts.
    70	 *
    71	 * USER1           -- Connected to P4[26]
    72	 * USER2           -- Connected to P2[22]
    73	 * USER3           -- Connected to P0[10]
    74	 *
    75	 * And a Joystick
    76	 *
    77	 * JOY_A           -- Connected to P2[25]
    78	 * JOY_B           -- Connected to P2[26]
    79	 * JOY_C           -- Connected to P2[23]
    80	 * JOY_D           -- Connected to P2[19]
    81	 * JOY_CTR         -- Connected to P0[14] (shared with SSP1 SSEL)
    82	 *
    83	 * For the interrupting buttons, interrupts are generated on both edges
    84	 * (press and release).
    85	 */
    86	
    87	#define GPIO_USER1       (GPIO_INPUT   | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT4 | GPIO_PIN26)
    88	#define GPIO_USER2       (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN22)
    89	#define GPIO_USER3       (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT0 | GPIO_PIN10)
    90	
    91	#define GPIO_JOY_A       (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN25)
    92	#define GPIO_JOY_B       (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN26)
    93	#define GPIO_JOY_C       (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN23)
    94	#define GPIO_JOY_D       (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN19)
    95	#define GPIO_JOY_CTR     (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT0 | GPIO_PIN14)
    96	
    97	/* IRQ numbers for the buttons that do support interrupts */
    98	
    99	#define GPIO_USER2_IRQ   LPC17_40_IRQ_P2p22
   100	#define GPIO_USER3_IRQ   LPC17_40_IRQ_P0p10
   101	#define GPIO_JOY_A_IRQ   LPC17_40_IRQ_P2p25
   102	#define GPIO_JOY_B_IRQ   LPC17_40_IRQ_P2p26
   103	#define GPIO_JOY_C_IRQ   LPC17_40_IRQ_P2p23
   104	#define GPIO_JOY_D_IRQ   LPC17_40_IRQ_P2p19
   105	#define GPIO_JOY_CTR_IRQ LPC17_40_IRQ_P0p14
   106	
   107	/* SD Card ******************************************************************/
   108	
   109	/* The SD card detect (CD) signal is on P0[13].  This signal is shared.
   110	 * It is also used for MOSI1 and USB_UP_LED.  The CD pin may be disconnected.
   111	 *  There is a jumper on board that enables the CD pin.
   112	 *
   113	 * The CD pin is interrupting:
   114	 */
   115	
   116	#define GPIO_SD_CD       (GPIO_INTBOTH | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT0 | GPIO_PIN13)
   117	
   118	/* LCD **********************************************************************/
   119	
   120	/* Backlight enable, P2[1].  Initial state is OFF (zero) */
   121	
   122	#define GPIO_LCD_BL      0
   123	
   124	/* XPT2046 Touchscreen ******************************************************/
   125	
   126	/* -------------- -------------------- ------------ -------------------------
   127	 * XTPT2046       Module               Module       Open1788 LED
   128	 *                Signal               Connector    Connector
   129	 * -------------- -------------------- ------------ -------------------------
   130	 * Pin 11 PENIRQ\ PENIRQ (pulled high) PORT3 Pin 1  P2.15 PENIRQ
   131	 * Pin 12 DOUT    MISO                 PORT3 Pin 4  P1.18 MISO1
   132	 *                                                  (Also USB HOST UP LED)
   133	 * Pin 13 BUSY    BUSY (pulled high)   PORT3 Pin 9  P2.14 BUSY
   134	 * Pin 14 DIN     MOSI                 PORT3 Pin 3  P0.13 MOSI1
   135	 *                                                  (Also USB Device up LED
   136	 *                                                  and SD CD pin)
   137	 * Pin 15 CS\     SSEL (pulled high)   PORT3 Pin 6  P1.8  GPIO
   138	 *                                                  (Also RMII_CRS_DV)
   139	 * Pin 16 DCLK    SCK                  PORT3 Pin 5  P1.19 SCK1
   140	 * -------------- -------------------- ------------ -------------------------
   141	 *
   142	 * Pins should not need to be configured as pull-ups because, according to
   143	 * the LCD schematic, the are pulled-up on board the LCD module.
   144	 */
   145	
   146	#define GPIO_TC_PENIRQ   (GPIO_INTFE | GPIO_FLOAT | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN15)
   147	#define GPIO_TC_BUSY     (GPIO_INPUT | GPIO_FLOAT | GPIO_PORT2 | GPIO_PIN14)
   148	#define GPIO_TC_CS       (GPIO_OUTPUT | GPIO_VALUE_ONE | GPIO_PORT0 | GPIO_PIN20)
   149	
   150	#define LPC17_40_IRQ_PENIRQ LPC17_40_IRQ_P2p15
   151	
   152	/****************************************************************************
   153	 * Public Data
   154	 ****************************************************************************/
   155

    41	/****************************************************************************
    42	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    43	 ****************************************************************************/
    44	
    45	/* If CONFIG_ARCH_LEDS is not defined, then the user can control the LEDs
    46	 * in any way.
    47	 * The following definitions are used to access individual LEDs.
    48	 *
    49	 * LED1 -- Connected to P1[14]
    50	 * LED2 -- Connected to P0[16]
    51	 * LED3 -- Connected to P1[13]
    52	 * LED4 -- Connected to P4[27]
    53	 *
    54	 * These LEDs are connected to ground so a high output value will illuminate
    55	 * them.
    56	 *
    57	 * If CONFIG_ARCH_LEDs is defined, then NuttX will control the four LEDs
    58	 * on the WaveShare Open1788K.  The following definitions describe how NuttX
    59	 * controls the LEDs:
    60	 *
    61	 *                                 LED1 LED2 LED3 LED4
    62	 *   LED_STARTED                0  OFF  OFF  OFF  OFF
    63	 *   LED_HEAPALLOCATE           1  ON   OFF  OFF  OFF
    64	 *   LED_IRQSENABLED            2  OFF   ON  OFF  OFF
    65	 *   LED_STACKCREATED           3  ON    ON  OFF  OFF
    66	 *   LED_INIRQ                  4  LED3 glows, on while in interrupt
    67	 *   LED_SIGNAL                 4  LED3 glows, on while in signal handler
    68	 *   LED_ASSERTION              4  LED3 glows, on while in assertion
    69	 *   LED_PANIC                  4  LED3 Flashes at 2Hz
    70	 *   LED_IDLE                   5  LED glows, ON while sleeping
    71	 */
    72	
    73	/* The following definitions map the encoded LED setting to GPIO settings */
    74	
    75	#define OPEN1788_LED1     (1 << 0)
    76	#define OPEN1788_LED2     (1 << 1)
    77	#define OPEN1788_LED3     (1 << 2)
    78	#define OPEN1788_LED4     (1 << 3)
    79	
    80	#define ON_SETBITS_SHIFT  (0)
    81	#define ON_CLRBITS_SHIFT  (4)
    82	#define OFF_SETBITS_SHIFT (8)
    83	#define OFF_CLRBITS_SHIFT (12)
    84	
    85	#define ON_BITS(v)        ((v) & 0xff)
    86	#define OFF_BITS(v)       (((v) >> 8) & 0x0ff)
    87	#define SETBITS(b)        ((b) & 0x0f)
    88	#define CLRBITS(b)        (((b) >> 4) & 0x0f)
    89	
    90	#define ON_SETBITS(v)     (SETBITS(ON_BITS(v))
    91	#define ON_CLRBITS(v)     (CLRBITS(ON_BITS(v))
    92	#define OFF_SETBITS(v)    (SETBITS(OFF_BITS(v))
    93	#define OFF_CLRBITS(v)    (CLRBITS(OFF_BITS(v))
    94	
    95	#define LED_STARTED_ON_SETBITS       ((0) << ON_SETBITS_SHIFT)
    96	#define LED_STARTED_ON_CLRBITS       ((OPEN1788_LED1|OPEN1788_LED2|OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << ON_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
    97	#define LED_STARTED_OFF_SETBITS      (0 << OFF_SETBITS_SHIFT)
    98	#define LED_STARTED_OFF_CLRBITS      ((OPEN1788_LED1|OPEN1788_LED2|OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << OFF_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
    99	
   100	#define LED_HEAPALLOCATE_ON_SETBITS  ((OPEN1788_LED1) << ON_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   101	#define LED_HEAPALLOCATE_ON_CLRBITS  ((OPEN1788_LED2|OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << ON_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   102	#define LED_HEAPALLOCATE_OFF_SETBITS (0 << OFF_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   103	#define LED_HEAPALLOCATE_OFF_CLRBITS ((OPEN1788_LED1|OPEN1788_LED2|OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << OFF_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   104	
   105	#define LED_IRQSENABLED_ON_SETBITS   ((OPEN1788_LED2) << ON_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   106	#define LED_IRQSENABLED_ON_CLRBITS   ((OPEN1788_LED1|OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << ON_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   107	#define LED_IRQSENABLED_OFF_SETBITS  ((OPEN1788_LED1) << OFF_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   108	#define LED_IRQSENABLED_OFF_CLRBITS  ((OPEN1788_LED2|OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << OFF_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   109	
   110	#define LED_STACKCREATED_ON_SETBITS  ((OPEN1788_LED1|OPEN1788_LED2) << ON_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   111	#define LED_STACKCREATED_ON_CLRBITS  ((OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << ON_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   112	#define LED_STACKCREATED_OFF_SETBITS ((OPEN1788_LED2) << OFF_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   113	#define LED_STACKCREATED_OFF_CLRBITS ((OPEN1788_LED1|OPEN1788_LED3|OPEN1788_LED4) << OFF_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   114	
   115	#define LED_EVENT_ON_SETBITS         ((OPEN1788_LED3) << ON_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   116	#define LED_EVENT_ON_CLRBITS         ((0) << ON_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   117	#define LED_EVENT_OFF_SETBITS        ((0) << OFF_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   118	#define LED_EVENT_OFF_CLRBITS        ((OPEN1788_LED3) << OFF_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   119	
   120	#define LED_IDLE_ON_SETBITS          ((0) << ON_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   121	#define LED_IDLE_ON_CLRBITS          ((OPEN1788_LED4) << ON_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   122	#define LED_IDLE_OFF_SETBITS         ((OPEN1788_LED4) << OFF_SETBITS_SHIFT)
   123	#define LED_IDLE_OFF_CLRBITS         ((0) << OFF_CLRBITS_SHIFT)
   124	
   125	/* Dump GPIO registers */
   126	
   127	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_LEDS_INFO
   128	#  define led_dumpgpio(m) lpc17_40_dumpgpio(???, m)
   129	#else
   130	#  define led_dumpgpio(m)
   131	#endif
   132	
   133	/****************************************************************************
   134	 * Private Data
   135	 ****************************************************************************/
   136	
   137	/****************************************************************************
   138	 * Private Data
   139	 ****************************************************************************/
   140	
   141	static const uint16_t g_ledbits[8] =
   142	{
   143	  (LED_STARTED_ON_SETBITS       | LED_STARTED_ON_CLRBITS |
   144	  LED_STARTED_OFF_SETBITS      | LED_STARTED_OFF_CLRBITS),
   145	
   146	  (LED_HEAPALLOCATE_ON_SETBITS  | LED_HEAPALLOCATE_ON_CLRBITS |
   147	  LED_HEAPALLOCATE_OFF_SETBITS | LED_HEAPALLOCATE_OFF_CLRBITS),
   148	
   149	  (LED_IRQSENABLED_ON_SETBITS   | LED_IRQSENABLED_ON_CLRBITS |
   150	  LED_IRQSENABLED_OFF_SETBITS  | LED_IRQSENABLED_OFF_CLRBITS),
   151	
   152	  (LED_STACKCREATED_ON_SETBITS  | LED_STACKCREATED_ON_CLRBITS |
   153	  LED_STACKCREATED_OFF_SETBITS | LED_STACKCREATED_OFF_CLRBITS),
   154	
   155	  (LED_EVENT_ON_SETBITS         | LED_EVENT_ON_CLRBITS |
   156	  LED_EVENT_OFF_SETBITS        | LED_EVENT_OFF_CLRBITS),
   157	
   158	  (LED_IDLE_ON_SETBITS          | LED_IDLE_ON_CLRBITS |
   159	  LED_IDLE_OFF_SETBITS         | LED_IDLE_OFF_CLRBITS)
   160	};
   161	
   162	/****************************************************************************
   163	 * Private Functions
   164	 ****************************************************************************/
   165	
   166	static inline void led_clrbits(unsigned int clrbits)
   167	{
   168	  if ((clrbits & OPEN1788_LED1) != 0)
   169	    {
   170	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED1, false);
   171	    }
   172	
   173	  if ((clrbits & OPEN1788_LED2) != 0)
   174	    {
   175	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED2, false);
   176	    }
   177	
   178	  if ((clrbits & OPEN1788_LED3) != 0)
   179	    {
   180	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED3, false);
   181	    }
   182	
   183	  if ((clrbits & OPEN1788_LED4) != 0)
   184	    {
   185	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED4, false);
   186	    }
   187	}
   188	
   189	static inline void led_setbits(unsigned int setbits)
   190	{
   191	  if ((setbits & OPEN1788_LED1) != 0)
   192	    {
   193	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED1, true);
   194	    }
   195	
   196	  if ((setbits & OPEN1788_LED2) != 0)
   197	    {
   198	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED2, true);
   199	    }
   200	
   201	  if ((setbits & OPEN1788_LED3) != 0)
   202	    {
   203	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED3, true);
   204	    }
   205	
   206	  if ((setbits & OPEN1788_LED4) != 0)
   207	    {
   208	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_LED4, true);
   209	    }
   210	}
   211	
   212	static void led_setonoff(unsigned int bits)
   213	{
   214	  led_clrbits(CLRBITS(bits));
   215	  led_setbits(SETBITS(bits));
   216	}
   217	
   218	/****************************************************************************
   219	 * Public Functions
   220	 ****************************************************************************/
   221	
   222	/****************************************************************************
   223	 * Name: board_autoled_initialize
   224	 ****************************************************************************/
   225	
   226	void board_autoled_initialize(void)
   227	{
   228	  /* Configure LED1-4 GPIOs for output */
   229	
   230	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LED1);
   231	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LED2);
   232	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LED3);
   233	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LED4);
   234	}
   235	
   236	/****************************************************************************
   237	 * Name: board_autoled_on
   238	 ****************************************************************************/
   239	
   240	void board_autoled_on(int led)
   241	{
   242	  led_setonoff(ON_BITS(g_ledbits[led]));
   243	}
   244	
   245	/****************************************************************************
   246	 * Name: board_autoled_off
   247	 ****************************************************************************/
   248	
   249	void board_autoled_off(int led)
   250	{
   251	  led_setonoff(OFF_BITS(g_ledbits[led]));
   252	}

    23	/****************************************************************************
    24	 * Included Files
    25	 ****************************************************************************/
    26	
    27	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    28	
    29	#include <nuttx/debug.h>
    30	
    31	#include <nuttx/board.h>
    32	#include <arch/board/board.h>
    33	
    34	#include "arm_internal.h"
    35	#include "lpc17_40_emc.h"
    36	
    37	#include "open1788.h"
    38	
    39	/****************************************************************************
    40	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    41	 ****************************************************************************/
    42	
    43	/****************************************************************************
    44	 * Private Functions
    45	 ****************************************************************************/
    46	
    47	/****************************************************************************
    48	 * Public Functions
    49	 ****************************************************************************/
    50	
    51	/****************************************************************************
    52	 * Name: lpc17_40_boardinitialize
    53	 *
    54	 * Description:
    55	 *   All LPC17xx/LPC40xx architectures must provide the following entry
    56	 *   point.
    57	 *   This entry point is called early in the initialization -- after all
    58	 *   memory has been configured and mapped but before any devices have been
    59	 *   initialized.
    60	 *
    61	 ****************************************************************************/
    62	
    63	void lpc17_40_boardinitialize(void)
    64	{
    65	  /* Initialize the EMC, SDRAM, NOR FLASH, and NAND FLASH */
    66	
    67	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_EMC
    68	  lpc17_40_emcinitialize();
    69	
    70	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAM
    71	  open1788_sdram_initialize();
    72	#endif
    73	
    74	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTNOR
    75	  open1788_nor_initialize();
    76	#endif
    77	
    78	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTNAND
    79	  open1788_nand_initialize();
    80	#endif
    81	#endif
    82	
    83	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP0) || defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP1) || \
    84	    defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP2)
    85	  /* Configure SSP chip selects if 1) at least one SSP is enabled, and 2)
    86	   * the weak function open1788_sspdev_initialize() has been brought into
    87	   * the link.
    88	   */
    89	
    90	  if (open1788_sspdev_initialize)
    91	    {
    92	      open1788_sspdev_initialize();
    93	    }
    94	#endif
    95	
    96	#ifdef CONFIG_ARCH_LEDS
    97	  /* Configure on-board LEDs if LED support has been selected. */
    98	
    99	  board_autoled_initialize();
   100	#endif
   101	
   102	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD
   103	  /* Configure the LCD GPIOs if LCD support has been selected. */
   104	
   105	  open1788_lcd_initialize();
   106	#endif
   107	}
   108	
   109	/****************************************************************************
   110	 * Name: board_late_initialize
   111	 *
   112	 * Description:
   113	 *   If CONFIG_BOARD_LATE_INITIALIZE is selected, then an additional
   114	 *   initialization call will be performed in the boot-up sequence to a
   115	 *   function called board_late_initialize(). board_late_initialize() will be
   116	 *   called immediately after up_initialize() is called and just before the
   117	 *   initial application is started.  This additional initialization phase
   118	 *   may be used, for example, to initialize board-specific device drivers.
   119	 *
   120	 ****************************************************************************/
   121	
   122	#ifdef CONFIG_BOARD_LATE_INITIALIZE
   123	void board_late_initialize(void)
   124	{
   125	  /* Perform board-specific initialization */
   126	
   127	  open1788_bringup();
   128	}
   129	#endif

    10	 * "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance with the
    11	 * License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
    12	 *

    16	 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT

    18	 * License for the specific language governing permissions and limitations
    19	 * under the License.

    49	/****************************************************************************
    50	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    51	 ****************************************************************************/
    52	
    53	/* Configuration ************************************************************/
    54	
    55	#define NSH_HAVE_MMCSD      1
    56	#define NSH_HAVE_USBHOST    1
    57	#define NSH_HAVE_USBHDEV    1
    58	
    59	#undef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CD
    60	#undef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CDINT
    61	
    62	/* MMC/SD support */
    63	
    64	#if !defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD) || !defined(CONFIG_MMCSD) && !defined(CONFIG_MMCD_SDIO)
    65	#  undef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD
    66	#endif
    67	
    68	/* Can't support MMC/SD features if mountpoints are disabled */
    69	
    70	#if defined(CONFIG_DISABLE_MOUNTPOINT)
    71	#  undef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD
    72	#endif
    73	
    74	/* MMC/SD support requires that an SPI support is enabled and an SPI port
    75	 * is selected
    76	 */
    77	
    78	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD
    79	#  if !defined(CONFIG_NSH_MMCSDSLOTNO)
    80	#     warning "Assuming slot MMC/SD slot 0"
    81	#     define CONFIG_NSH_MMCSDSLOTNO 0
    82	#  endif
    83	#endif
    84	
    85	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD
    86	#  if !defined(CONFIG_NSH_MMCSDMINOR)
    87	#     warning "Assuming /dev/mmcsd0"
    88	#     define CONFIG_NSH_MMCSDMINOR 0
    89	#  endif
    90	#endif
    91	
    92	/* The SD card detect (CD) signal is on P0[13]. This signal is shared.
    93	 * It is also used for MOSI1 and USB_UP_LED.
    94	 * The CD pin may be disconnected.
    95	 * There is a jumper on board that enables the CD pin.
    96	 */
    97	
    98	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD
    99	#  ifdef CONFIG_MMCSD_HAVE_CARDDETECT
   100	#    define NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CD 1
   101	#    ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ
   102	#      define NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CDINT 1
   103	#    endif
   104	#  endif
   105	#endif
   106	
   107	#if defined(NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CD) && \
   108	    (defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP1) || defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_USBDEV))
   109	#  warning "Use of SD Card Detect pin conflicts with SSP1 and/or USB device"
   110	#endif
   111	
   112	/* USB Host */
   113	
   114	#ifndef CONFIG_USBHOST
   115	#  undef NSH_HAVE_USBHOST
   116	#endif
   117	
   118	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_USBHOST
   119	#  undef NSH_HAVE_USBHOST
   120	#endif
   121	
   122	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_USBHOST
   123	#  ifndef CONFIG_USBHOST_DEFPRIO
   124	#    define CONFIG_USBHOST_DEFPRIO 50
   125	#  endif
   126	#  ifndef CONFIG_USBHOST_STACKSIZE
   127	#    ifdef CONFIG_USBHOST_HUB
   128	#      define CONFIG_USBHOST_STACKSIZE 1536
   129	#    else
   130	#      define CONFIG_USBHOST_STACKSIZE 1024
   131	#    endif
   132	#  endif
   133	#endif
   134	
   135	/* USB Device */
   136	
   137	#ifndef CONFIG_USBDEV
   138	#  undef NSH_HAVE_USBDEV
   139	#endif
   140	
   141	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_USBDEV
   142	#  undef NSH_HAVE_USBDEV
   143	#endif
   144	
   145	/****************************************************************************
   146	 * Private Data
   147	 ****************************************************************************/
   148	
   149	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_USBHOST
   150	static struct usbhost_connection_s *g_usbconn;
   151	#endif
   152	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD
   153	static struct sdio_dev_s *g_sdiodev;
   154	#endif
   155	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CD
   156	static bool g_sd_inserted;
   157	#endif
   158	
   159	/****************************************************************************
   160	 * Private Functions
   161	 ****************************************************************************/
   162	
   163	/****************************************************************************
   164	 * Name: nsh_waiter
   165	 *
   166	 * Description:
   167	 *   Wait for USB devices to be connected.
   168	 *
   169	 ****************************************************************************/
   170	
   171	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_USBHOST
   172	static int nsh_waiter(int argc, char *argv[])
   173	{
   174	  struct usbhost_hubport_s *hport;
   175	
   176	  syslog(LOG_INFO, "nsh_waiter: Running\n");
   177	  for (; ; )
   178	    {
   179	      /* Wait for the device to change state */
   180	
   181	      DEBUGVERIFY(CONN_WAIT(g_usbconn, &hport));
   182	      syslog(LOG_INFO, "nsh_waiter: %s\n",
   183	             hport->connected ? "connected" : "disconnected");
   184	
   185	      /* Did we just become connected? */
   186	
   187	      if (hport->connected)
   188	        {
   189	          /* Yes.. enumerate the newly connected device */
   190	
   191	          CONN_ENUMERATE(g_usbconn, hport);
   192	        }
   193	    }
   194	
   195	  /* Keep the compiler from complaining */
   196	
   197	  return 0;
   198	}
   199	#endif
   200	
   201	/****************************************************************************
   202	 * Name: nsh_cdinterrupt
   203	 *
   204	 * Description:
   205	 *   Card detect interrupt handler.
   206	 *
   207	 ****************************************************************************/
   208	
   209	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CDINT
   210	static int nsh_cdinterrupt(int irq, void *context, void *arg)
   211	{
   212	  bool present;
   213	
   214	  present = !lpc17_40_gpioread(GPIO_SD_CD);
   215	  if (present != g_sd_inserted)
   216	    {
   217	      sdio_mediachange(g_sdiodev, present);
   218	      g_sd_inserted = present;
   219	    }
   220	
   221	  return OK;
   222	}
   223	#endif
   224	
   225	/****************************************************************************
   226	 * Name: nsh_sdinitialize
   227	 *
   228	 * Description:
   229	 *   Initialize SPI-based microSD.
   230	 *
   231	 ****************************************************************************/
   232	
   233	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD
   234	static int nsh_sdinitialize(void)
   235	{
   236	  int ret;
   237	
   238	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CD
   239	  /* Configure the SD card detect GPIO */
   240	
   241	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_SD_CD);
   242	
   243	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CDINT
   244	  /* Attach an interrupt handler to get notifications when a card is
   245	   * inserted or deleted.
   246	   */
   247	
   248	  irq_attach(LPC17_40_IRQ_P0p13, nsh_cdinterrupt, NULL);
   249	  up_enable_irq(LPC17_40_IRQ_P0p13);
   250	
   251	#endif
   252	#endif
   253	
   254	  /* First, get an instance of the SDIO interface */
   255	
   256	  g_sdiodev = sdio_initialize(CONFIG_NSH_MMCSDSLOTNO);
   257	  if (!g_sdiodev)
   258	    {
   259	      syslog(LOG_ERR, "ERROR: Failed to initialize SDIO slot %d\n",
   260	             CONFIG_NSH_MMCSDSLOTNO);
   261	      return -ENODEV;
   262	    }
   263	
   264	  /* Now bind the SDIO interface to the MMC/SD driver */
   265	
   266	  ret = mmcsd_slotinitialize(CONFIG_NSH_MMCSDMINOR, g_sdiodev);
   267	  if (ret != OK)
   268	    {
   269	      syslog(LOG_ERR,
   270	             "ERROR: Failed to bind SDIO to the MMC/SD driver: %d\n",
   271	             ret);
   272	      return ret;
   273	    }
   274	
   275	  /* Check if there is a card in the slot and inform the SDCARD driver.  If
   276	   * we do not support the card  detect, then let's assume that there is
   277	   * one.
   278	   */
   279	
   280	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_MMCSD_CD
   281	  g_sd_inserted = !lpc17_40_gpioread(GPIO_SD_CD);
   282	  sdio_mediachange(g_sdiodev, g_sd_inserted);
   283	#else
   284	  sdio_mediachange(g_sdiodev, true);
   285	#endif
   286	  return OK;
   287	}
   288	#else
   289	#  define nsh_sdinitialize() (OK)
   290	#endif
   291	
   292	/****************************************************************************
   293	 * Name: nsh_usbhostinitialize
   294	 *
   295	 * Description:
   296	 *   Initialize SPI-based microSD.
   297	 *
   298	 ****************************************************************************/
   299	
   300	#ifdef NSH_HAVE_USBHOST
   301	static int nsh_usbhostinitialize(void)
   302	{
   303	  int ret;
   304	
   305	  /* First, register all of the class drivers needed to support the drivers
   306	   * that we care about:
   307	   */
   308	
   309	  syslog(LOG_INFO, "Register class drivers\n");
   310	
   311	#ifdef CONFIG_USBHOST_MSC
   312	  /* Register the USB host Mass Storage Class */
   313	
   314	  ret = usbhost_msc_initialize();
   315	  if (ret != OK)
   316	    {
   317	      syslog(LOG_ERR,
   318	             "ERROR: Failed to register the mass storage class: %d\n", ret);
   319	    }
   320	#endif
   321	
   322	#ifdef CONFIG_USBHOST_CDCACM
   323	  /* Register the CDC/ACM serial class */
   324	
   325	  ret = usbhost_cdcacm_initialize();
   326	  if (ret != OK)
   327	    {
   328	      syslog(LOG_ERR,
   329	             "ERROR: Failed to register the CDC/ACM serial class: %d\n",
   330	             ret);
   331	    }
   332	#endif
   333	
   334	  /* Then get an instance of the USB host interface */
   335	
   336	  syslog(LOG_INFO, "Initialize USB host\n");
   337	  g_usbconn = lpc17_40_usbhost_initialize(0);
   338	  if (g_usbconn)
   339	    {
   340	      /* Start a thread to handle device connection. */
   341	
   342	      syslog(LOG_INFO, "Start nsh_waiter\n");
   343	
   344	      ret = kthread_create("usbhost", CONFIG_USBHOST_DEFPRIO,
   345	                           CONFIG_USBHOST_STACKSIZE,
   346	                           nsh_waiter, NULL);
   347	      return ret < 0 ? -ENOEXEC : OK;
   348	    }
   349	
   350	  return -ENODEV;
   351	}
   352	#else
   353	#  define nsh_usbhostinitialize() (OK)
   354	#endif
   355	
   356	/****************************************************************************
   357	 * Public Functions
   358	 ****************************************************************************/
   359	
   360	/****************************************************************************
   361	 * Name: open1788_bringup
   362	 *
   363	 * Description:
   364	 *   Perform architecture-specific initialization
   365	 *
   366	 *   CONFIG_BOARD_LATE_INITIALIZE=y :
   367	 *     Called from board_late_initialize().
   368	 *
   369	 ****************************************************************************/
   370	
   371	int open1788_bringup(void)
   372	{
   373	  int ret;
   374	
   375	#ifdef CONFIG_FS_PROCFS
   376	  /* Mount the procfs file system at the default location, /proc */
   377	
   378	  ret = nx_mount(NULL, "/proc", "procfs", 0, NULL);
   379	  if (ret < 0)
   380	    {
   381	      syslog(LOG_ERR, "ERROR: Failed to mount procfs: %d\n", ret);
   382	    }
   383	#endif
   384	
   385	  /* Initialize SPI-based microSD */
   386	
   387	  ret = nsh_sdinitialize();
   388	  if (ret == OK)
   389	    {
   390	      /* Initialize USB host */
   391	
   392	      ret = nsh_usbhostinitialize();
   393	    }
   394	
   395	#ifdef CONFIG_VIDEO_FB
   396	  /* Initialize and register the framebuffer driver */
   397	
   398	  ret = fb_register(0, 0);
   399	  if (ret < 0)
   400	    {
   401	      syslog(LOG_ERR, "ERROR: fb_register() failed: %d\n", ret);
   402	    }
   403	
   404	  extern void ea_lcd_backlight_init(void);
   405	  ea_lcd_backlight_init();
   406	#endif
   407	
   408	#ifdef CONFIG_INPUT_ADS7843E
   409	  /* Initialize the touchscreen */
   410	
   411	  ret = open1788_tsc_setup(0);
   412	  if (ret < 0)
   413	    {
   414	      syslog(LOG_ERR, "ERROR: open1788_tsc_setup failed: %d\n", ret);
   415	    }
   416	#endif
   417	
   418	#ifdef CONFIG_OPEN1788_DJOYSTICK
   419	  /* Initialize and register the joystick driver */
   420	
   421	  ret = lpc17_40_djoy_initialization();
   422	  if (ret != OK)
   423	    {
   424	      syslog(LOG_ERR,
   425	             "ERROR: Failed to register the joystick driver: %d\n", ret);
   426	      return ret;
   427	    }
   428	#endif
   429	
   430	  return ret;
   431	}

     2	 * boards/arm/lpc17xx_40xx/open1788/src/lpc17_40_buttons.c

     7	 * contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file distributed with
     8	 * this work for additional information regarding copyright ownership.  The
     9	 * ASF licenses this file to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
    10	 * "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance with the
    11	 * License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
    12	 *
    13	 *   http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
    14	 *
    15	 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
    16	 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT
    17	 * WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.  See the
    18	 * License for the specific language governing permissions and limitations
    19	 * under the License.
    20	 *
    21	 ****************************************************************************/
    22	
    23	/****************************************************************************
    24	 * Included Files
    25	 ****************************************************************************/
    26	
    27	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    28	
    29	#include <stdint.h>
    30	#include <stdbool.h>
    31	#include <errno.h>
    32	
    33	#include <nuttx/arch.h>
    34	#include <nuttx/board.h>
    35	#include <nuttx/irq.h>
    36	
    37	#include <arch/board/board.h>
    38	
    39	#include "lpc17_40_gpio.h"
    40	#include "open1788.h"
    41	
    42	#ifdef CONFIG_ARCH_BUTTONS
    43	
    44	/****************************************************************************
    45	 * Private Data
    46	 ****************************************************************************/
    47	
    48	/* The Open1788 supports several buttons.  All will read "1" when open and
    49	 * "0" when closed
    50	 *
    51	 * USER1           -- Connected to P4[26]
    52	 * USER2           -- Connected to P2[22]
    53	 * USER3           -- Connected to P0[10]
    54	 *
    55	 * And a Joystick
    56	 *
    57	 * JOY_A           -- Connected to P2[25]
    58	 * JOY_B           -- Connected to P2[26]
    59	 * JOY_C           -- Connected to P2[23]
    60	 * JOY_D           -- Connected to P2[19]
    61	 * JOY_CTR         -- Connected to P0[14]
    62	 *
    63	 * The switches are all connected to ground and should be pulled up and
    64	 * sensed with a value of '0' when closed.
    65	 */
    66	
    67	/* Pin configuration for each Open1788 button.  This array is indexed by
    68	 * the BUTTON_* and JOYSTICK_* definitions in board.h
    69	 */
    70	
    71	static const lpc17_40_pinset_t g_buttoncfg[NUM_BUTTONS] =
    72	{
    73	  GPIO_USER1, GPIO_USER2, GPIO_USER3, GPIO_JOY_A,
    74	  GPIO_JOY_B, GPIO_JOY_C, GPIO_JOY_D, GPIO_JOY_CTR
    75	};
    76	
    77	#if defined(CONFIG_ARCH_IRQBUTTONS) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ)
    78	/* This array provides the mapping from button ID numbers to button IRQ
    79	 * numbers.
    80	 */
    81	
    82	static const uint8_t g_buttonirq[NUM_BUTTONS] =
    83	{
    84	  0,              GPIO_USER2_IRQ, GPIO_USER3_IRQ, GPIO_JOY_A_IRQ,
    85	  GPIO_JOY_B_IRQ, GPIO_JOY_C_IRQ, GPIO_JOY_D_IRQ, GPIO_JOY_CTR_IRQ
    86	};
    87	#endif
    88	
    89	/****************************************************************************
    90	 * Public Functions
    91	 ****************************************************************************/
    92	
    93	/****************************************************************************
    94	 * Name: board_button_initialize
    95	 *
    96	 * Description:
    97	 *   board_button_initialize() must be called to initialize button resources.
    98	 *   After that, board_buttons() may be called to collect the current state
    99	 *   of all buttons or board_button_irq() may be called to register button
   100	 *   interrupt handlers.
   101	 *
   102	 ****************************************************************************/
   103	
   104	uint32_t board_button_initialize(void)
   105	{
   106	  int i;
   107	
   108	  /* Configure the GPIO pins as interrupting inputs. */
   109	
   110	  for (i = 0; i < NUM_BUTTONS; i++)
   111	    {
   112	      lpc17_40_configgpio(g_buttoncfg[i]);
   113	    }
   114	
   115	  return NUM_BUTTONS;
   116	}
   117	
   118	/****************************************************************************
   119	 * Name: board_buttons
   120	 *
   121	 * Description:
   122	 *   board_button_initialize() must be called to initialize button resources.
   123	 *   After that, board_buttons() may be called to collect the current state
   124	 *   of all buttons.
   125	 *
   126	 *   board_buttons() may be called at any time to harvest the state of every
   127	 *   button.  The state of the buttons is returned as a bitset with one
   128	 *   bit corresponding to each button:  If the bit is set, then the button
   129	 *   is pressed.  See the BOARD_BUTTON_*_BIT and BOARD_JOYSTICK_*_BIT
   130	 *   definitions in board.h for the meaning of each bit.
   131	 *
   132	 ****************************************************************************/
   133	
   134	uint32_t board_buttons(void)
   135	{
   136	  uint32_t ret = 0;
   137	  int i;
   138	
   139	  /* Check that state of each key */
   140	
   141	  for (i = 0; i < NUM_BUTTONS; i++)
   142	    {
   143	      /* A LOW value means that the key is pressed. */
   144	
   145	      bool released = lpc17_40_gpioread(g_buttoncfg[i]);
   146	
   147	      /* Accumulate the set of depressed (not released) keys */
   148	
   149	      if (!released)
   150	        {
   151	            ret |= (1 << i);
   152	        }
   153	    }
   154	
   155	  return ret;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/****************************************************************************
   159	 * Button support.
   160	 *
   161	 * Description:
   162	 *   board_button_initialize() must be called to initialize button resources.
   163	 *   After that, board_button_irq() may be called to register button
   164	 *   interrupt handlers.
   165	 *
   166	 *   board_button_irq() may be called to register an interrupt handler that
   167	 *   will be called when a button is depressed or released.  The ID value is
   168	 *   a button enumeration value that uniquely identifies a button resource.
   169	 *   See the BOARD_BUTTON_* and BOARD_JOYSTICK_* definitions in board.h for
   170	 *   the meaning of enumeration values.
   171	 *
   172	 *   Note that board_button_irq() also enables button interrupts.  Button
   173	 *   interrupts will remain enabled after the interrupt handler is attached.
   174	 *   Interrupts may be disabled (and detached) by calling board_button_irq
   175	 *   with irqhandler equal to NULL.
   176	 *
   177	 ****************************************************************************/
   178	
   179	#if defined(CONFIG_ARCH_IRQBUTTONS) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ)
   180	int board_button_irq(int id, xcpt_t irqhandler, void *arg)
   181	{
   182	  irqstate_t flags;
   183	  int ret = -EINVAL;
   184	  int irq;
   185	
   186	  /* Verify that the button ID is within range */
   187	
   188	  if ((unsigned)id < NUM_BUTTONS)
   189	    {
   190	      /* Get the IRQ number for the button; A value of zero indicates that
   191	       * the button does not support the interrupt function.
   192	       */
   193	
   194	      irq = g_buttonirq[id];
   195	      if (irq > 0)
   196	        {
   197	          /* Disable interrupts until we are done */
   198	
   199	          flags = enter_critical_section();
   200	
   201	          /* Configure the interrupt.  Either attach and enable the new
   202	           * interrupt or disable and detach the old interrupt handler.
   203	           */
   204	
   205	          if (irqhandler)
   206	            {
   207	              /* Attach then enable the new interrupt handler */
   208	
   209	              irq_attach(irq, irqhandler, arg);
   210	              up_enable_irq(irq);
   211	            }
   212	          else
   213	            {
   214	              /* Disable then detach the old interrupt handler */
   215	
   216	              up_disable_irq(irq);
   217	              irq_detach(irq);
   218	            }
   219	
   220	          leave_critical_section(flags);
   221	        }
   222	
   223	      ret = OK;
   224	    }
   225	
   226	  return ret;
   227	}
   228	#endif
   229	#endif /* CONFIG_ARCH_BUTTONS */

    22

    24	 * Included Files
    25	 ****************************************************************************/
    26	
    27	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    28	
    29	#include <stdint.h>
    30	#include <assert.h>
    31	
    32	#include <nuttx/debug.h>
    33	#include <nuttx/irq.h>
    34	#include <nuttx/arch.h>
    35	#include <nuttx/input/djoystick.h>
    36	
    37	#include "lpc17_40_gpio.h"
    38	#include "open1788.h"
    39	
    40	/****************************************************************************
    41	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    42	 ****************************************************************************/
    43	
    44	/* The Open1788 supports several buttons.
    45	 * All will read "1" when open and "0" when closed
    46	 *
    47	 *   USER1           Connected to P4[26]
    48	 *   USER2           Connected to P2[22]
    49	 *   USER3           Connected to P0[10]
    50	 *
    51	 * And a Joystick
    52	 *
    53	 *   JOY_A           Connected to P2[25]
    54	 *   JOY_B           Connected to P2[26]
    55	 *   JOY_C           Connected to P2[23]
    56	 *   JOY_D           Connected to P2[19]
    57	 *   JOY_CTR         Connected to P0[14]
    58	 *
    59	 * The switches are all connected to ground and should be pulled up and
    60	 * sensed with a value of '0' when closed.
    61	 *
    62	 * Mapping to DJOYSTICK buttons:
    63	 *
    64	 *   DJOY_UP         JOY_B
    65	 *   DJOY_DOWN       JOY_C
    66	 *   DJOY_LEFT       JOY_A
    67	 *   DJOY_RIGHT      JOY_D
    68	 *   DJOY_BUTTON_1   JOY_CTR
    69	 *   DJOY_BUTTON_2   USER1
    70	 *   DJOY_BUTTON_3   USER2
    71	 *   DJOY_BUTTON_4   USER3
    72	 */
    73	
    74	/* Number of Joystick discretes */
    75	
    76	#define DJOY_NGPIOS  8
    77	
    78	/****************************************************************************
    79	 * Private Function Prototypes
    80	 ****************************************************************************/
    81	
    82	static djoy_buttonset_t djoy_supported(
    83	                         const struct djoy_lowerhalf_s *lower);
    84	static djoy_buttonset_t djoy_sample(
    85	                         const struct djoy_lowerhalf_s *lower);
    86	static void djoy_enable(const struct djoy_lowerhalf_s *lower,
    87	                        djoy_buttonset_t press, djoy_buttonset_t release,
    88	                        djoy_interrupt_t handler, void *arg);
    89	
    90	static void djoy_disable(void);
    91	static int djoy_interrupt(int irq, void *context, void *arg);
    92	
    93	/****************************************************************************
    94	 * Private Data
    95	 ****************************************************************************/
    96	
    97	/* Pin configuration for each Open1788 joystick "button."  Indexed using
    98	 * DJOY_* definitions in include/nuttx/input/djoystick.h.
    99	 */
   100	
   101	static const lpc17_40_pinset_t g_joygpio[DJOY_NGPIOS] =
   102	{
   103	  GPIO_JOY_B,   GPIO_JOY_C, GPIO_JOY_A, GPIO_JOY_D,
   104	  GPIO_JOY_CTR, GPIO_USER1, GPIO_USER2, GPIO_USER3
   105	};
   106	
   107	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ
   108	/* This array provides the mapping from button ID numbers to button IRQ
   109	 * numbers.  Indexed using DJOY_* definitions in
   110	 * include/nuttx/input/djoystick.h.
   111	 */
   112	
   113	static const uint8_t g_buttonirq[DJOY_NGPIOS] =
   114	{
   115	  GPIO_JOY_B_IRQ,   GPIO_JOY_C_IRQ, GPIO_JOY_A_IRQ, GPIO_JOY_D_IRQ,
   116	  GPIO_JOY_CTR_IRQ, 0,              GPIO_USER2_IRQ, GPIO_USER3_IRQ
   117	};
   118	#endif
   119	
   120	/* Current interrupt handler and argument */
   121	
   122	static djoy_interrupt_t g_djoyhandler;
   123	static void *g_djoyarg;
   124	
   125	/* This is the discrete joystick lower half driver interface */
   126	
   127	static const struct djoy_lowerhalf_s g_djoylower =
   128	{
   129	  .dl_supported  = djoy_supported,
   130	  .dl_sample     = djoy_sample,
   131	  .dl_enable     = djoy_enable,
   132	};
   133	
   134	/****************************************************************************
   135	 * Private Functions
   136	 ****************************************************************************/
   137	
   138	/****************************************************************************
   139	 * Name: djoy_supported
   140	 *
   141	 * Description:
   142	 *   Return the set of buttons supported on the discrete joystick device
   143	 *
   144	 ****************************************************************************/
   145	
   146	static djoy_buttonset_t djoy_supported(
   147	                           const struct djoy_lowerhalf_s *lower)
   148	{
   149	  iinfo("Supported: %02x\n", DJOY_ALLBITS);
   150	  return (djoy_buttonset_t)DJOY_ALLBITS;
   151	}
   152	
   153	/****************************************************************************
   154	 * Name: djoy_sample
   155	 *
   156	 * Description:
   157	 *   Return the current state of all discrete joystick buttons
   158	 *
   159	 ****************************************************************************/
   160	
   161	static djoy_buttonset_t djoy_sample(const struct djoy_lowerhalf_s *lower)
   162	{
   163	  djoy_buttonset_t ret = 0;
   164	  int i;
   165	
   166	  /* Read each joystick GPIO value */
   167	
   168	  for (i = 0; i < DJOY_NGPIOS; i++)
   169	    {
   170	      /* A LOW value means that the key is pressed. */
   171	
   172	      bool released = lpc17_40_gpioread(g_joygpio[i]);
   173	
   174	      /* Accumulate the set of depressed (not released) keys */
   175	
   176	      if (!released)
   177	        {
   178	            ret |= (1 << i);
   179	        }
   180	    }
   181	
   182	  iinfo("Returning: %02x\n", DJOY_ALLBITS);
   183	  return ret;
   184	}
   185	
   186	/****************************************************************************
   187	 * Name: djoy_enable
   188	 *
   189	 * Description:
   190	 *   Enable interrupts on the selected set of joystick buttons.  And empty
   191	 *   set will disable all interrupts.
   192	 *
   193	 ****************************************************************************/
   194	
   195	static void djoy_enable(const struct djoy_lowerhalf_s *lower,
   196	                        djoy_buttonset_t press, djoy_buttonset_t release,
   197	                        djoy_interrupt_t handler, void *arg)
   198	{
   199	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ
   200	  irqstate_t flags;
   201	  djoy_buttonset_t either = press | release;
   202	  int irq;
   203	  int i;
   204	
   205	  iinfo("press: %02x release: %02x handler: %p arg: %p\n",
   206	        press, release, handler, arg);
   207	
   208	  /* Start with all interrupts disabled */
   209	
   210	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   211	  djoy_disable();
   212	
   213	  /* If no events are indicated or if no handler is provided, then this
   214	   * must really be a request to disable interrupts.
   215	   */
   216	
   217	  /* REVISIT: Currently does not distinguish press/release selections */
   218	
   219	  if (either && handler != NULL)
   220	    {
   221	      /* Save the new the handler and argument */
   222	
   223	      g_djoyhandler = handler;
   224	      g_djoyarg     = arg;
   225	
   226	      /* Attach and enable interrupts each GPIO. */
   227	
   228	      for (i = 0; i < DJOY_NGPIOS; i++)
   229	        {
   230	          irq = g_buttonirq[i];
   231	          if (irq > 0)
   232	            {
   233	              irq_attach(irq, djoy_interrupt, arg);
   234	              up_enable_irq(irq);
   235	            }
   236	        }
   237	    }
   238	
   239	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   240	#endif
   241	}
   242	
   243	/****************************************************************************
   244	 * Name: djoy_disable
   245	 *
   246	 * Description:
   247	 *   Disable all joystick interrupts
   248	 *
   249	 ****************************************************************************/
   250	
   251	static void djoy_disable(void)
   252	{
   253	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ
   254	  irqstate_t flags;
   255	  int irq;
   256	  int i;
   257	
   258	  /* Disable and detach all button handlers for each GPIO */
   259	
   260	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   261	  for (i = 0; i < DJOY_NGPIOS; i++)
   262	    {
   263	      irq = g_buttonirq[i];
   264	      if (irq > 0)
   265	        {
   266	          up_disable_irq(irq);
   267	          irq_detach(irq);
   268	        }
   269	    }
   270	
   271	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   272	#endif
   273	
   274	  /* Nullify the handler and argument */
   275	
   276	  g_djoyhandler = NULL;
   277	  g_djoyarg     = NULL;
   278	}
   279	
   280	/****************************************************************************
   281	 * Name: djoy_interrupt
   282	 *
   283	 * Description:
   284	 *   Discrete joystick interrupt handler
   285	 *
   286	 ****************************************************************************/
   287	
   288	static int djoy_interrupt(int irq, void *context, void *arg)
   289	{
   290	  DEBUGASSERT(g_djoyhandler != NULL);
   291	  if (g_djoyhandler != NULL)
   292	    {
   293	      g_djoyhandler(&g_djoylower, g_djoyarg);
   294	    }
   295	
   296	  return OK;
   297	}
   298	
   299	/****************************************************************************
   300	 * Public Functions
   301	 ****************************************************************************/
   302	
   303	/****************************************************************************
   304	 * Name: lpc17_40_djoy_initialization
   305	 *
   306	 * Description:
   307	 *   Initialize and register the discrete joystick driver
   308	 *
   309	 ****************************************************************************/
   310	
   311	int lpc17_40_djoy_initialization(void)
   312	{
   313	  int i;
   314	
   315	  /* Configure the GPIO pins as inputs.    NOTE: This is unnecessary for
   316	   * interrupting pins since it will also be done by lpc17_40_gpiosetevent().
   317	   */
   318	
   319	  for (i = 0; i < DJOY_NGPIOS; i++)
   320	    {
   321	      lpc17_40_configgpio(g_joygpio[i]);
   322	    }
   323	
   324	  /* Make sure that all interrupts are disabled */
   325	
   326	  djoy_disable();
   327	
   328	  /* Register the joystick device as /dev/djoy0 */
   329	
   330	  return djoy_register(CONFIG_OPEN1788_DJOYDEV, &g_djoylower);
   331	}

--- FILE: boards/arm/lpc17xx_40xx/open1788/src/lpc17_40_lcd.c ---
     1	#include <nuttx/config.h>
     2	#include <stdint.h>
     3	#include <stdbool.h>
     4	#include <stdio.h>
     5	#include <nuttx/arch.h>
     6	#include <nuttx/i2c/i2c_master.h>
     7	#include "lpc17_40_lcd.h"
     8	#include "lpc17_40_gpio.h"
     9	#include "lpc17_40_i2c.h"
    10	#include "open1788.h"
    11	
    12	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD
    13	#define EA_PCA9532_ADDR      0x64
    14	#define EA_PCA9532_PSC0_INC  (0x02 | 0x10)
    15	
    16	static struct i2c_master_s *g_lcd_i2c = NULL;
    17	
    18	static int ea_pca9532_write(struct i2c_master_s *i2c, uint8_t *buf, size_t len)
    19	{
    20	  struct i2c_msg_s msg = { .frequency = 100000, .addr = EA_PCA9532_ADDR,
    21	                           .flags = 0, .buffer = buf, .length = len };
    22	  return I2C_TRANSFER(i2c, &msg, 1);
    23	}
    24	
    25	void open1788_lcd_initialize(void) {}
    26	
    27	void ea_lcd_backlight_init(void)
    28	{
    29	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0
    30	  uint8_t init_all[] = { EA_PCA9532_PSC0_INC, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x00, 0x01, 0x01, 0x00 };
    31	  uint8_t pwr_on[]   = { 0x06, 0x01 };
    32	  uint8_t disp_on[]  = { 0x07, 0x00 };
    33	  uint8_t bl_on[]    = { 0x08, 0x00 };
    34	  int ret;
    35	
    36	  g_lcd_i2c = lpc17_40_i2cbus_initialize(0);
    37	  if (g_lcd_i2c != NULL)
    38	    {
    39	      ret  = ea_pca9532_write(g_lcd_i2c, init_all, sizeof(init_all));
    40	      up_mdelay(10);
    41	      ret |= ea_pca9532_write(g_lcd_i2c, pwr_on, sizeof(pwr_on));
    42	      up_mdelay(100);
    43	      ret |= ea_pca9532_write(g_lcd_i2c, disp_on, sizeof(disp_on));
    44	      up_mdelay(10);
    45	      ret |= ea_pca9532_write(g_lcd_i2c, bl_on, sizeof(bl_on));
    46	      printf("EA PCA9532 backlight init: ret=%d\n", ret);
    47	    }
    48	#endif
    49	}
    50	
    51	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BACKLIGHT
    52	void lpc17_40_backlight(bool blon)
    53	{
    54	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0
    55	  if (g_lcd_i2c != NULL)
    56	    {
    57	      uint8_t cmd[] = { 0x08, (uint8_t)(blon ? 0x00 : 0x01) };
    58	      ea_pca9532_write(g_lcd_i2c, cmd, sizeof(cmd));
    59	    }
    60	#else
    61	  (void)blon;
    62	#endif
    63	}
    64	#endif
    65	#endif

    24	 * Included Files
    25	 ****************************************************************************/

    27	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    28

    32	
    33	#include "arm_internal.h"

    37	
    38	/****************************************************************************
    39	 * Public Functions
    40	 ****************************************************************************/
    41	
    42	/****************************************************************************
    43	 * Name: open1788_nand_initialize
    44	 *
    45	 * Description:
    46	 *   Initialize NAND FLASH
    47	 *
    48	 ****************************************************************************/
    49	
    50	void open1788_nand_initialize(void)
    51	{
    52	  uint32_t regval;
    53	
    54	  /* Set the memory width and byte lanes */
    55	
    56	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_EMC_STATICCONFIG1);
    57	  regval &= ~EMC_STATICCONFIG_MW_MASK;
    58	  regbal |= (EMC_STATICCONFIG_MW_8BIT | EMC_STATICCONFIG_PB);
    59	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICCONFIG1);
    60	
    61	  /* Configure timing */
    62	
    63	  putreg32(2, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITWEN1);
    64	  putreg32(2, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITOEN1);
    65	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITRD1);
    66	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITPAGE1);
    67	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITWR1);
    68	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITTURN1);
    69	
    70	  /* GPIO P2[21] connects to the Ready/Busy pin of the NAND part.
    71	   * We need to reconfigure this pin as normal GPIO input.
    72	   */
    73	
    74	  lpc17_40_gpioconfig(GPIO_NAND_RB);
    75	}
    76	
    77	#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_EMC && CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTNAND */
