    37	
    38	/****************************************************************************
    39	 * Public Functions
    40	 ****************************************************************************/
    41	
    42	/****************************************************************************
    43	 * Name: open1788_nor_initialize
    44	 *
    45	 * Description:
    46	 *   Initialize NOR FLASH
    47	 *
    48	 ****************************************************************************/
    49	
    50	void open1788_nor_initialize(void)
    51	{
    52	  uint32_t regval;
    53	
    54	  /* Set the memory width and byte lanes */
    55	
    56	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_EMC_STATICCONFIG0);
    57	  regval &= ~EMC_STATICCONFIG_MW_MASK;
    58	  regbal |= (EMC_STATICCONFIG_MW_16BIT | EMC_STATICCONFIG_PB);
    59	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICCONFIG0);
    60	
    61	  /* Configure timing */
    62	
    63	  putreg32(2, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITWEN0);
    64	  putreg32(2, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITOEN0);
    65	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITRD0);
    66	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITPAGE0);
    67	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITWR0);
    68	  putreg32(31, LPC17_40_EMC_STATICWAITTURN0);
    69	
    70	  up_mdelay(10);
    71	}
    72	
    73	#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_EMC && CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTNOR */

     2	 * boards/arm/lpc17xx_40xx/open1788/src/lpc17_40_sdraminitialize.c

    22	
    23	/****************************************************************************
    24	 * Included Files
    25	 ****************************************************************************/
    26	
    27	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    28	
    29	#include <nuttx/debug.h>
    30	
    31	#include <nuttx/arch.h>
    32	#include <arch/board/board.h>
    33	
    34	#include "arm_internal.h"
    35	#include "hardware/lpc17_40_syscon.h"
    36	#include "lpc17_40_emc.h"
    37	
    38	#include "open1788.h"
    39	
    40	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EMC) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAM)
    41	
    42	/****************************************************************************
    43	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    44	 ****************************************************************************/
    45	
    46	/* The core clock is LPC17_40_EMCCLK which may be either LPC17_40_CCLK*
    47	 * (undivided), or LPC17_40_CCLK / 2 as determined by settings in the board.h
    48	 *  header file.
    49	 *
    50	 * For example:
    51	 *   LPC17_40_CCLCK      =  120,000,000
    52	 *   EMCCLKSEL        -> LPC17_40_CCLK divided by 2
    53	 *   LPC17_40_EMCCLK     =  60,000,000
    54	 *   LPC17_40_EMCCLK_MHZ =  60 (Rounded to an integer)
    55	 *   EMC_NSPERCLK     =  16.667 (Represented with 4 bits of fraction, 267)
    56	 *
    57	 *   EMC_NS2CLK(63)   = ((63 << 4) + 266) / 267 = 4 (actual 3.78)
    58	 *   EMC_NS2CLK(20)   = ((20 << 4) + 266) / 267 = 2 (actual 1.20)
    59	 */
    60	
    61	#define LPC17_40_EMCCLK_MHZ    ((LPC17_40_EMCCLK + 500000) / 1000000)
    62	#define EMC_NSPERCLK_B4     (((1000 << 4) + (LPC17_40_EMCCLK_MHZ >> 1)) / LPC17_40_EMCCLK_MHZ)
    63	#define EMC_NS2CLK(ns)      (((ns << 4) + (EMC_NSPERCLK_B4 - 1)) / EMC_NSPERCLK_B4)
    64	#define MDKCFG_RASCAS0VAL   0x00000303
    65	
    66	/* Set up for 32-bit SDRAM at CS0 */
    67	
    68	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMSIZE
    69	#  define SDRAM_SIZE CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMSIZE
    70	#endif
    71	
    72	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_16BIT
    73	#  ifndef SDRAM_SIZE
    74	#    define SDRAM_SIZE      0x02000000 /* 256Mbit */
    75	#  endif
    76	#else /* if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_32BIT) */
    77	#  undef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_32BIT
    78	#  define CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_32BIT 1
    79	#  ifndef SDRAM_SIZE
    80	#    define SDRAM_SIZE      0x04000000 /* 512Mbit */
    81	#  endif
    82	#endif
    83	
    84	#define SDRAM_BASE          0xa0000000 /* CS0 */
    85	
    86	/****************************************************************************
    87	 * Public Functions
    88	 ****************************************************************************/
    89	
    90	/****************************************************************************
    91	 * Name: open1788_sdram_initialize
    92	 *
    93	 * Description:
    94	 *   Initialize SDRAM
    95	 *
    96	 ****************************************************************************/
    97	
    98	void open1788_sdram_initialize(void)
    99	{
   100	  uint32_t regval;
   101	  int i;
   102	
   103	  /* Reconfigure delays:
   104	   *
   105	   * CMDDLY: Programmable delay value for EMC outputs in command delayed
   106	   *   mode.  The delay amount is roughly CMDDLY * 250 picoseconds.
   107	   * FBCLKDLY: Programmable delay value for the feedback clock that controls
   108	   *   input data sampling.  The delay amount is roughly (FBCLKDLY+1) * 250
   109	   *   picoseconds.
   110	   * CLKOUT0DLY: Programmable delay value for the CLKOUT0 output. This would
   111	   *   typically be used in clock delayed mode.  The delay amount is roughly
   112	   *  (CLKOUT0DLY+1) * 250 picoseconds.
   113	   * CLKOUT1DLY: Programmable delay value for the CLKOUT1 output. This would
   114	   *  typically be used in clock delayed mode.  The delay amount is roughly
   115	   *  (CLKOUT1DLY+1) * 250 picoseconds.
   116	   */
   117	
   118	  regval = SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL_CMDDLY(32) |
   119	           SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL_FBCLKDLY(32) |
   120	           SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL_CLKOUT0DLY(1) |
   121	           SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL_CLKOUT1DLY(1);
   122	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL);
   123	
   124	  /* Configure the SDRAM */
   125	
   126	  putreg32(EMC_NS2CLK(20), LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICRP);   /* TRP   = 20 nS */
   127	
   128	  putreg32(15, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICRAS); /* RAS   = 42ns to 100K ns,  */
   129	  putreg32(0, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICSREX); /* TSREX = 1 clock */
   130	  putreg32(1, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICAPR);  /* TAPR  = 2 clocks? */
   131	
   132	  putreg32(EMC_NS2CLK(20) + 2, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICDAL);  /* TDAL  = TRP + TDPL = 20ns + 2clk  */
   133	
   134	  putreg32(1, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICWR);                /* TWR   = 2 clocks */
   135	  putreg32(EMC_NS2CLK(63), LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICRC);   /* H57V2562GTR-75C TRC = 63ns(min) */
   136	  putreg32(EMC_NS2CLK(63), LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICRFC);  /* H57V2562GTR-75C TRFC = TRC */
   137	  putreg32(15, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICXSR);              /* Exit self-refresh to active */
   138	  putreg32(EMC_NS2CLK(63), LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICRRD);  /* 3 clock, TRRD = 15ns (min) */
   139	  putreg32(1, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICMRD);               /* 2 clock, TMRD = 2 clocks (min) */
   140	
   141	  /* Command delayed strategy, using EMCCLKDELAY */
   142	
   143	  putreg32(EMC_DYNAMICREADCONFIG_RD_CMD, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICREADCONFIG);
   144	
   145	  /* H57V2562GTR-75C: TCL=3CLK, TRCD = 20ns(min), 3 CLK = 24ns */
   146	
   147	  putreg32(MDKCFG_RASCAS0VAL, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICRASCAS0);
   148	
   149	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_16BIT
   150	  /* For Manley lpc1778 SDRAM:
   151	   * H57V2562GTR-75C, 256Mb, 16Mx16, 4 banks, row=13, column=9:
   152	   *
   153	   * 256Mb, 16Mx16, 4 banks, row=13, column=9, RBC
   154	   */
   155	
   156	  putreg32(EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG_MD_SDRAM |  EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG_AM0(13),
   157	           LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG0);
   158	
   159	#elif defined CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_32BIT
   160	  /* 256Mb, 16Mx16, 4 banks, row=13, column=9, RBC */
   161	
   162	  putreg32(EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG_MD_SDRAM |  EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG_AM0(13) |
   163	           EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG_AM1, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG0);
   164	#endif
   165	
   166	  up_mdelay(100);
   167	
   168	  /* Issue NOP command */
   169	
   170	  putreg32(EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_CE | EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_CS |
   171	           EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_I_NOP, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL);
   172	
   173	  /* Wait 200 Msec */
   174	
   175	  up_mdelay(200);
   176	
   177	  /* Issue PALL command */
   178	
   179	  putreg32(EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_CE | EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_CS |
   180	           EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_I_PALL, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL);
   181	
   182	  putreg32(2, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICREFRESH); /* ( n * 16 ) -> 32 clock cycles */
   183	
   184	  /* Wait 128 AHB clock cycles */
   185	
   186	  for (i = 0; i < 128; i++);
   187	
   188	  /* 64ms/8192 = 7.8125us, nx16x8.33ns < 7.8125us, n < 58.6 */
   189	
   190	  regval = 64000000 / (1 << 13);
   191	  regval -= 16;
   192	  regval >>= 4;
   193	  regval = regval * LPC17_40_EMCCLK_MHZ / 1000;
   194	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICREFRESH);
   195	
   196	  /* Issue MODE command */
   197	
   198	  putreg32(EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_CE | EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_CS |
   199	           EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_I_MODE, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL);
   200	
   201	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_16BIT
   202	  getreg16(SDRAM_BASE | (0x33 << 12));  /* 8 burst, 3 CAS latency */
   203	#elif defined CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDRAM_32BIT
   204	  getreg32(SDRAM_BASE | (0x32 << 13)); /* 4 burst, 3 CAS latency */
   205	#endif
   206	
   207	  /* Issue NORMAL command */
   208	
   209	  putreg32(EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL_I_NORMAL, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONTROL);
   210	
   211	  /* Enable buffer */
   212	
   213	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG0);
   214	  regval |= EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG_B;
   215	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_EMC_DYNAMICCONFIG0);
   216	  up_mdelay(12);
   217	
   218	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL);
   219	  regval &= ~SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL_CMDDLY_MASK;
   220	  regval |= SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL_CMDDLY(18);
   221	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_EMCDLYCTL);
   222	}
   223	
   224	#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_EMC && CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAM */

    87	  ssp_dumpgpio(GPIO_TC_CS, "AFTER SSP1 Initialization");
    88	#endif
    89	
    90	  /* Configure the SSP2 chip select GPIOs. */
    91	
    92	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP2
    93	#endif
    94	}

    96	/****************************************************************************
    97	 * Name:  lpc17_40_ssp0/1/2select and lpc17_40_ssp0/1/2status
    98	 *
    99	 * Description:
   100	 *   The external functions, lpc17_40_ssp0/1/2select and
   101	 *   lpc17_40_ssp0/1/2status must be provided by board-specific logic.
   102	 *   They are implementations of the select and status methods of the SPI
   103	 *   interface defined by struct spi_ops_s (see include/nuttx/spi/spi.h).
   104	 *   All other methods (including lpc17_40_sspbus_initialize())
   105	 *   are provided by common LPC17xx/LPC40xx logic.
   106	 *   To use this common SPI logic on your board:
   107	 *
   108	 *   1. Provide logic in lpc17_40_boardinitialize() to configure SPI/SSP
   109	 *      chip select pins.
   110	 *   2. Provide lpc17_40_ssp0/1/2select() and lpc17_40_ssp0/1/2status()
   111	 *      functions in your board-specific logic.  These functions will perform
   112	 *      chip selection and status operations using GPIOs in the way your
   113	 *      board is configured.
   114	 *   3. Add a calls to lpc17_40_sspbus_initialize() in your low level
   115	 *      application initialization logic
   116	 *   4. The handle returned by lpc17_40_sspbus_initialize() may then be used
   117	 *      to bind the SPI driver to higher level logic (e.g., calling
   118	 *      mmcsd_spislotinitialize(), for example, will bind the SPI driver to
   119	 *      the SPI MMC/SD driver).
   120	 *
   121	 ****************************************************************************/
   122	
   123	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP0
   124	void  lpc17_40_ssp0select(struct spi_dev_s *dev, uint32_t devid,
   125	                          bool selected)
   126	{
   127	  spiinfo("devid: %d CS: %s\n", (int)devid,
   128	          selected ? "assert" : "de-assert");
   129	  if (devid == SPIDEV_TOUCHSCREEN(0))
   130	    {
   131	      /* Assert/de-assert the CS pin to the touchscreen */
   132	
   133	      ssp_dumpgpio(GPIO_TC_CS, "lpc17_40_ssp0select() Entry");
   134	      lpc17_40_gpiowrite(GPIO_TC_CS, !selected);
   135	      ssp_dumpgpio(GPIO_TC_CS, "lpc17_40_ssp0select() Exit");
   136	    }
   137	}
   138	
   139	uint8_t lpc17_40_ssp0status(struct spi_dev_s *dev, uint32_t devid)
   140	{
   141	  spiinfo("Returning nothing\n");
   142	  return 0;
   143	}
   144	#endif
   145

   151	          selected ? "assert" : "de-assert");

   153	    {
   154	      /* Assert/de-assert the CS pin to the touchscreen */
   155	
   156	      ssp_dumpgpio(GPIO_TC_CS, "lpc17_40_ssp1select() Entry");

   158	      ssp_dumpgpio(GPIO_TC_CS, "lpc17_40_ssp1select() Exit");
   159	    }
   160	}
   161

    36	#include <nuttx/irq.h>
    37	#include <nuttx/wdog.h>
    38	#include <nuttx/clock.h>
    39	#include <nuttx/spi/spi.h>
    40	#include <nuttx/input/touchscreen.h>
    41	#include <nuttx/input/ads7843e.h>

    43	#include "arm_internal.h"
    44	#include "lpc17_40_gpio.h"
    45	#include "hardware/lpc17_40_gpio.h"

    49	#ifdef CONFIG_INPUT_ADS7843E
    50	
    51	/****************************************************************************
    52	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    53	 ****************************************************************************/
    54	
    55	/* Configuration ************************************************************/
    56	
    57	#ifndef CONFIG_INPUT
    58	#  error "Touchscreen support requires CONFIG_INPUT"
    59	#endif
    60	
    61	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP0
    62	#  error "Touchscreen support requires CONFIG_LPC17_40_SSP0"
    63	#endif
    64	
    65	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ
    66	#  error "Touchscreen support requires CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPIOIRQ"
    67	#endif
    68	
    69	#ifndef CONFIG_ADS7843E_FREQUENCY
    70	#  define CONFIG_ADS7843E_FREQUENCY 500000
    71	#endif
    72	
    73	#ifndef CONFIG_ADS7843E_SPIDEV
    74	#  define CONFIG_ADS7843E_SPIDEV 0
    75	#endif
    76	
    77	#if CONFIG_ADS7843E_SPIDEV != 0
    78	#  error "CONFIG_ADS7843E_SPIDEV must be one"
    79	#endif
    80	
    81	#ifndef CONFIG_ADS7843E_DEVMINOR
    82	#  define CONFIG_ADS7843E_DEVMINOR 0
    83	#endif
    84	
    85	/* REVISIT:  Currently, XPT2046 reports BUSY all of the time.  This is
    86	 * probably GPIO setting issues.  But there is this cryptic statement in
    87	 * the XPT2046 spec:  "No DCLK delay required with dedicated serial port."
    88	 *
    89	 * The busy state is used by the XPT2046 driver to control the delay
    90	 * between sending the command, then reading the returned data.
    91	 */
    92	
    93	#define XPT2046_NO_BUSY 1
    94	
    95	/****************************************************************************
    96	 * Private Types
    97	 ****************************************************************************/
    98	
    99	/****************************************************************************
   100	 * Private Function Prototypes
   101	 ****************************************************************************/
   102	
   103	/* IRQ/GPIO access callbacks.  These operations all hidden behind
   104	 * callbacks to isolate the XPT2046 driver from differences in GPIO
   105	 * interrupt handling by varying boards and MCUs.  If possible,
   106	 * interrupts should be configured on both rising and falling edges
   107	 * so that contact and loss-of-contact events can be detected.
   108	 *
   109	 * attach  - Attach the XPT2046 interrupt handler to the GPIO interrupt
   110	 * enable  - Enable or disable the GPIO interrupt
   111	 * clear   - Acknowledge/clear any pending GPIO interrupt
   112	 * pendown - Return the state of the pen down GPIO input
   113	 */
   114	
   115	static int  tsc_attach(struct ads7843e_config_s *state, xcpt_t isr);
   116	static void tsc_enable(struct ads7843e_config_s *state, bool enable);
   117	static void tsc_clear(struct ads7843e_config_s *state);
   118	static bool tsc_busy(struct ads7843e_config_s *state);
   119	static bool tsc_pendown(struct ads7843e_config_s *state);
   120	
   121	/****************************************************************************
   122	 * Private Data
   123	 ****************************************************************************/
   124	
   125	/* A reference to a structure of this type must be passed to the XPT2046
   126	 * driver.  This structure provides information about the configuration
   127	 * of the XPT2046 and provides some board-specific hooks.
   128	 *
   129	 * Memory for this structure is provided by the caller.  It is not copied
   130	 * by the driver and is presumed to persist while the driver is active.
   131	 */
   132	
   133	static struct ads7843e_config_s g_tscinfo =
   134	{
   135	  .frequency = CONFIG_ADS7843E_FREQUENCY,
   136	  .attach    = tsc_attach,
   137	  .enable    = tsc_enable,
   138	  .clear     = tsc_clear,
   139	  .busy      = tsc_busy,
   140	  .pendown   = tsc_pendown,
   141	};
   142	
   143	/****************************************************************************
   144	 * Private Functions
   145	 ****************************************************************************/
   146	
   147	/* IRQ/GPIO access callbacks.  These operations all hidden behind
   148	 * callbacks to isolate the XPT2046 driver from differences in GPIO
   149	 * interrupt handling by varying boards and MCUs.  If possible,
   150	 * interrupts should be configured on both rising and falling edges
   151	 * so that contact and loss-of-contact events can be detected.
   152	 *
   153	 * attach  - Attach the XPT2046 interrupt handler to the GPIO interrupt
   154	 * enable  - Enable or disable the GPIO interrupt
   155	 * clear   - Acknowledge/clear any pending GPIO interrupt
   156	 * pendown - Return the state of the pen down GPIO input
   157	 */
   158	
   159	static xcpt_t g_tsc_upper_isr = NULL;
   160	static volatile bool g_tsc_irq_fired = false;
   161	
   162	static int tsc_isr_wrapper(int irq, void *context, void *arg)
   163	{
   164	  g_tsc_irq_fired = true;
   165	  return g_tsc_upper_isr ? g_tsc_upper_isr(irq, context, arg) : OK;
   166	}
   167	
   168	static int tsc_attach(struct ads7843e_config_s *state, xcpt_t handler)
   169	{
   170	  g_tsc_upper_isr = handler;
   171	  irq_attach(LPC17_40_IRQ_PENIRQ, tsc_isr_wrapper, NULL);
   172	  return OK;
   173	}
   174	
   175	static void tsc_enable(struct ads7843e_config_s *state, bool enable)
   176	{
   177	  iinfo("enable:%d\n", enable);
   178	  if (enable)
   179	    {
   180	      /* Enable PENIRQ interrupts.
   181	       * NOTE: The pin interrupt is enabled from worker thread
   182	       * logic after completion of processing of the touchscreen interrupt.
   183	       */
   184	
   185	      g_tsc_irq_fired = false;
   186	      putreg32((1 << 15), LPC17_40_GPIOINT2_INTCLR);
   187	      up_enable_irq(LPC17_40_IRQ_PENIRQ);
   188	    }
   189	  else
   190	    {
   191	      /* Disable PENIRQ interrupts.
   192	       * NOTE: The PENIRQ interrupt will be disabled from
   193	       * interrupt handling logic.
   194	       */
   195	
   196	      up_disable_irq(LPC17_40_IRQ_PENIRQ);
   197	    }
   198	}
   199	
   200	static void tsc_clear(struct ads7843e_config_s *state)
   201	{
   202	  putreg32((1 << 15), LPC17_40_GPIOINT2_INTCLR);
   203	}
   204	
   205	static bool tsc_busy(struct ads7843e_config_s *state)
   206	{
   207	/* The busy state is used by the XPT2046 driver to control the delay
   208	 * between sending the command, then reading the returned data.
   209	 */
   210	
   211	#if 1
   212	/* REVISIT:  Currently, XPT2046 reports BUSY all of the time.  This is
   213	 * probably GPIO setting issues.  But there is this cryptic statement in
   214	 * the XPT2046 spec:  "No DCLK delay required with dedicated serial port."
   215	 */
   216	
   217	  return false;
   218	
   219	#else /* XPT2046_NO_BUSY */
   220	
   221	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_INPUT) && defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_INFO)
   222	  static bool last = (bool)-1;
   223	#endif
   224	
   225	  /* REVISIT:  This might need to be inverted */
   226	
   227	  bool busy = lpc17_40_gpioread(GPIO_TC_BUSY);
   228	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_INPUT) && defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_INFO)
   229	  if (busy != last)
   230	    {
   231	      iinfo("busy:%d\n", busy);
   232	      last = busy;
   233	    }
   234	#endif
   235	
   236	  return busy;
   237	
   238	#endif /* XPT2046_NO_BUSY */
   239	}
   240	
   241	static bool tsc_pendown(struct ads7843e_config_s *state)
   242	{
   243	  /* XPT2046 uses an an internal pullup resistor. The PENIRQ output goes low
   244	   * due to the current path through the touch screen to ground, which
   245	   * initiates an interrupt to the processor via TP_INT.
   246	   */
   247	
   248	  bool pendown = !lpc17_40_gpioread(GPIO_TC_PENIRQ) || g_tsc_irq_fired;
   249	  g_tsc_irq_fired = false;
   250	  iinfo("pendown:%d\n", pendown);
   251	  return pendown;
   252	}
   253	
   254	/****************************************************************************
   255	 * Public Functions
   256	 ****************************************************************************/
   257	
   258	/****************************************************************************
   259	 * Name: open1788_tsc_setup
   260	 *
   261	 * Description:
   262	 *   This function is called by board-bringup logic to configure the
   263	 *   touchscreen device.  This function will register the driver as
   264	 *   /dev/inputN where N is the minor device number.
   265	 *
   266	 * Input Parameters:
   267	 *   minor - The input device minor number
   268	 *
   269	 * Returned Value:
   270	 *   Zero is returned on success.  Otherwise, a negated errno value is
   271	 *   returned to indicate the nature of the failure.
   272	 *
   273	 ****************************************************************************/
   274	
   275	int open1788_tsc_setup(int minor)
   276	{
   277	  struct spi_dev_s *dev;
   278	  int ret;
   279	
   280	  iinfo("minor:%d\n", minor);
   281	  DEBUGASSERT(minor == 0);
   282	
   283	  /* Configure and enable the XPT2046 PENIRQ pin as an interrupting input. */
   284	
   285	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_TC_PENIRQ);
   286	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_TC_CS);
   287	
   288	  /* Configure the XPT2046 BUSY pin as a normal input. */
   289	
   290	#ifndef XPT2046_NO_BUSY
   291	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_TC_BUSY);
   292	#endif
   293	
   294	  /* Get an instance of the SPI interface */
   295	
   296	  dev = lpc17_40_sspbus_initialize(CONFIG_ADS7843E_SPIDEV);
   297	  if (!dev)
   298	    {
   299	      ierr("ERROR: Failed to initialize SPI bus %d\n",
   300	           CONFIG_ADS7843E_SPIDEV);
   301	      return -ENODEV;
   302	    }
   303	
   304	  /* Initialize and register the SPI touchscreen device */
   305	
   306	  ret = ads7843e_register(dev, &g_tscinfo, CONFIG_ADS7843E_DEVMINOR);
   307	  if (ret < 0)
   308	    {
   309	      ierr("ERROR: Failed to register touchscreen device minor=%d\n",
   310	           CONFIG_ADS7843E_DEVMINOR);
   311	
   312	      /* up_spiuninitialize(dev); */
   313	
   314	      return -ENODEV;
   315	    }
   316	
   317	  return OK;
   318	}
   319	
   320	#endif /* CONFIG_INPUT_ADS7843E */

--- FILE: arch/arm/src/lpc17xx_40xx/lpc17_40_start.c ---
     1	/****************************************************************************
     2	 * arch/arm/src/lpc17xx_40xx/lpc17_40_start.c
     3	 *
     4	 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     5	 *
     6	 * Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one or more
     7	 * contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file distributed with
     8	 * this work for additional information regarding copyright ownership.  The
     9	 * ASF licenses this file to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
    10	 * "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance with the
    11	 * License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
    12	 *
    13	 *   http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
    14	 *
    15	 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
    16	 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT
    17	 * WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.  See the
    18	 * License for the specific language governing permissions and limitations
    19	 * under the License.
    20	 *
    21	 ****************************************************************************/
    22	
    23	/****************************************************************************
    24	 * Included Files
    25	 ****************************************************************************/
    26	
    27	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    28	
    29	#include <stdint.h>
    30	#include <assert.h>
    31	#include <nuttx/debug.h>
    32	
    33	#include <nuttx/init.h>
    34	
    35	#include <arch/irq.h>
    36	
    37	#include "arm_internal.h"
    38	#include "nvic.h"
    39	
    40	#include "lpc17_40_clockconfig.h"
    41	#include "lpc17_40_lowputc.h"
    42	#include "lpc17_40_userspace.h"
    43	#include "lpc17_40_start.h"
    44	
    45	/****************************************************************************
    46	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    47	 ****************************************************************************/
    48	
    49	/* .data is positioned first in the primary RAM followed immediately by .bss.
    50	 * The IDLE thread stack lies just after .bss and has size give by
    51	 * CONFIG_IDLETHREAD_STACKSIZE;  The heap then begins just after the IDLE.
    52	 * ARM EABI requires 64 bit stack alignment.
    53	 */
    54	
    55	#define HEAP_BASE      ((uintptr_t)_ebss + CONFIG_IDLETHREAD_STACKSIZE)
    56	
    57	/****************************************************************************
    58	 * Public Data
    59	 ****************************************************************************/
    60	
    61	/* g_idle_topstack: _sbss is the start of the BSS region as defined by the
    62	 * linker script. _ebss lies at the end of the BSS region. The idle task
    63	 * stack starts at the end of BSS and is of size CONFIG_IDLETHREAD_STACKSIZE.
    64	 * The IDLE thread is the thread that the system boots on and, eventually,
    65	 * becomes the IDLE, do nothing task that runs only when there is nothing
    66	 * else to run.  The heap continues from there until the end of memory.
    67	 * g_idle_topstack is a read-only variable the provides this computed
    68	 * address.
    69	 */
    70	
    71	const uintptr_t g_idle_topstack = HEAP_BASE;
    72	
    73	/****************************************************************************
    74	 * Private Functions
    75	 ****************************************************************************/
    76	
    77	/****************************************************************************
    78	 * Name: showprogress
    79	 *
    80	 * Description:
    81	 *   Print a character on the UART to show boot status.
    82	 *
    83	 ****************************************************************************/
    84	
    85	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_FEATURES
    86	#  define showprogress(c) arm_lowputc(c)
    87	#else
    88	#  define showprogress(c)
    89	#endif
    90	
    91	/****************************************************************************
    92	 * Public Functions
    93	 ****************************************************************************/
    94	
    95	#ifdef CONFIG_ARMV7M_STACKCHECK
    96	/* we need to get r10 set before we can allow instrumentation calls */
    97	
    98	void __start(void) noinstrument_function;
    99	#endif
   100	
   101	/****************************************************************************
   102	 * Name: __start
   103	 *
   104	 * Description:
   105	 *   This is the reset entry point.
   106	 *
   107	 ****************************************************************************/
   108	
   109	void __start(void)
   110	{
   111	  const uint32_t *src;
   112	  uint32_t *dest;
   113	
   114	  /* Disable interrupts for the case that started by loader */
   115	
   116	  cpsid();
   117	
   118	#ifdef CONFIG_ARMV7M_STACKCHECK
   119	  /* Set the stack limit before we attempt to call any functions */
   120	
   121	  __asm__ volatile("sub r10, sp, %0" : :
   122	                   "r"(CONFIG_IDLETHREAD_STACKSIZE - 64) :);
   123	#endif
   124	
   125	  /* Configure the uart so that we can get debug output as soon as possible */
   126	
   127	  lpc17_40_clockconfig();
   128	  arm_fpuconfig();
   129	  lpc17_40_lowsetup();
   130	  showprogress('A');
   131	
   132	  /* Clear .bss.  We'll do this inline (vs. calling memset) just to be
   133	   * certain that there are no issues with the state of global variables.
   134	   */
   135	
   136	  for (dest = (uint32_t *)_sbss; dest < (uint32_t *)_ebss; )
   137	    {
   138	      *dest++ = 0;
   139	    }
   140	
   141	  showprogress('B');
   142	
   143	  /* Move the initialized data section from his temporary holding spot in
   144	   * FLASH into the correct place in SRAM.  The correct place in SRAM is
   145	   * give by _sdata and _edata.  The temporary location is in FLASH at the
   146	   * end of all of the other read-only data (.text, .rodata) at _eronly.
   147	   */
   148	
   149	  for (src = (const uint32_t *)_eronly,
   150	       dest = (uint32_t *)_sdata; dest < (uint32_t *)_edata;
   151	      )
   152	    {
   153	      *dest++ = *src++;
   154	    }
   155	
   156	  showprogress('C');
   157	
   158	#ifdef CONFIG_ARMV7M_STACKCHECK
   159	  arm_stack_check_init();
   160	#endif
   161	
   162	  /* Perform early serial initialization */
   163	
   164	#ifdef USE_EARLYSERIALINIT
   165	  arm_earlyserialinit();
   166	#endif
   167	  showprogress('D');
   168	
   169	  /* For the case of the separate user-/kernel-space build, perform whatever
   170	   * platform specific initialization of the user memory is required.
   171	   * Normally this just means initializing the user space .data and .bss
   172	   * segments.
   173	   */
   174	
   175	#ifdef CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED
   176	  lpc17_40_userspace();
   177	  showprogress('E');
   178	#endif
   179	
   180	  /* Initialize onboard resources */
   181	
   182	  lpc17_40_boardinitialize();
   183	  showprogress('F');
   184	
   185	  /* Then start NuttX */
   186	
   187	  showprogress('\r');
   188	  showprogress('\n');
   189	  nx_start();
   190	
   191	  /* Shouldn't get here */
   192	
   193	  for (; ; );
   194	}

--- FILE: arch/arm/src/lpc17xx_40xx/lpc17_40_allocateheap.c ---
     1	/****************************************************************************
     2	 * arch/arm/src/lpc17xx_40xx/lpc17_40_allocateheap.c
     3	 *
     4	 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     5	 *
     6	 * Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one or more
     7	 * contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file distributed with
     8	 * this work for additional information regarding copyright ownership.  The
     9	 * ASF licenses this file to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
    10	 * "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance with the
    11	 * License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
    12	 *
    13	 *   http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
    14	 *
    15	 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
    16	 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT
    17	 * WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.  See the
    18	 * License for the specific language governing permissions and limitations
    19	 * under the License.
    20	 *
    21	 ****************************************************************************/
    22	
    23	/****************************************************************************
    24	 * Included Files
    25	 ****************************************************************************/
    26	
    27	#include <nuttx/config.h>
    28	
    29	#include <sys/types.h>
    30	#include <assert.h>
    31	#include <nuttx/debug.h>
    32	
    33	#include <nuttx/arch.h>
    34	#include <nuttx/board.h>
    35	#include <nuttx/kmalloc.h>
    36	#include <arch/board/board.h>
    37	
    38	#include "chip.h"
    39	#include "mpu.h"
    40	#include "arm_internal.h"
    41	#include "hardware/lpc17_40_memorymap.h"
    42	#include "lpc17_40_emacram.h"
    43	#include "lpc17_40_ohciram.h"
    44	#include "lpc17_40_mpuinit.h"
    45	#include "lpc17_40_start.h"
    46	
    47	/****************************************************************************
    48	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    49	 ****************************************************************************/
    50	
    51	/* Configuration ************************************************************/
    52	
    53	/* The configured RAM start address must be the beginning of CPU SRAM */
    54	
    55	#if CONFIG_RAM_START != LPC17_40_SRAM_BASE
    56	#  warning "CONFIG_RAM_START is not at LPC17_40_SRAM_BASE"
    57	#  undef CONFIG_RAM_START
    58	#  undef CONFIG_RAM_END
    59	#  define CONFIG_RAM_START LPC17_40_SRAM_BASE
    60	#  define CONFIG_RAM_END  (LPC17_40_SRAM_BASE+LPC17_40_CPUSRAM_SIZE)
    61	#endif
    62	
    63	/* The configured RAM size must be less then or equal to the CPU SRAM size */
    64	
    65	#if CONFIG_RAM_SIZE > LPC17_40_CPUSRAM_SIZE
    66	#  warning "CONFIG_RAM_SIZE is larger than the size of CPU SRAM"
    67	#  undef CONFIG_RAM_SIZE
    68	#  undef CONFIG_RAM_END
    69	#  define CONFIG_RAM_SIZE LPC17_40_CPUSRAM_SIZE
    70	#  define CONFIG_RAM_END (LPC17_40_SRAM_BASE+LPC17_40_CPUSRAM_SIZE)
    71	#elif CONFIG_RAM_SIZE < LPC17_40_CPUSRAM_SIZE
    72	#  warning "CONFIG_RAM_END is before end of CPU SRAM... not all of CPU SRAM used"
    73	#endif
    74	
    75	/* Figure out how much heap be have in AHB SRAM (if any).  Complications:
    76	 * 1) AHB SRAM Bank 0 or 1 may on may not be supported in the hardware.
    77	 * 2) Some or all of Bank 0 may be used for Ethernet Packet buffering.
    78	 *    Ethernet packet buffering is used from the beginning of Bank 0 and
    79	 *    any free memory at the end of Bank 0 will be contiguous with any
    80	 *    free memory at the beginning of Bank 1.
    81	 * 3) Some or all of Bank 1 may be used for OHCI descriptor memory.  OCHI
    82	 *    memory is used from the end of Bank 1 and any free memory at the
    83	 *    beginning of Bank 1 will be contiguous with any free memory at the
    84	 *    end of Bank 0.
    85	 */
    86	
    87	#undef LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPBASE /* Assume that nothing is available */
    88	#undef LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPSIZE
    89	
    90	/* If we have Bank 0, then we may possibly also have Bank 1 */
    91	
    92	#ifdef LPC17_40_HAVE_BANK0
    93	
    94	/* We have BANK0 (and, hence, possibly Bank1).  Is Bank0 all used for
    95	 * Ethernet packet buffering?  Or is there any part of Bank0 available for
    96	 * the heap.
    97	 */
    98	
    99	#  ifdef LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPSIZE
   100	
   101	/* Some or all of Bank0 is available for the heap.  The heap will begin
   102	 * in bank 1.
   103	 */
   104	
   105	#     define LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPBASE LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPBASE
   106	
   107	/* Is Bank1 present? Has there available heap memory in Bank 1? */
   108	
   109	#    if defined(LPC17_40_HAVE_BANK1) && defined(LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE)
   110	
   111	/* Yes... the heap space available is the unused memory at the end
   112	 * of Bank0 plus the unused memory at the beginning of Bank 1.
   113	 */
   114	
   115	#       define LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPSIZE (LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPSIZE + LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE)
   116	#    else
   117	
   118	/* No... the heap space available is only the unused memory at the
   119	 * end of Bank 0.
   120	 */
   121	
   122	#       define LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPSIZE LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPSIZE
   123	
   124	#    endif /* LPC17_40_HAVE_BANK1 && LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE */
   125	#  else /* !LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPSIZE */
   126	
   127	/* We have Bank 0, but no memory is available for the heap there.
   128	 * Do we have Bank 1? Is any heap memory available in Bank 1?
   129	 */
   130	
   131	#    if defined(LPC17_40_HAVE_BANK1) && defined(LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE)
   132	
   133	/* Yes... the heap space available is the unused memory at the
   134	 * beginning of Bank1.
   135	 */
   136	
   137	#      define LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPBASE LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPBASE
   138	#      define LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPSIZE LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE
   139	
   140	#    endif /* LPC17_40_HAVE_BANK1 && LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE */
   141	#  endif /* LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPSIZE */
   142	#endif /* LPC17_40_HAVE_BANK0 */
   143	
   144	/* Sanity checking */
   145	
   146	#if !defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMHEAP) && !defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTSRAM0HEAP)
   147	#  define LPC17_40_EXT_MM_REGIONS 0
   148	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMHEAP) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTSRAM0HEAP)
   149	#  define LPC17_40_EXT_MM_REGIONS 2
   150	#else
   151	#  define LPC17_40_EXT_MM_REGIONS 1
   152	#endif
   153	
   154	#ifdef LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPBASE
   155	#  if CONFIG_MM_REGIONS < 2 + LPC17_40_EXT_MM_REGIONS
   156	#    warning "CONFIG_MM_REGIONS < 2: Available AHB SRAM Bank(s) not included in HEAP"
   157	#  endif
   158	#  if (CONFIG_MM_REGIONS > 2 + LPC17_40_EXT_MM_REGIONS)
   159	#    warning "CONFIG_MM_REGIONS > 2: Are additional regions handled by application?"
   160	#  endif
   161	#else
   162	#  if CONFIG_MM_REGIONS > 1 + LPC17_40_EXT_MM_REGIONS
   163	#    warning "CONFIG_MM_REGIONS > 1: This configuration has no available AHB SRAM Bank0/1"
   164	#    warning "CONFIG_MM_REGIONS > 1: Are additional regions handled by application?"
   165	#  endif
   166	#endif
   167	
   168	/****************************************************************************
   169	 * Public Functions
   170	 ****************************************************************************/
   171	
   172	/****************************************************************************
   173	 * Name: up_allocate_heap
   174	 *
   175	 * Description:
   176	 *   This function will be called to dynamically set aside the heap region.
   177	 *
   178	 *   For the kernel build (CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED=y) with both kernel- and
   179	 *   user-space heaps (CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP=y), this function provides the
   180	 *   size of the unprotected, user-space heap.
   181	 *
   182	 *   If a protected kernel-space heap is provided, the kernel heap must be
   183	 *   allocated (and protected) by an analogous up_allocate_kheap().
   184	 *
   185	 *   The following memory map is assumed for the flat build:
   186	 *
   187	 *     .data region.  Size determined at link time.
   188	 *     .bss  region  Size determined at link time.
   189	 *     IDLE thread stack.  Size determined by CONFIG_IDLETHREAD_STACKSIZE.
   190	 *     Heap.  Extends to the end of SRAM.
   191	 *
   192	 *   The following memory map is assumed for the kernel build:
   193	 *
   194	 *     Kernel .data region.  Size determined at link time.
   195	 *     Kernel .bss  region  Size determined at link time.
   196	 *     Kernel IDLE thread stack.  Size determined by
   197	 *            CONFIG_IDLETHREAD_STACKSIZE.
   198	 *     Padding for alignment
   199	 *     User .data region.  Size determined at link time.
   200	 *     User .bss region  Size determined at link time.
   201	 *     Kernel heap.  Size determined by CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAPSIZE.
   202	 *     User heap.  Extends to the end of SRAM.
   203	 *
   204	 ****************************************************************************/
   205	
   206	void up_allocate_heap(void **heap_start, size_t *heap_size)
   207	{
   208	#if defined(CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED) && defined(CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP)
   209	  /* Get the unaligned size and position of the user-space heap.
   210	   * This heap begins after the user-space .bss section at an offset
   211	   * of CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAPSIZE (subject to alignment).
   212	   */
   213	
   214	  uintptr_t ubase = (uintptr_t)USERSPACE->us_bssend +
   215	                     CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAPSIZE;
   216	  size_t    usize = CONFIG_RAM_END - ubase;
   217	  int       log2;
   218	
   219	  DEBUGASSERT(ubase < (uintptr_t)CONFIG_RAM_END);
   220	
   221	  /* Adjust that size to account for MPU alignment requirements.
   222	   * NOTE that there is an implicit assumption that the CONFIG_RAM_END
   223	   * is aligned to the MPU requirement.
   224	   */
   225	
   226	  log2  = (int)mpu_log2regionfloor(usize);
   227	  DEBUGASSERT((CONFIG_RAM_END & ((1 << log2) - 1)) == 0);
   228	
   229	  usize = (1 << log2);
   230	  ubase = CONFIG_RAM_END - usize;
   231	
   232	  /* Return the user-space heap settings */
   233	
   234	  board_autoled_on(LED_HEAPALLOCATE);
   235	  *heap_start = (void *)ubase;
   236	  *heap_size  = usize;
   237	
   238	  /* Allow user-mode access to the user heap memory */
   239	
   240	  lpc17_40_mpu_uheap((uintptr_t)ubase, usize);
   241	
   242	#elif CONFIG_MM_REGIONS >= 3 && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAM) && \
   243	    defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMHEAP)
   244	  /* We are going to allocate a DRAM heap.  In the case where a bootloader
   245	   * is used (and has initialized SDRAM), it is possible that .data, .bss,
   246	   * and the IDLE stack reside in SDRAM.
   247	   */
   248	
   249	  uintptr_t sram_start = CONFIG_RAM_START;
   250	
   251	  /* Is SRAM the primary RAM? I.e., do .data, .bss, and the IDLE thread
   252	   * stack lie in SRAM?
   253	   */
   254	
   255	  if (g_idle_topstack >= CONFIG_RAM_START &&
   256	      g_idle_topstack < CONFIG_RAM_END)
   257	    {
   258	      /* Yes, then the SRAM heap starts in SRAM after the IDLE stack */
   259	
   260	      sram_start = g_idle_topstack;
   261	    }
   262	  else
   263	    {
   264	      /* Use the entire SRAM for heap */
   265	
   266	      sram_start = CONFIG_RAM_START;
   267	    }
   268	
   269	  board_autoled_on(LED_HEAPALLOCATE);
   270	  *heap_start = (void *)sram_start;
   271	  *heap_size  = CONFIG_RAM_END - sram_start;
   272	
   273	#else
   274	
   275	  /* Return the heap settings (assuming that .data, .bss, and the IDLE
   276	   * stack lie in SRAM).
   277	   */
   278	
   279	  board_autoled_on(LED_HEAPALLOCATE);
   280	  *heap_start = (void *)g_idle_topstack;
   281	  *heap_size  = CONFIG_RAM_END - g_idle_topstack;
   282	#endif
   283	}
   284	
   285	/****************************************************************************
   286	 * Name: up_allocate_kheap
   287	 *
   288	 * Description:
   289	 *   For the kernel build (CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED=y) with both kernel- and
   290	 *   user-space heaps (CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP=y), this function allocates
   291	 *   (and protects) the kernel-space heap.
   292	 *
   293	 *   REVISIT:  This does not account for the possibility that the kernel
   294	 *   heap might lie in SDRAM.
   295	 *
   296	 ****************************************************************************/
   297	
   298	#if defined(CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED) && defined(CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP)
   299	void up_allocate_kheap(void **heap_start, size_t *heap_size)
   300	{
   301	  /* Get the unaligned size and position of the user-space heap.
   302	   * This heap begins after the user-space .bss section at an offset
   303	   * of CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAPSIZE (subject to alignment).
   304	   */
   305	
   306	  uintptr_t ubase = (uintptr_t)USERSPACE->us_bssend +
   307	                    CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAPSIZE;
   308	  size_t    usize = CONFIG_RAM_END - ubase;
   309	  int       log2;
   310	
   311	  DEBUGASSERT(ubase < (uintptr_t)CONFIG_RAM_END);
   312	
   313	  /* Adjust that size to account for MPU alignment requirements.
   314	   * NOTE that there is an implicit assumption that the CONFIG_RAM_END
   315	   * is aligned to the MPU requirement.
   316	   */
   317	
   318	  log2  = (int)mpu_log2regionfloor(usize);
   319	  DEBUGASSERT((CONFIG_RAM_END & ((1 << log2) - 1)) == 0);
   320	
   321	  usize = (1 << log2);
   322	  ubase = CONFIG_RAM_END - usize;
   323	
   324	  /* Return the kernel heap settings (i.e., the part of the heap region
   325	   * that was not dedicated to the user heap).
   326	   */
   327	
   328	  *heap_start = (void *)USERSPACE->us_bssend;
   329	  *heap_size  = ubase - (uintptr_t)USERSPACE->us_bssend;
   330	}
   331	#endif
   332	
   333	/****************************************************************************
   334	 * Name: arm_addregion
   335	 *
   336	 * Description:
   337	 *   Memory may be added in non-contiguous chunks.  Additional chunks are
   338	 *   added by calling this function.
   339	 *
   340	 ****************************************************************************/
   341	
   342	#if CONFIG_MM_REGIONS > 1
   343	void arm_addregion(void)
   344	{
   345	  /* Banks 0 and 1 are each 16Kb.  If both are present, they occupy a
   346	   * contiguous 32Kb memory region.
   347	   *
   348	   * If Ethernet is enabled, it will take some or all of bank 0 for packet
   349	   * buffering and descriptor tables; If USB host is enabled, it will take
   350	   * some or all of bank 1 for descriptor memory.  The complex conditional
   351	   * compilation above should boil this all down to a very simple check
   352	   * here:
   353	   *
   354	   * Is any memory available in AHB SRAM for the heap?
   355	   */
   356	
   357	#ifdef LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPBASE
   358	#if defined(CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED) && defined(CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP)
   359	
   360	  /* Yes.. allow user-mode access to the AHB SRAM user heap memory */
   361	
   362	#if defined(LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPBASE) && defined(LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPSIZE)
   363	  lpc17_40_mpu_uheap((uintptr_t)LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPBASE,
   364	                     LPC17_40_BANK0_HEAPSIZE);
   365	#endif
   366	
   367	#if defined(LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPBASE) && defined(LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE)
   368	  lpc17_40_mpu_uheap((uintptr_t)LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPBASE,
   369	                      LPC17_40_BANK1_HEAPSIZE);
   370	#endif
   371	
   372	#endif /* CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED && CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP */
   373	
   374	  /* Add the AHB SRAM user heap region. */
   375	
   376	  kumm_addregion((void *)LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPBASE, LPC17_40_AHB_HEAPSIZE);
   377	#endif
   378	
   379	#if CONFIG_MM_REGIONS >= 3
   380	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAM) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMHEAP)
   381	    {
   382	      /* Memory may be reserved at the beginning of DRAM for other purposes
   383	       * (for example for video framebuffers).  Memory can similar be
   384	       * reserved at the end of DRAM using LPC17_40_EXTDRAMSIZE.  The amount
   385	       * to be added to the heap will be from DRAM_BASE +
   386	       * LPC17_40_EXTDRAMHEAP_OFFSET through DRAM_BASE + LPC17_40_EXTDRAMSIZE
   387	       * where (DRAM_BASE is the base address of CS0).
   388	       */
   389	
   390	      uintptr_t dram_end = LPC17_40_EXTDRAM_CS0 +
   391	                           CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMSIZE;
   392	      uintptr_t dram_start;
   393	      uintptr_t heap_size;
   394	
   395	      /* Is SDRAM the primary RAM? I.e., do .data, .bss, and the IDLE thread
   396	       * stack lie in SDRAM?
   397	       */
   398	
   399	      if (g_idle_topstack >= LPC17_40_EXTDRAM_CS0 &&
   400	          g_idle_topstack < dram_end)
   401	        {
   402	          /* Yes, then the SDRAM heap starts in SDRAM after the IDLE stack */
   403	
   404	          dram_start = g_idle_topstack;
   405	        }
   406	      else
   407	        {
   408	          /* Use the entire SDRAM for heap (possible reserving a portion at
   409	           * the beginning of DRAM).
   410	           */
   411	
   412	          dram_start = LPC17_40_EXTDRAM_CS0 +
   413	                       CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMHEAP_OFFSET;
   414	        }
   415	
   416	      heap_size = dram_end - dram_start;
   417	
   418	#if defined(CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED) && defined(CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP)
   419	      /* Allow user-mode access to the external DRAM heap memory.
   420	       *
   421	       * REVISIT:  In PROTECTED mode, it would be necessary to align
   422	       * dram_start to an address that is easily spanned by an MPU
   423	       * region if dram_start is not at the beginning of CS0.
   424	       */
   425	
   426	      lpc17_40_mpu_uheap(dram_start, heap_size);
   427	#endif
   428	
   429	      /* Add external DRAM heap memory to the user heap */
   430	
   431	      kumm_addregion((void *)dram_start, heap_size);
   432	    }
   433	#endif
   434	
   435	#if !defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTDRAMHEAP) || (CONFIG_MM_REGIONS >= 4)
   436	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTSRAM0) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTSRAM0HEAP)
   437	#if defined(CONFIG_BUILD_PROTECTED) && defined(CONFIG_MM_KERNEL_HEAP)
   438	  /* Allow user-mode access to external SRAM heap memory */
   439	
   440	  lpc17_40_mpu_uheap((uintptr_t)LPC17_40_EXTSRAM_CS0,
   441	                      CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTSRAM0SIZE);
   442	
   443	#endif
   444	  /* Add external SRAM heap memory to the user heap */
   445	
   446	  kumm_addregion((void *)LPC17_40_EXTSRAM_CS0,
   447	                  CONFIG_LPC17_40_EXTSRAM0SIZE);
   448	#endif
   449	#endif
   450	#endif /* CONFIG_MM_REGIONS >= 3 */
   451	}
   452	#endif

    48	  (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH + CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HPULSE + \
    49	   CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HFRONTPORCH + CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HBACKPORCH)
    50	#define LPC17_40_LCD_LINES_PER_FRAME \
    51	  (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT + CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VPULSE + \
    52	   CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VFRONTPORCH + CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VBACKPORCH)
    53	#define LPC17_40_LCD_PIXEL_CLOCK \
    54	  (LPC17_40_LCD_CLK_PER_LINE * LPC17_40_LCD_LINES_PER_FRAME * \
    55	   CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_REFRESH_FREQ)
    56	
    57	/* Framebuffer characteristics in bytes */
    58	
    59	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP1)
    60	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 1 + 7) / 8)
    61	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP2)
    62	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 2 + 7) / 8)
    63	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP4)
    64	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 4 + 7) / 8)
    65	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP8)
    66	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 8 + 7) / 8)
    67	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP16)
    68	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 16 + 7) / 8)
    69	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP24)
    70	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 32 + 7) / 8)
    71	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP16_565)
    72	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 16 + 7) / 8)
    73	#else /* defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP12_444) */
    74	#  define LPC17_40_STRIDE ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * 16 + 7) / 8)
    75	#endif
    76	
    77	#define LPC17_40_FBSIZE (LPC17_40_STRIDE * CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT)
    78	
    79	/* Delays */
    80	
    81	#define LPC17_40_LCD_PWRDIS_DELAY 10000
    82	#define LPC17_40_LCD_PWREN_DELAY  10000

    98	static int lpc17_40_getplaneinfo(struct fb_vtable_s *vtable, int planeno,
    99	             struct fb_planeinfo_s *pinfo);

   102	 * mapping

   107	             struct fb_cmap_s *cmap);

   109	             const struct fb_cmap_s *cmap);
   110	#endif
   111

   113	 * cursor

   133	  .yres     = CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT,
   134	  .nplanes  = 1,
   135	};

   142	  .fblen    = LPC17_40_FBSIZE,
   143	  .stride   = LPC17_40_STRIDE,
   144	  .display  = 0,
   145	  .bpp      = LPC17_40_BPP,
   146	};

   157	#endif
   158	#endif

   167	  .getplaneinfo  = lpc17_40_getplaneinfo,

   178	/****************************************************************************
   179	 * Public Data
   180	 ****************************************************************************/
   181	
   182	/****************************************************************************
   183	 * Private Functions
   184	 ****************************************************************************/
   185	
   186	/****************************************************************************
   187	 * Name: lpc17_40_getvideoinfo
   188	 ****************************************************************************/
   189	
   190	static int lpc17_40_getvideoinfo(struct fb_vtable_s *vtable,
   191	                                 struct fb_videoinfo_s *vinfo)
   192	{
   193	  lcdinfo("vtable=%p vinfo=%p\n", vtable, vinfo);
   194	  if (vtable && vinfo)
   195	    {
   196	      memcpy(vinfo, &g_videoinfo, sizeof(struct fb_videoinfo_s));
   197	      return OK;
   198	    }
   199	
   200	  lcderr("ERROR: Returning EINVAL\n");
   201	  return -EINVAL;
   202	}
   203	
   204	/****************************************************************************
   205	 * Name: lpc17_40_getplaneinfo
   206	 ****************************************************************************/
   207	
   208	static int lpc17_40_getplaneinfo(struct fb_vtable_s *vtable, int planeno,
   209	                                 struct fb_planeinfo_s *pinfo)
   210	{
   211	  lcdinfo("vtable=%p planeno=%d pinfo=%p\n", vtable, planeno, pinfo);
   212	  if (vtable && planeno == 0 && pinfo)
   213	    {
   214	      memcpy(pinfo, &g_planeinfo, sizeof(struct fb_planeinfo_s));
   215	      return OK;
   216	    }
   217	
   218	  lcderr("ERROR: Returning EINVAL\n");
   219	  return -EINVAL;
   220	}
   221	
   222	/****************************************************************************
   223	 * Name: lpc17_40_getcmap
   224	 ****************************************************************************/
   225	
   226	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_CMAP
   227	static int lpc17_40_getcmap(struct fb_vtable_s *vtable,
   228	                            struct fb_cmap_s *cmap)
   229	{
   230	  uint32_t *pal;
   231	  uint32_t rgb;
   232	  int last;
   233	  int i;
   234	
   235	  lcdinfo("vtable=%p cmap=%p first=%d len=%d\n",
   236	          vtable, cmap, cmap->first, cmap->len);
   237	
   238	  DEBUGASSERT(vtable && cmap &&
   239	              cmap->first < 256 && (cmap->first + cmap->len) < 256);
   240	
   241	  pal  = (uint32_t *)LPC17_40_LCD_PAL(cmap->first >> 1);
   242	  last = cmap->first + cmap->len;
   243	
   244	  /* Handle the case where the first color starts on an odd boundary */
   245	
   246	  i = cmap->first;
   247	  if ((i & 1) != 0)
   248	    {
   249	      rgb  = *pal++;
   250	      i++;
   251	
   252	      /* Save the odd palette value */
   253	
   254	      cmap->red[i]    = (rgb & LCD_PAL_R1_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_R1_SHIFT;
   255	      cmap->green[i]  = (rgb & LCD_PAL_G1_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_G1_SHIFT;
   256	      cmap->blue[i]   = (rgb & LCD_PAL_B1_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_B1_SHIFT;
   257	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_TRANSPARENCY
   258	      cmap->transp[i] = 0;
   259	#endif
   260	    }
   261	
   262	  /* Handle even colors */
   263	
   264	  for (; i < last; i += 2)
   265	    {
   266	      rgb  = *pal++;
   267	
   268	      /* Save the even palette value */
   269	
   270	      cmap->red[i]    = (rgb & LCD_PAL_R0_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_R0_SHIFT;
   271	      cmap->green[i]  = (rgb & LCD_PAL_G0_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_G0_SHIFT;
   272	      cmap->blue[i]   = (rgb & LCD_PAL_B0_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_B0_SHIFT;
   273	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_TRANSPARENCY
   274	      cmap->transp[i] = 0;
   275	#endif
   276	
   277	      /* Handle the case where the len ends on an odd boundary */
   278	
   279	      if ((i + 1) < last)
   280	        {
   281	          /* Save the even palette value */
   282	
   283	          cmap->red[i + 1]    = (rgb & LCD_PAL_R1_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_R1_SHIFT;
   284	          cmap->green[i + 1]  = (rgb & LCD_PAL_G1_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_G1_SHIFT;
   285	          cmap->blue[i + 1]   = (rgb & LCD_PAL_B1_MASK) >> LCD_PAL_B1_SHIFT;
   286	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_TRANSPARENCY
   287	          cmap->transp[i + 1] = 0;
   288	#endif
   289	        }
   290	    }
   291	
   292	  return OK;
   293	}
   294	#endif
   295	
   296	/****************************************************************************
   297	 * Name: lpc17_40_putcmap
   298	 ****************************************************************************/
   299	
   300	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_CMAP
   301	static int lpc17_40_putcmap(struct fb_vtable_s *vtable,
   302	                            const struct fb_cmap_s *cmap)
   303	{
   304	  uint32_t *pal;
   305	  uint32_t rgb0;
   306	  uint32_t rgb1;
   307	  int last;
   308	  int i;
   309	
   310	  lcdinfo("vtable=%p cmap=%p first=%d len=%d\n",
   311	          vtable, cmap, cmap->first, cmap->len);
   312	
   313	  DEBUGASSERT(vtable && cmap);
   314	
   315	  pal  = (uint32_t *)LPC17_40_LCD_PAL(cmap->first >> 1);
   316	  last = cmap->first + cmap->len;
   317	
   318	  /* Handle the case where the first color starts on an odd boundary */
   319	
   320	  i = cmap->first;
   321	  if ((i & 1) != 0)
   322	    {
   323	      rgb0  = *pal;
   324	      rgb0 &= (LCD_PAL_R0_MASK | LCD_PAL_G0_MASK | LCD_PAL_B0_MASK |
   325	               LCD_PAL_I0);
   326	      rgb1 |= ((uint32_t)cmap->red[i]   << LCD_PAL_R0_SHIFT |
   327	               (uint32_t)cmap->green[i] << LCD_PAL_G0_SHIFT |
   328	               (uint32_t)cmap->blue[i]  << LCD_PAL_B0_SHIFT);
   329	
   330	      /* Save the new palette value */
   331	
   332	      *pal++ = (rgb0 | rgb1);
   333	      i++;
   334	    }
   335	
   336	  /* Handle even colors */
   337	
   338	  for (; i < last; i += 2)
   339	    {
   340	      uint32_t rgb0 = ((uint32_t)cmap->red[i]   << LCD_PAL_R0_SHIFT |
   341	                       (uint32_t)cmap->green[i] << LCD_PAL_G0_SHIFT |
   342	                       (uint32_t)cmap->blue[i]  << LCD_PAL_B0_SHIFT);
   343	
   344	      /* Handle the case where the len ends on an odd boundary */
   345	
   346	      if ((i + 1) >= last)
   347	        {
   348	          rgb1  = *pal;
   349	          rgb1 &= (LCD_PAL_R1_MASK | LCD_PAL_G1_MASK | LCD_PAL_B1_MASK |
   350	                   LCD_PAL_I1);
   351	        }
   352	      else
   353	        {
   354	          rgb1  = ((uint32_t)cmap->red[i + 1]   << LCD_PAL_R1_SHIFT |
   355	                   (uint32_t)cmap->green[i + 1] << LCD_PAL_G1_SHIFT |
   356	                   (uint32_t)cmap->blue[i + 1]  << LCD_PAL_B1_SHIFT);
   357	        }
   358	
   359	      /* Save the new palette value */
   360	
   361	      *pal++ = (rgb0 | rgb1);
   362	    }
   363	
   364	  return OK;
   365	}
   366	#endif
   367	
   368	/****************************************************************************
   369	 * Name: lpc17_40_getcursor
   370	 ****************************************************************************/
   371	
   372	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_HWCURSOR
   373	static int lpc17_40_getcursor(struct fb_vtable_s *vtable,
   374	                              struct fb_cursorattrib_s *attrib)
   375	{
   376	  lcdinfo("vtable=%p attrib=%p\n", vtable, attrib);
   377	  if (vtable && attrib)
   378	    {
   379	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_HWCURSORIMAGE
   380	      attrib->fmt = LPC17_40_COLOR_FMT;
   381	#endif
   382	
   383	      lcdinfo("pos: (x=%d, y=%d)\n", g_cpos.x, g_cpos.y);
   384	      attrib->pos = g_cpos;
   385	
   386	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_HWCURSORSIZE
   387	      attrib->mxsize.h = CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT;
   388	      attrib->mxsize.w = CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH;
   389	
   390	      lcdinfo("size: (h=%d, w=%d)\n", g_csize.h, g_csize.w);
   391	      attrib->size = g_csize;
   392	#endif
   393	      return OK;
   394	    }
   395	
   396	  lcderr("ERROR: Returning EINVAL\n");
   397	  return -EINVAL;
   398	}
   399	#endif
   400	
   401	/****************************************************************************
   402	 * Name: lpc17_40_setcursor
   403	 ****************************************************************************/
   404	
   405	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_HWCURSOR
   406	static int lpc17_40_setcursor(struct fb_vtable_s *vtable,
   407	                              struct fb_setcursor_s *settings)
   408	{
   409	  lcdinfo("vtable=%p settings=%p\n", vtable, settings);
   410	  if (vtable && settings)
   411	    {
   412	      lcdinfo("flags: %02x\n", settings->flags);
   413	      if ((flags & FB_CUR_SETPOSITION) != 0)
   414	        {
   415	          g_cpos = settings->pos;
   416	          lcdinfo("pos: (h:%d, w:%d)\n", g_cpos.x, g_cpos.y);
   417	        }
   418	
   419	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_HWCURSORSIZE
   420	      if ((flags & FB_CUR_SETSIZE) != 0)
   421	        {
   422	          g_csize = settings->size;
   423	          lcdinfo("size: (h:%d, w:%d)\n", g_csize.h, g_csize.w);
   424	        }
   425	#endif
   426	
   427	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_HWCURSORIMAGE
   428	      if ((flags & FB_CUR_SETIMAGE) != 0)
   429	        {
   430	          lcdinfo("image: (h:%d, w:%d) @ %p\n",
   431	                  settings->img.height, settings->img.width,
   432	                  settings->img.image);
   433	        }
   434	#endif
   435	
   436	      return OK;
   437	    }
   438	
   439	  lcderr("ERROR: Returning EINVAL\n");
   440	  return -EINVAL;
   441	}
   442	#endif
   443	
   444	/****************************************************************************
   445	 * Public Functions
   446	 ****************************************************************************/
   447	
   448	/****************************************************************************
   449	 * Name: up_fbinitialize
   450	 *
   451	 * Description:
   452	 *   Initialize the framebuffer video hardware associated with the display.
   453	 *
   454	 * Input Parameters:
   455	 *   display - In the case of hardware with multiple displays, this
   456	 *     specifies the display.  Normally this is zero.
   457	 *
   458	 * Returned Value:
   459	 *   Zero is returned on success; a negated errno value is returned on any
   460	 *   failure.
   461	 *
   462	 ****************************************************************************/
   463	
   464	int up_fbinitialize(int display)
   465	{
   466	  uint32_t regval;
   467	  int i;
   468	
   469	  lcdinfo("Entry\n");
   470	
   471	  /* Give LCD bus priority */
   472	
   473	  regval = ((SYSCON_MATRIXARB_PRI_ICODE(SYSCON_MATRIXARB_PRI_LOW)) |
   474	            (SYSCON_MATRIXARB_PRI_DCODE(SYSCON_MATRIXARB_PRI_HIGHEST)) |
   475	            (SYSCON_MATRIXARB_PRI_LCD(SYSCON_MATRIXARB_PRI_HIGHEST)));
   476	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_MATRIXARB);
   477	
   478	  /* Configure pins */
   479	
   480	  /* Video data:
   481	   *
   482	   * REVISIT:  The conditional logic is not correct here.
   483	   * See arch/arm/src/lpc54xx/lpc454_lcd.c
   484	   */
   485	
   486	  lcdinfo("Configuring pins\n");
   487	
   488	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD0);
   489	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD1);
   490	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD2);
   491	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD3);
   492	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD4);
   493	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD5);
   494	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD6);
   495	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD7);
   496	
   497	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD8);
   498	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD9);
   499	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD10);
   500	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD11);
   501	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD12);
   502	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD13);
   503	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD14);
   504	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD15);
   505	
   506	#if LPC17_40_BPP > 16
   507	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD16);
   508	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD17);
   509	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD18);
   510	#endif
   511	#if LPC17_40_BPP > 16 || defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP16_565)
   512	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD19);
   513	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD20);
   514	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD21);
   515	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD22);
   516	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_VD23);
   517	#endif
   518	
   519	  /* Other pins */
   520	
   521	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_DCLK);
   522	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_LP);
   523	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_FP);
   524	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_ENABM);
   525	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_LCD_PWR);
   526	
   527	  /* Turn on LCD clock */
   528	
   529	  modifyreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP, 0, SYSCON_PCONP_PCLCD);
   530	
   531	  lcdinfo("Configuring the LCD controller\n");
   532	
   533	  /* Disable the cursor */
   534	
   535	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_LCD_CRSR_CRTL);
   536	  regval &= ~LCD_CRSR_CTRL_CRSON;
   537	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_CRSR_CRTL);
   538	
   539	  /* Clear any pending interrupts */
   540	
   541	  putreg32(LCD_INTCLR_ALL, LPC17_40_LCD_INTCLR);
   542	
   543	  /* Disable GLCD controller */
   544	
   545	  putreg32(0, LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   546	
   547	  /* Initialize pixel clock (assuming clock source is the peripheral clock) */
   548	
   549	  putreg32(((uint32_t)BOARD_PCLK_FREQUENCY /
   550	            (uint32_t)LPC17_40_LCD_PIXEL_CLOCK) + 1,
   551	           LPC17_40_SYSCON_LCDCFG);
   552	
   553	  /* Set the bits per pixel */
   554	
   555	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   556	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_MASK;
   557	
   558	#if defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP1)
   559	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_1;      /* 1 bpp */
   560	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP2)
   561	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_2;      /* 2 bpp */
   562	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP4)
   563	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_4;      /* 4 bpp */
   564	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP8)
   565	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_8;      /* 8 bpp */
   566	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP16)
   567	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_16;     /* 16 bpp */
   568	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP24)
   569	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_24;     /* 24-bit TFT panel only */
   570	#elif defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP16_565)
   571	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_565;    /* 16 bpp, 5:6:5 mode */
   572	#else /* defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BPP12_444) */
   573	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDBPP_444;    /* 12 bpp, 4:4:4 mode */
   574	#endif
   575	
   576	  /* TFT panel */
   577	
   578	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_TFTPANEL
   579	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDTFT;
   580	#endif
   581	
   582	  /* Swap red and blue.  The colors will be 0x00RRGGBB, not 0x00BBGGRR. */
   583	
   584	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_BGR;
   585	
   586	  /* Single panel */
   587	
   588	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_LCDDUAL;
   589	
   590	  /* Select monochrome or color LCD */
   591	
   592	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_MONOCHROME
   593	  /* Select monochrome LCD */
   594	
   595	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_BGR;
   596	
   597	  /* Select 4- or 8-bit monochrome interface */
   598	
   599	#  if LPC17_40_BPP > 4
   600	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDMONO8;
   601	#  else
   602	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_LCDMONO8;
   603	#  endif
   604	
   605	#else
   606	  /* Select color LCD */
   607	
   608	  regval &= ~(LCD_CTRL_LCDBW | LCD_CTRL_LCDMONO8);
   609	
   610	#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_MONOCHROME */
   611	
   612	  /* Little endian byte order */
   613	
   614	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_BEBO;
   615	
   616	  /* Little endian pixel order */
   617	
   618	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_BEPO;
   619	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   620	
   621	  /* Initialize horizontal timing */
   622	
   623	  putreg32(0, LPC17_40_LCD_TIMH);
   624	
   625	  regval = (((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH / 16) - 1) << LCD_TIMH_PPL_SHIFT |
   626	            (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HPULSE - 1)        << LCD_TIMH_HSW_SHIFT |
   627	            (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HFRONTPORCH - 1)   << LCD_TIMH_HFP_SHIFT |
   628	            (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HBACKPORCH - 1)    << LCD_TIMH_HBP_SHIFT);
   629	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_TIMH);
   630	
   631	  /* Initialize vertical timing */
   632	
   633	  putreg32(0, LPC17_40_LCD_TIMV);
   634	
   635	  regval = ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT - 1) << LCD_TIMV_LPP_SHIFT |
   636	            (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VPULSE - 1)  << LCD_TIMV_VSW_SHIFT |
   637	            (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VFRONTPORCH) << LCD_TIMV_VFP_SHIFT |
   638	            (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VBACKPORCH)  << LCD_TIMV_VBP_SHIFT);
   639	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_TIMV);
   640	
   641	  /* Initialize clock and signal polarity */
   642	
   643	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_LCD_POL);
   644	
   645	  /* LCDFP pin is active LOW and inactive HIGH */
   646	
   647	  regval |= LCD_POL_IVS;
   648	
   649	  /* LCDLP pin is active LOW and inactive HIGH */
   650	
   651	  regval |= LCD_POL_IHS;
   652	
   653	  /* Data is driven out into the LCD on the falling edge */
   654	
   655	  regval &= ~LCD_POL_IPC;
   656	
   657	  /* Set number of clocks per line */
   658	
   659	  regval |= ((CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH - 1) << LCD_POL_CPL_SHIFT);
   660	
   661	  /* Bypass internal pixel clock divider */
   662	
   663	  regval |= LCD_POL_BCD;
   664	
   665	  /* LCD_ENAB_M is active high */
   666	
   667	  regval &= ~LCD_POL_IOE;
   668	
   669	  /* Select CCLK for the LCD block clock source */
   670	
   671	  regval &= ~LCD_POL_CLKSEL;
   672	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_POL);
   673	
   674	  /* Frame base address doubleword aligned */
   675	
   676	  putreg32(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VRAMBASE & ~7, LPC17_40_LCD_UPBASE);
   677	  putreg32(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VRAMBASE & ~7, LPC17_40_LCD_LPBASE);
   678	
   679	  /* Clear the display */
   680	
   681	  lpc17_40_lcdclear(CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BACKCOLOR);
   682	
   683	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BACKLIGHT
   684	  /* Turn on the back light */
   685	
   686	  lpc17_40_backlight(true);
   687	#endif
   688	
   689	  putreg32(0, LPC17_40_LCD_INTMSK);
   690	  lcdinfo("Enabling the display\n");
   691	
   692	  for (i = LPC17_40_LCD_PWREN_DELAY; i; i--);
   693	
   694	  /* Enable LCD */
   695	
   696	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   697	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDEN;
   698	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   699	
   700	  /* Enable LCD power */
   701	
   702	  for (i = LPC17_40_LCD_PWREN_DELAY; i; i--);
   703	
   704	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   705	  regval |= LCD_CTRL_LCDPWR;
   706	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   707	
   708	  return OK;
   709	}
   710	
   711	/****************************************************************************
   712	 * Name: up_fbgetvplane
   713	 *
   714	 * Description:
   715	 *   Return a a reference to the framebuffer object for the specified video
   716	 *   plane of the specified plane.  Many OSDs support multiple planes of
   717	 *   video.
   718	 *
   719	 * Input Parameters:
   720	 *   display - In the case of hardware with multiple displays, this
   721	 *     specifies the display.  Normally this is zero.
   722	 *   vplane - Identifies the plane being queried.
   723	 *
   724	 * Returned Value:
   725	 *   A non-NULL pointer to the frame buffer access structure is returned on
   726	 *   success; NULL is returned on any failure.
   727	 *
   728	 ****************************************************************************/
   729	
   730	struct fb_vtable_s *up_fbgetvplane(int display, int vplane)
   731	{
   732	  lcdinfo("vplane: %d\n", vplane);
   733	  if (vplane == 0)
   734	    {
   735	      return &g_fbobject;
   736	    }
   737	  else
   738	    {
   739	      return NULL;
   740	    }
   741	}
   742	
   743	/****************************************************************************
   744	 * Name: up_fbuninitialize
   745	 *
   746	 * Description:
   747	 *   Uninitialize the framebuffer support for the specified display.
   748	 *
   749	 * Input Parameters:
   750	 *   display - In the case of hardware with multiple displays, this
   751	 *     specifies the display.  Normally this is zero.
   752	 *
   753	 * Returned Value:
   754	 *   None
   755	 *
   756	 ****************************************************************************/
   757	
   758	void up_fbuninitialize(int display)
   759	{
   760	  uint32_t regval;
   761	  int i;
   762	
   763	  /* We assume there is only one use of the LCD and so we do not need to
   764	   * worry about mutually exclusive access to the LCD hardware.
   765	   */
   766	
   767	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BACKLIGHT
   768	  /* Turn off the back light */
   769	
   770	  lpc17_40_backlight(false);
   771	#endif
   772	
   773	  /* Disable the LCD controller */
   774	
   775	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   776	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_LCDPWR;
   777	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   778	
   779	  for (i = LPC17_40_LCD_PWRDIS_DELAY; i; i--);
   780	
   781	  regval &= ~LCD_CTRL_LCDEN;
   782	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_LCD_CTRL);
   783	
   784	  /* Turn off clocking to the LCD. modifyreg32() can do this atomically. */
   785	
   786	  modifyreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP, SYSCON_PCONP_PCLCD, 0);
   787	}
   788	
   789	/****************************************************************************
   790	 * Name:  lpc17_40_lcdclear
   791	 *
   792	 * Description:
   793	 *   This is a non-standard LCD interface just for the LPC17xx/LPC40xx.
   794	 *   Clearing the display in the normal way by writing a sequences of runs
   795	 *   that covers the entire display can be slow.  Here the display is
   796	 *   cleared by simply setting all VRAM memory to the specified color.
   797	 *
   798	 ****************************************************************************/
   799	
   800	void lpc17_40_lcdclear(nxgl_mxpixel_t color)
   801	{
   802	  int i;
   803	#if LPC17_40_BPP > 16
   804	  uint32_t *dest = (uint32_t *)CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VRAMBASE;
   805	
   806	  lcdinfo("Clearing display: color=%08jx VRAM=%08x size=%d\n",
   807	          (uintmax_t)color, CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VRAMBASE,
   808	          CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT *
   809	          sizeof(uint32_t));
   810	
   811	#else
   812	  uint16_t *dest = (uint16_t *)CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VRAMBASE;
   813	
   814	  lcdinfo("Clearing display: color=%08x VRAM=%08x size=%d\n",
   815	          color, CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VRAMBASE,
   816	          CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT *
   817	          sizeof(uint16_t));
   818	#endif
   819	
   820	  for (i = 0; i < (CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_HWIDTH * CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_VHEIGHT);
   821	       i++)
   822	    {
   823	      *dest++ = color;
   824	    }
   825	}

--- FILE: arch/arm/src/lpc17xx_40xx/lpc17_40_i2c.c ---
     1	/****************************************************************************

    78	#ifndef GPIO_I2C1_SCL
    79	#  define GPIO_I2C1_SCL GPIO_I2C1_SCL_1
    80	#  define GPIO_I2C1_SDA GPIO_I2C1_SDA_1
    81	#endif
    82	
    83	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0_FREQUENCY
    84	#  define CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0_FREQUENCY 100000
    85	#endif
    86	
    87	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C1_FREQUENCY
    88	#  define CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C1_FREQUENCY 100000
    89	#endif
    90	
    91	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C2_FREQUENCY
    92	#  define CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C2_FREQUENCY 100000
    93	#endif
    94	
    95	#define LPC17_40_I2C1_FREQUENCY 400000

    97	/****************************************************************************
    98	 * Private Types
    99	 ****************************************************************************/
   100	
   101	struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s
   102	{
   103	  struct i2c_master_s dev;     /* Generic I2C device */
   104	  unsigned int     base;       /* Base address of registers */
   105	  uint16_t         irqid;      /* IRQ for this device */
   106	
   107	  mutex_t          lock;       /* Only one thread can access at a time */
   108	  sem_t            wait;       /* Place to wait for state machine completion */
   109	  volatile uint8_t state;      /* State of state machine */
   110	  struct wdog_s    timeout;    /* Watchdog to timeout when bus hung */
   111	  uint32_t         frequency;  /* Current I2C frequency */
   112	
   113	  struct i2c_msg_s *msgs;      /* remaining transfers - first one is in progress */
   114	  unsigned int     nmsg;       /* number of transfer remaining */
   115	
   116	  uint16_t         wrcnt;      /* number of bytes sent to tx fifo */
   117	  uint16_t         rdcnt;      /* number of bytes read from rx fifo */
   118	};
   119	
   120	/****************************************************************************
   121	 * Private Function Prototypes
   122	 ****************************************************************************/
   123	
   124	static int  lpc17_40_i2c_start(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv);
   125	static void lpc17_40_i2c_stop(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv);
   126	static int  lpc17_40_i2c_interrupt(int irq, void *context, void *arg);
   127	static void lpc17_40_i2c_timeout(wdparm_t arg);
   128	static void lpc17_40_i2c_setfrequency(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv,
   129	              uint32_t frequency);
   130	static void lpc17_40_stopnext(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv);
   131	
   132	/* I2C device operations */
   133	
   134	static int  lpc17_40_i2c_transfer(struct i2c_master_s *dev,
   135	                                  struct i2c_msg_s *msgs, int count);
   136	#ifdef CONFIG_I2C_RESET
   137	static int  lpc17_40_i2c_reset(struct i2c_master_s *dev);
   138	#endif

   146	{
   147	  .lock = NXMUTEX_INITIALIZER,
   148	  .wait = SEM_INITIALIZER(0),
   149	};
   150	#endif
   151	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C1
   152	static struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s g_i2c1dev =
   153	{
   154	  .lock = NXMUTEX_INITIALIZER,
   155	  .wait = SEM_INITIALIZER(0),
   156	};
   157	#endif
   158	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C2
   159	static struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s g_i2c2dev =
   160	{
   161	  .lock = NXMUTEX_INITIALIZER,
   162	  .wait = SEM_INITIALIZER(0),
   163	};
   164	#endif

   166	struct i2c_ops_s lpc17_40_i2c_ops =
   167	{
   168	  .transfer = lpc17_40_i2c_transfer
   169	#ifdef CONFIG_I2C_RESET
   170	  , .reset  = lpc17_40_i2c_reset
   171	#endif
