   174	/****************************************************************************
   175	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2c_setfrequency
   176	 *
   177	 * Description:
   178	 *   Set the frequency for the next transfer
   179	 *
   180	 ****************************************************************************/
   181	
   182	static void lpc17_40_i2c_setfrequency(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv,
   183	                                   uint32_t frequency)
   184	{
   185	  if (frequency != priv->frequency)
   186	    {
   187	      if (frequency > 100000)
   188	        {
   189	          /* Asymmetric per 400Khz I2C spec */
   190	
   191	          putreg32(LPC17_40_CCLK / (83 + 47) * 47 / frequency,
   192	                   priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_SCLH_OFFSET);
   193	          putreg32(LPC17_40_CCLK / (83 + 47) * 83 / frequency,
   194	                   priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_SCLL_OFFSET);
   195	        }
   196	      else
   197	        {
   198	          /* 50/50 mark space ratio */
   199	
   200	          putreg32(LPC17_40_CCLK / 100 * 50 / frequency,
   201	                   priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_SCLH_OFFSET);
   202	          putreg32(LPC17_40_CCLK / 100 * 50 / frequency,
   203	                   priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_SCLL_OFFSET);
   204	        }
   205	
   206	      priv->frequency = frequency;
   207	    }
   208	}
   209	
   210	/****************************************************************************
   211	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2c_start
   212	 *
   213	 * Description:
   214	 *   Perform a I2C transfer start
   215	 *
   216	 ****************************************************************************/
   217	
   218	static int lpc17_40_i2c_start(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv)
   219	{
   220	  uint32_t total_len = 0;
   221	  uint32_t freq = 1000000;
   222	  uint32_t timeout;
   223	  int i;
   224	
   225	  nxsem_reset(&priv->wait, 0);
   226	
   227	  putreg32(I2C_CONCLR_STAC | I2C_CONCLR_SIC,
   228	           priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONCLR_OFFSET);
   229	  putreg32(I2C_CONSET_STA, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONSET_OFFSET);
   230	
   231	  /* Get the total transaction length and the minimum frequency */
   232	
   233	  for (i = 0; i < priv->nmsg; i++)
   234	    {
   235	      total_len += priv->msgs[i].length;
   236	      if (priv->msgs[i].frequency < freq)
   237	        {
   238	          freq = priv->msgs[i].frequency;
   239	        }
   240	    }
   241	
   242	  /* Calculate the approximate timeout */
   243	
   244	  timeout = ((total_len * (9000000 / CONFIG_USEC_PER_TICK)) / freq) + 1;
   245	
   246	  /* Initializes the I2C state machine to a known value */
   247	
   248	  priv->state = 0x00;
   249	
   250	  wd_start(&priv->timeout, timeout,
   251	           lpc17_40_i2c_timeout, (wdparm_t)priv);
   252	  nxsem_wait(&priv->wait);
   253	
   254	  /* Remaining messages should be zero or an error occurred */
   255	
   256	  return priv->nmsg ? -ENXIO : OK;
   257	}
   258	
   259	/****************************************************************************
   260	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2c_stop
   261	 *
   262	 * Description:
   263	 *   Perform a I2C transfer stop
   264	 *
   265	 ****************************************************************************/
   266	
   267	static void lpc17_40_i2c_stop(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv)
   268	{
   269	  if (priv->state != 0x38)
   270	    {
   271	      putreg32(I2C_CONSET_STO | I2C_CONSET_AA,
   272	               priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONSET_OFFSET);
   273	    }
   274	
   275	  wd_cancel(&priv->timeout);
   276	  nxsem_post(&priv->wait);
   277	}
   278	
   279	/****************************************************************************
   280	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2c_timeout
   281	 *
   282	 * Description:
   283	 *   Watchdog timer for timeout of I2C operation
   284	 *
   285	 ****************************************************************************/
   286	
   287	static void lpc17_40_i2c_timeout(wdparm_t arg)
   288	{
   289	  struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *)arg;
   290	
   291	  irqstate_t flags = enter_critical_section();
   292	  priv->state = 0xff;
   293	  nxsem_post(&priv->wait);
   294	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   295	}
   296	
   297	/****************************************************************************
   298	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2c_transfer
   299	 *
   300	 * Description:
   301	 *   Perform a sequence of I2C transfers
   302	 *
   303	 ****************************************************************************/
   304	
   305	static int lpc17_40_i2c_transfer(struct i2c_master_s *dev,
   306	                                 struct i2c_msg_s *msgs, int count)
   307	{
   308	  struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *)dev;
   309	  int ret;
   310	
   311	  DEBUGASSERT(dev != NULL && msgs != NULL && count > 0);
   312	
   313	  /* Get exclusive access to the I2C bus */
   314	
   315	  nxmutex_lock(&priv->lock);
   316	
   317	  /* Set up for the transfer */
   318	
   319	  priv->wrcnt = 0;
   320	  priv->rdcnt = 0;
   321	  priv->msgs  = msgs;
   322	  priv->nmsg  = count;
   323	
   324	  /* Configure the I2C frequency.
   325	   * REVISIT: Note that the frequency is set only on the first message.
   326	   * This could be extended to support different transfer frequencies for
   327	   * each message segment.
   328	   */
   329	
   330	  lpc17_40_i2c_setfrequency(priv, msgs->frequency);
   331	
   332	  /* Perform the transfer */
   333	
   334	  ret = lpc17_40_i2c_start(priv);
   335	
   336	  nxmutex_unlock(&priv->lock);
   337	  return ret;
   338	}
   339	
   340	/****************************************************************************
   341	 * Name: lpc17_40_stopnext
   342	 *
   343	 * Description:
   344	 *   Check if we need to issue STOP at the next message
   345	 *
   346	 ****************************************************************************/
   347	
   348	static void lpc17_40_stopnext(struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv)
   349	{
   350	  priv->nmsg--;
   351	
   352	  if (priv->nmsg > 0)
   353	    {
   354	      priv->msgs++;
   355	
   356	      /* Check if a restart condition should be issued */
   357	
   358	      if (priv->msgs->flags & I2C_M_NOSTART)
   359	        {
   360	          priv->wrcnt = 0;
   361	
   362	          /* Starts transmitting the data buffer of the next message without
   363	           * issuing a restart.
   364	           */
   365	
   366	          putreg32(priv->msgs->buffer[priv->wrcnt],
   367	                   priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_DAT_OFFSET);
   368	        }
   369	      else
   370	        {
   371	          putreg32(I2C_CONSET_STA, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONSET_OFFSET);
   372	        }
   373	    }
   374	  else
   375	    {
   376	      lpc17_40_i2c_stop(priv);
   377	    }
   378	}
   379	
   380	/****************************************************************************
   381	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2c_interrupt
   382	 *
   383	 * Description:
   384	 *   The I2C Interrupt Handler
   385	 *
   386	 ****************************************************************************/
   387	
   388	static int lpc17_40_i2c_interrupt(int irq, void *context, void *arg)
   389	{
   390	  struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *)arg;
   391	  struct i2c_msg_s *msg;
   392	  uint32_t state;
   393	
   394	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL);
   395	
   396	  /* Reference UM10360 19.10.5 */
   397	
   398	  state = getreg32(priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_STAT_OFFSET);
   399	  msg  = priv->msgs;
   400	
   401	  /* Checks if a timeout occurred */
   402	
   403	  if (priv->state == 0xff)
   404	    {
   405	      state = 0xff;
   406	    }
   407	  else
   408	    {
   409	      priv->state = state;
   410	      state &= 0xf8;  /* state mask, only 0xX8 is possible */
   411	    }
   412	
   413	  switch (state)
   414	    {
   415	    case 0x08:     /* A START condition has been transmitted. */
   416	    case 0x10:     /* A Repeated START condition has been transmitted. */
   417	
   418	      /* Set address */
   419	
   420	      putreg32(((I2C_M_READ & msg->flags) == I2C_M_READ) ?
   421	        I2C_READADDR8(msg->addr) :
   422	        I2C_WRITEADDR8(msg->addr), priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_DAT_OFFSET);
   423	
   424	      /* Clear start bit */
   425	
   426	      putreg32(I2C_CONCLR_STAC, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONCLR_OFFSET);
   427	      break;
   428	
   429	    /* Write cases */
   430	
   431	    case 0x18: /* SLA+W has been transmitted; ACK has been received  */
   432	      priv->wrcnt = 0;
   433	      putreg32(msg->buffer[0], priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_DAT_OFFSET); /* put first byte */
   434	      break;
   435	
   436	    case 0x28: /* Data byte in DAT has been transmitted; ACK has been received. */
   437	      priv->wrcnt++;
   438	
   439	      if (priv->wrcnt < msg->length)
   440	        {
   441	          putreg32(msg->buffer[priv->wrcnt], priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_DAT_OFFSET); /* Put next byte */
   442	        }
   443	      else
   444	        {
   445	          lpc17_40_stopnext(priv);
   446	        }
   447	      break;
   448	
   449	    /* Read cases */
   450	
   451	    case 0x40:  /* SLA+R has been transmitted; ACK has been received */
   452	      priv->rdcnt = 0;
   453	      if (msg->length > 1)
   454	        {
   455	          putreg32(I2C_CONSET_AA, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONSET_OFFSET); /* Set ACK next read */
   456	        }
   457	      else
   458	        {
   459	          putreg32(I2C_CONCLR_AAC, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONCLR_OFFSET);  /* Do not ACK because only one byte */
   460	        }
   461	      break;
   462	
   463	    case 0x50:  /* Data byte has been received; ACK has been returned. */
   464	      priv->rdcnt++;
   465	      msg->buffer[priv->rdcnt - 1] =
   466	        getreg32(priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_BUFR_OFFSET);
   467	
   468	      if (priv->rdcnt >= (msg->length - 1))
   469	        {
   470	          putreg32(I2C_CONCLR_AAC, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONCLR_OFFSET);  /* Do not ACK any more */
   471	        }
   472	      break;
   473	
   474	    case 0x58:  /* Data byte has been received; NACK has been returned. */
   475	      msg->buffer[priv->rdcnt] =
   476	        getreg32(priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_BUFR_OFFSET);
   477	      lpc17_40_stopnext(priv);
   478	      break;
   479	
   480	    default:
   481	      lpc17_40_i2c_stop(priv);
   482	      break;
   483	    }
   484	
   485	  putreg32(I2C_CONCLR_SIC, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONCLR_OFFSET); /* clear interrupt */
   486	
   487	  return OK;
   488	}
   489	
   490	/****************************************************************************
   491	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2c_reset
   492	 *
   493	 * Description:
   494	 *   Perform an I2C bus reset in an attempt to break loose stuck I2C devices.
   495	 *
   496	 * Input Parameters:
   497	 *   dev   - Device-specific state data
   498	 *
   499	 * Returned Value:
   500	 *   Zero (OK) on success; a negated errno value on failure.
   501	 *
   502	 ****************************************************************************/
   503	
   504	#ifdef CONFIG_I2C_RESET
   505	static int lpc17_40_i2c_reset(struct i2c_master_s *dev)
   506	{
   507	  return OK;
   508	}
   509	#endif /* CONFIG_I2C_RESET */
   510	
   511	/****************************************************************************
   512	 * Public Functions
   513	 ****************************************************************************/
   514	
   515	/****************************************************************************
   516	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2cbus_initialize
   517	 *
   518	 * Description:
   519	 *   Initialise an I2C device
   520	 *
   521	 ****************************************************************************/
   522	
   523	struct i2c_master_s *lpc17_40_i2cbus_initialize(int port)
   524	{
   525	  struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv;
   526	
   527	  irqstate_t flags;
   528	  uint32_t regval;
   529	
   530	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   531	
   532	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0
   533	  if (port == 0)
   534	    {
   535	      priv        = &g_i2c0dev;
   536	      priv->base  = LPC17_40_I2C0_BASE;
   537	      priv->irqid = LPC17_40_IRQ_I2C0;
   538	
   539	      /* Enable clocking */
   540	
   541	      regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   542	      regval |= SYSCON_PCONP_PCI2C0;
   543	      putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   544	
   545	#if defined(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCLKSEL0)
   546	      regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCLKSEL0);
   547	      regval &= ~SYSCON_PCLKSEL0_I2C0_MASK;
   548	      regval |= (SYSCON_PCLKSEL_CCLK << SYSCON_PCLKSEL0_I2C0_SHIFT);
   549	      putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCLKSEL0);
   550	#endif
   551	
   552	      /* Pin configuration */
   553	
   554	      lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_I2C0_SCL);
   555	      lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_I2C0_SDA);
   556	
   557	      /* Set default frequency */
   558	
   559	      lpc17_40_i2c_setfrequency(priv, CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0_FREQUENCY);
   560	    }
   561	  else
   562	#endif
   563	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C1
   564	  if (port == 1)
   565	    {
   566	      priv        = &g_i2c1dev;
   567	      priv->base  = LPC17_40_I2C1_BASE;
   568	      priv->irqid = LPC17_40_IRQ_I2C1;
   569	
   570	      /* Enable clocking */
   571	
   572	      regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   573	      regval |= SYSCON_PCONP_PCI2C1;
   574	      putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   575	
   576	      regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCLKSEL1);
   577	      regval &= ~SYSCON_PCLKSEL1_I2C1_MASK;
   578	      regval |= (SYSCON_PCLKSEL_CCLK << SYSCON_PCLKSEL1_I2C1_SHIFT);
   579	      putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCLKSEL1);
   580	
   581	      /* Pin configuration */
   582	
   583	      lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_I2C1_SCL);
   584	      lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_I2C1_SDA);
   585	
   586	      /* Set default frequency */
   587	
   588	      lpc17_40_i2c_setfrequency(priv, CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C1_FREQUENCY);
   589	    }
   590	  else
   591	#endif
   592	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C2
   593	  if (port == 2)
   594	    {
   595	      priv        = &g_i2c2dev;
   596	      priv->base  = LPC17_40_I2C2_BASE;
   597	      priv->irqid = LPC17_40_IRQ_I2C2;
   598	
   599	      /* Enable clocking */
   600	
   601	      regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   602	      regval |= SYSCON_PCONP_PCI2C2;
   603	      putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   604	
   605	      regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCLKSEL1);
   606	      regval &= ~SYSCON_PCLKSEL1_I2C2_MASK;
   607	      regval |= (SYSCON_PCLKSEL_CCLK << SYSCON_PCLKSEL1_I2C2_SHIFT);
   608	      putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCLKSEL1);
   609	
   610	      /* Pin configuration */
   611	
   612	      lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_I2C2_SCL);
   613	      lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_I2C2_SDA);
   614	
   615	      /* Set default frequency */
   616	
   617	      lpc17_40_i2c_setfrequency(priv, CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C2_FREQUENCY);
   618	    }
   619	  else
   620	#endif
   621	    {
   622	      i2cerr("ERROR: LPC I2C Only supports ports 0, 1 and 2\n");
   623	      leave_critical_section(flags);
   624	      return NULL;
   625	    }
   626	
   627	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   628	
   629	  putreg32(I2C_CONSET_I2EN, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONSET_OFFSET);
   630	
   631	  /* Attach Interrupt Handler */
   632	
   633	  irq_attach(priv->irqid, lpc17_40_i2c_interrupt, priv);
   634	
   635	  /* Enable Interrupt Handler */
   636	
   637	  up_enable_irq(priv->irqid);
   638	
   639	  /* Install our operations */
   640	
   641	  priv->dev.ops = &lpc17_40_i2c_ops;
   642	  return &priv->dev;
   643	}
   644	
   645	/****************************************************************************
   646	 * Name: lpc17_40_i2cbus_uninitialize
   647	 *
   648	 * Description:
   649	 *   Uninitialise an I2C device
   650	 *
   651	 ****************************************************************************/
   652	
   653	int lpc17_40_i2cbus_uninitialize(struct i2c_master_s *dev)
   654	{
   655	  struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_i2cdev_s *)dev;
   656	
   657	  /* Disable I2C */
   658	
   659	  putreg32(I2C_CONCLRT_I2ENC, priv->base + LPC17_40_I2C_CONCLR_OFFSET);
   660	
   661	  /* Cancel the watchdog timer */
   662	
   663	  wd_cancel(&priv->timeout);
   664	
   665	  /* Disable interrupts */
   666	
   667	  up_disable_irq(priv->irqid);
   668	
   669	  /* Detach Interrupt Handler */
   670	
   671	  irq_detach(priv->irqid);
   672	  return OK;
   673	}
   674	
   675	#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0 || CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C1 || CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C2 */

    59	/****************************************************************************
    60	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    61	 ****************************************************************************/
    62	
    63	/* Configuration ************************************************************/
    64	
    65	/* Required system configuration options:
    66	 *
    67	 *   CONFIG_ARCH_DMA - Enable architecture-specific DMA subsystem
    68	 *     initialization.  Required if CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA is enabled.
    69	 *   CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPDMA - Enable LPC17XX_40XX GPDMA support.  Required if
    70	 *     CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA is enabled
    71	 *   CONFIG_SCHED_WORKQUEUE -- Callback support requires work queue support.
    72	 *
    73	 * Driver-specific configuration options:
    74	 *
    75	 *   CONFIG_SDIO_MUXBUS - Setting this configuration enables some locking
    76	 *     APIs to manage concurrent accesses on the SD card bus.  This is not
    77	 *     needed for the simple case of a single SD card, for example.
    78	 *   CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA - Enable SD card DMA.  This is a marginally
    79	 *   optional.
    80	 *     For most usages, SD accesses will cause data overruns if used without
    81	 *     DMA. NOTE the above system DMA configuration options.
    82	 *   CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_WIDTH_D1_ONLY - This may be selected to force the
    83	 *     driver operate with only a single data line (the default is to use
    84	 *     all 4 SD data lines).
    85	 *   CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_* - Enables some very low-level debug output
    86	 *     This also requires CONFIG_DEBUG_FS and CONFIG_DEBUG_INFO
    87	 */
    88	
    89	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
    90	#  warning "Large Non-DMA transfer may result in RX overrun failures"
    91	#else
    92	#  ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPDMA
    93	#    error "CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA support requires CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPDMA"
    94	#  endif
    95	#  ifndef CONFIG_SDIO_DMA
    96	#    error CONFIG_SDIO_DMA must be defined with CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
    97	#  endif
    98	#endif
    99	
   100	#ifndef CONFIG_SCHED_WORKQUEUE
   101	#  error "Callback support requires CONFIG_SCHED_WORKQUEUE"
   102	#endif
   103	
   104	/* Friendly CLKCR bit re-definitions ****************************************/
   105	
   106	/* Mode dependent settings.  These depend on clock devisor settings that must
   107	 * be defined in the board-specific board.h header file: SDCARD_INIT_CLKDIV,
   108	 * SDCARD_MMCXFR_CLKDIV, and SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV.
   109	 */
   110	
   111	#define LPC17_40_CLCKCR_INIT         (SDCARD_INIT_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D1)
   112	#define SDCARD_CLOCK_MMCXFR       (SDCARD_MMCXFR_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D1)
   113	#define SDCARD_CLOCK_SDXFR        (SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D1)
   114	#define SDCARD_CLOCK_SDWIDEXFR    (SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D4)
   115	
   116	/* Timing */
   117	
   118	#define SDCARD_CMDTIMEOUT         (100000)
   119	#define SDCARD_LONGTIMEOUT        (0x7fffffff)
   120	
   121	/* Big DTIMER setting */
   122	
   123	#define SDCARD_DTIMER_DATATIMEOUT (0x000fffff)
   124	
   125	/* DMA channel/stream configuration register settings.  The following
   126	 * must be selected.  The DMA driver will select the remaining fields.
   127	 *
   128	 * - 32-bit DMA
   129	 * - Memory increment
   130	 * - Direction (memory-to-peripheral, peripheral-to-memory)
   131	 * - Memory burst size (F4 only)
   132	 */
   133	
   134	/* DMA control register settings.  All CONTROL register fields need to be
   135	 * specified except for the transfer size which is passed as a separate
   136	 * parameter and for the terminal count interrupt enable bit which is
   137	 * controlled by the driver.
   138	 */
   139	
   140	#define SDCARD_RXDMA32_CONTROL    (DMACH_CONTROL_SBSIZE_8 | DMACH_CONTROL_DBSIZE_8 | \
   141	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_SWIDTH_32BIT | DMACH_CONTROL_DWIDTH_32BIT | \
   142	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_DI)
   143	#define SDCARD_TXDMA32_CONTROL    (DMACH_CONTROL_SBSIZE_8 | DMACH_CONTROL_DBSIZE_8 | \
   144	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_SWIDTH_32BIT | DMACH_CONTROL_DWIDTH_32BIT | \
   145	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_SI)
   146	
   147	/* DMA configuration register settings.  Only the SRCPER, DSTPER, and
   148	 * XFRTTYPE fields of the CONFIG register need be specified.
   149	 */
   150	
   151	#define SDCARD_RXDMA32_CONFIG     (DMACH_CONFIG_SRCPER_SDCARD | DMACH_CONFIG_XFRTYPE_P2M_SC)
   152	#define SDCARD_TXDMA32_CONFIG     (DMACH_CONFIG_DSTPER_SDCARD | DMACH_CONFIG_XFRTYPE_M2P_DC)
   153	
   154	/* FIFO sizes */
   155	
   156	#define SDCARD_HALFFIFO_WORDS     (8)
   157	#define SDCARD_HALFFIFO_BYTES     (8*4)
   158	
   159	/* Data transfer interrupt mask bits */
   160	
   161	#define SDCARD_RECV_MASK     (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   162	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_RXOVERRIE | \
   163	                              SDCARD_MASK0_RXFIFOHFIE | SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   164	#define SDCARD_SEND_MASK     (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   165	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_TXUNDERRIE | \
   166	                              SDCARD_MASK0_TXFIFOHEIE | SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   167	#define SDCARD_DMARECV_MASK  (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   168	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_RXOVERRIE | \
   169	                              SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   170	#define SDCARD_DMASEND_MASK  (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   171	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_TXUNDERRIE | \
   172	                              SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   173	
   174	/* Event waiting interrupt mask bits */
   175	
   176	#define SDCARD_CMDDONE_STA   (SDCARD_STATUS_CMDSENT)
   177	#define SDCARD_RESPDONE_STA  (SDCARD_STATUS_CTIMEOUT | SDCARD_STATUS_CCRCFAIL | \
   178	                              SDCARD_STATUS_CMDREND)
   179	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_STA   (0)
   180	
   181	#define SDCARD_CMDDONE_MASK  (SDCARD_MASK0_CMDSENTIE)
   182	#define SDCARD_RESPDONE_MASK (SDCARD_MASK0_CCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_CTIMEOUTIE | \
   183	                              SDCARD_MASK0_CMDRENDIE)
   184	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_MASK  (0)
   185	
   186	#define SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR   (SDCARD_CLEAR_CMDSENTC)
   187	#define SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR  (SDCARD_CLEAR_CTIMEOUTC | SDCARD_CLEAR_CCRCFAILC | \
   188	                              SDCARD_CLEAR_CMDRENDC)
   189	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_ICR   (SDCARD_CLEAR_DATAENDC | SDCARD_CLEAR_DCRCFAILC | \
   190	                              SDCARD_CLEAR_DTIMEOUTC | SDCARD_CLEAR_RXOVERRC | \
   191	                              SDCARD_CLEAR_TXUNDERRC | SDCARD_CLEAR_STBITERRC)
   192	
   193	#define SDCARD_WAITALL_ICR   (SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR | SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | \
   194	                              SDCARD_XFRDONE_ICR)
   195	
   196	/* Let's wait until we have both SD card transfer complete and DMA
   197	 * complete.
   198	 */
   199	
   200	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_FLAG  (1)
   201	#define SDCARD_DMADONE_FLAG  (2)
   202	#define SDCARD_ALLDONE       (3)
   203	
   204	/* Register logging support */
   205	
   206	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   207	#  ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   208	#    define SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP  0
   209	#    define SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_ENABLE 1
   210	#    define SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP   2
   211	#    define SAMPLENDX_END_TRANSFER  3
   212	#    define SAMPLENDX_DMA_CALLBACK  4
   213	#    define DEBUG_NSAMPLES          5
   214	#  else
   215	#    define SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP  0
   216	#    define SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP   1
   217	#    define SAMPLENDX_END_TRANSFER  2
   218	#    define DEBUG_NSAMPLES          3
   219	#  endif
   220	#endif

   222	/****************************************************************************
   223	 * Private Types
   224	 ****************************************************************************/
   225	
   226	/* This structure defines the state of the LPC17XX_40XX SD card interface */
   227	
   228	struct lpc17_40_dev_s
   229	{
   230	  struct sdio_dev_s  dev;        /* Standard, base SD card interface */
   231	
   232	  /* LPC17XX_40XX-specific extensions */
   233	
   234	  /* Event support */
   235	
   236	  sem_t              waitsem;         /* Implements event waiting */
   237	  sdio_eventset_t    waitevents;      /* Set of events to be waited for */
   238	  uint32_t           waitmask;        /* Interrupt enables for event waiting */
   239	  volatile sdio_eventset_t wkupevent; /* The event that caused the wakeup */
   240	  struct wdog_s      waitwdog;        /* Watchdog that handles event timeouts */
   241	
   242	  /* Callback support */
   243	
   244	  sdio_statset_t     cdstatus;   /* Card status */
   245	  sdio_eventset_t    cbevents;   /* Set of events to be cause callbacks */
   246	  worker_t           callback;   /* Registered callback function */
   247	  void              *cbarg;      /* Registered callback argument */
   248	  struct work_s      cbwork;     /* Callback work queue structure */
   249	
   250	  /* Interrupt mode data transfer support */
   251	
   252	  uint32_t          *buffer;     /* Address of current R/W buffer */
   253	  size_t             remaining;  /* Number of bytes remaining in the transfer */
   254	  uint32_t           xfrmask;    /* Interrupt enables for data transfer */
   255	
   256	  /* DMA data transfer support */
   257	
   258	  bool               widebus;    /* Required for DMA support */
   259	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   260	  volatile uint8_t   xfrflags;   /* Used to synchronize SD card and DMA completion events */
   261	  bool               dmamode;    /* true: DMA mode transfer */
   262	  DMA_HANDLE         dma;        /* Handle for DMA channel */
   263	#endif
   264	};
   265	
   266	/* Register logging support */
   267	
   268	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   269	struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s
   270	{
   271	  uint8_t  pwr;
   272	  uint16_t clkcr;
   273	  uint16_t dctrl;
   274	  uint32_t dtimer;
   275	  uint32_t dlen;
   276	  uint32_t dcount;
   277	  uint32_t sta;
   278	  uint32_t mask;
   279	  uint32_t fifocnt;
   280	};
   281	
   282	struct lpc17_40_sampleregs_s
   283	{
   284	  struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s sdcard;
   285	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_DMA) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA)
   286	  struct lpc17_40_dmaregs_s  dma;
   287	#endif
   288	};
   289	#endif

   406	/****************************************************************************
   407	 * Private Data
   408	 ****************************************************************************/
   409	
   410	struct lpc17_40_dev_s g_scard_dev =
   411	{
   412	  .dev =
   413	  {
   414	#ifdef CONFIG_SDIO_MUXBUS
   415	    .lock             = lpc17_40_lock,
   416	#endif
   417	    .reset            = lpc17_40_reset,
   418	    .capabilities     = lpc17_40_capabilities,
   419	    .status           = lpc17_40_status,
   420	    .widebus          = lpc17_40_widebus,
   421	    .clock            = lpc17_40_clock,
   422	    .attach           = lpc17_40_attach,
   423	    .sendcmd          = lpc17_40_sendcmd,
   424	    .recvsetup        = lpc17_40_recvsetup,
   425	    .sendsetup        = lpc17_40_sendsetup,
   426	    .cancel           = lpc17_40_cancel,
   427	    .waitresponse     = lpc17_40_waitresponse,
   428	    .recv_r1          = lpc17_40_recvshortcrc,
   429	    .recv_r2          = lpc17_40_recvlong,
   430	    .recv_r3          = lpc17_40_recvshort,
   431	    .recv_r4          = lpc17_40_recvnotimpl,
   432	    .recv_r5          = lpc17_40_recvnotimpl,
   433	    .recv_r6          = lpc17_40_recvshortcrc,
   434	    .recv_r7          = lpc17_40_recvshort,
   435	    .waitenable       = lpc17_40_waitenable,
   436	    .eventwait        = lpc17_40_eventwait,
   437	    .callbackenable   = lpc17_40_callbackenable,
   438	    .registercallback = lpc17_40_registercallback,
   439	#ifdef CONFIG_SDIO_DMA
   440	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   441	    .dmarecvsetup     = lpc17_40_dmarecvsetup,
   442	    .dmasendsetup     = lpc17_40_dmasendsetup,
   443	#else
   444	    .dmarecvsetup     = lpc17_40_recvsetup,
   445	    .dmasendsetup     = lpc17_40_sendsetup,
   446	#endif
   447	#endif
   448	  },
   449	  .waitsem = SEM_INITIALIZER(0),
   450	};

   458	/****************************************************************************
   459	 * Private Functions
   460	 ****************************************************************************/
   461	
   462	/****************************************************************************
   463	 * Low-level Helpers
   464	 ****************************************************************************/
   465	
   466	/****************************************************************************
   467	 * Name: lpc17_40_setclock
   468	 *
   469	 * Description:
   470	 *   Modify oft-changed bits in the CLKCR register.  Only the following bit-
   471	 *   fields are changed:
   472	 *
   473	 *   CLKDIV, PWRSAV, BYPASS, and WIDBUS
   474	 *
   475	 * Input Parameters:
   476	 *   clkcr - A new CLKCR setting for the above mentions bits (other bits
   477	 *           are ignored.
   478	 *
   479	 * Returned Value:
   480	 *   None
   481	 *
   482	 ****************************************************************************/
   483	
   484	static inline void lpc17_40_setclock(uint32_t clkcr)
   485	{
   486	  uint32_t regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
   487	
   488	  /* Clear CLKDIV, PWRSAV, BYPASS, and WIDBUS bits */
   489	
   490	  regval &= ~(SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKDIV_MASK | SDCARD_CLOCK_PWRSAV |
   491	              SDCARD_CLOCK_BYPASS | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS |
   492	              SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
   493	
   494	  /* Replace with user provided settings */
   495	
   496	  clkcr  &=  (SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKDIV_MASK | SDCARD_CLOCK_PWRSAV |
   497	              SDCARD_CLOCK_BYPASS | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS |
   498	              SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
   499	
   500	  regval |=  clkcr;
   501	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
   502	
   503	  mcinfo("CLKCR: %08" PRIx32 " PWR: %08" PRIx32 "\n",
   504	         getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK), getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR));
   505	}
   506	
   507	/****************************************************************************
   508	 * Name: lpc17_40_configwaitints
   509	 *
   510	 * Description:
   511	 *   Enable/disable SD card interrupts needed to support the wait function
   512	 *
   513	 * Input Parameters:
   514	 *   priv       - A reference to the SD card device state structure
   515	 *   waitmask   - The set of bits in the SD card MASK register to set
   516	 *   waitevents - Waited for events
   517	 *   wkupevent  - Wake-up events
   518	 *
   519	 * Returned Value:
   520	 *   None
   521	 *
   522	 ****************************************************************************/
   523	
   524	static void lpc17_40_configwaitints(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv,
   525	                                    uint32_t waitmask,
   526	                                    sdio_eventset_t waitevents,
   527	                                    sdio_eventset_t wkupevent)
   528	{
   529	  irqstate_t flags;
   530	
   531	  /* Save all of the data and set the new interrupt mask in one, atomic
   532	   * operation.
   533	   */
   534	
   535	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   536	  priv->waitevents = waitevents;
   537	  priv->wkupevent  = wkupevent;
   538	  priv->waitmask   = waitmask;
   539	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   540	  priv->xfrflags   = 0;
   541	#endif
   542	  putreg32(priv->xfrmask | priv->waitmask, LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
   543	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   544	}
   545	
   546	/****************************************************************************
   547	 * Name: lpc17_40_configxfrints
   548	 *
   549	 * Description:
   550	 *   Enable SD card interrupts needed to support the data transfer event
   551	 *
   552	 * Input Parameters:
   553	 *   priv    - A reference to the SD card device state structure
   554	 *   xfrmask - The set of bits in the SD card MASK register to set
   555	 *
   556	 * Returned Value:
   557	 *   None
   558	 *
   559	 ****************************************************************************/
   560	
   561	static void lpc17_40_configxfrints(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv,
   562	                                   uint32_t xfrmask)
   563	{
   564	  irqstate_t flags;
   565	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   566	  priv->xfrmask = xfrmask;
   567	  putreg32(priv->xfrmask | priv->waitmask, LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
   568	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   569	}
   570	
   571	/****************************************************************************
   572	 * Name: lpc17_40_setpwrctrl
   573	 *
   574	 * Description:
   575	 *   Change the PWRCTRL field of the SD card POWER register to turn the
   576	 *   SD card ON or OFF
   577	 *
   578	 * Input Parameters:
   579	 *   clkcr - A new PWRCTRL setting
   580	 *
   581	 * Returned Value:
   582	 *   None
   583	 *
   584	 ****************************************************************************/
   585	
   586	static void lpc17_40_setpwrctrl(uint32_t pwrctrl)
   587	{
   588	  uint32_t regval;
   589	
   590	  /* Set the new value of the PWRCTRL field of the PWR register.  Also, as a
   591	   * side-effect, clear the OPENDRAIN and ROD bits as well.
   592	   */
   593	
   594	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR);
   595	  regval &= ~(SDCARD_PWR_CTRL_MASK | SDCARD_PWR_OPENDRAIN | SDCARD_PWR_ROD);
   596	  regval |= pwrctrl;
   597	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR);
   598	}

   620	/****************************************************************************
   621	 * Name: lpc17_40_sdcard_sample
   622	 *
   623	 * Description:
   624	 *   Sample SD card registers
   625	 *
   626	 ****************************************************************************/
   627	
   628	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   629	static void lpc17_40_sdcard_sample(struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s *regs)
   630	{
   631	  regs->pwr     = (uint8_t)getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR);
   632	  regs->clkcr   = (uint16_t)getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
   633	  regs->dctrl   = (uint16_t)getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
   634	  regs->dtimer  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DTIMER);
   635	  regs->dlen    = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DLEN);
   636	  regs->dcount  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCOUNT);
   637	  regs->sta     = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS);
   638	  regs->mask    = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
   639	  regs->fifocnt = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFOCNT);
   640	}
   641	#endif

   643	/****************************************************************************
   644	 * Name: lpc17_40_sample
   645	 *
   646	 * Description:
   647	 *   Sample SD card/DMA registers
   648	 *
   649	 ****************************************************************************/
   650	
   651	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   652	static void lpc17_40_sample(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv, int index)
   653	{
   654	  struct lpc17_40_sampleregs_s *regs = &g_sampleregs[index];
   655	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_DMA) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA)
   656	  if (priv->dmamode)
   657	    {
   658	      lpc17_40_dmasample(priv->dma, &regs->dma);
   659	    }
   660	#endif
   661	
   662	  lpc17_40_sdcard_sample(&regs->sdcard);
   663	}
   664	#endif

   666	/****************************************************************************
   667	 * Name: lpc17_40_sdcard_dump
   668	 *
   669	 * Description:
   670	 *   Dump one register sample
   671	 *
   672	 ****************************************************************************/
   673	
   674	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   675	static void lpc17_40_sdcard_dump(struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s *regs,
   676	                                 const char *msg)
   677	{
   678	  mcinfo("SD Card Registers: %s\n", msg);
   679	  mcinfo("  POWER[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR,     regs->pwr);
   680	  mcinfo("  CLKCR[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK,   regs->clkcr);
   681	  mcinfo("  DCTRL[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL,   regs->dctrl);
   682	  mcinfo(" DTIMER[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DTIMER,  regs->dtimer);
   683	  mcinfo("   DLEN[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DLEN,    regs->dlen);
   684	  mcinfo(" DCOUNT[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCOUNT,  regs->dcount);
   685	  mcinfo("    STA[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS,  regs->sta);
   686	  mcinfo("   MASK[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0,   regs->mask);
   687	  mcinfo("FIFOCNT[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFOCNT, regs->fifocnt);
   688	}
   689	#endif
