    90	/****************************************************************************
    91	 * Name: nsh_initialize
    92	 *
    93	 * Description:
    94	 *   This interface is used to initialize the NuttShell (NSH).
    95	 *   nsh_initialize() should be called once during application start-up prior
    96	 *   to executing nsh_consolemain().
    97	 *
    98	 * Input Parameters:
    99	 *   None
   100	 *
   101	 * Returned Value:
   102	 *   None
   103	 *
   104	 ****************************************************************************/
   105	
   106	void nsh_initialize(void)
   107	{
   108	#if defined (CONFIG_NSH_SYMTAB)
   109	  struct boardioc_symtab_s symdesc;
   110	#endif
   111	#if defined(CONFIG_ETC_ROMFS) && !defined(CONFIG_NSH_DISABLESCRIPT)
   112	  FAR struct console_stdio_s *pstate;
   113	#endif
   114	
   115	  /* populate NSH prompt string */
   116	
   117	  nsh_update_prompt();
   118	
   119	#if defined(CONFIG_NSH_READLINE) && defined(CONFIG_READLINE_TABCOMPLETION)
   120	  /* Configure readline prompt */
   121	
   122	  readline_prompt(nsh_prompt());
   123	
   124	#  ifdef CONFIG_READLINE_HAVE_EXTMATCH
   125	  /* Set up for tab completion on NSH commands */
   126	
   127	  readline_extmatch(&g_nsh_extmatch);
   128	#  endif
   129	#endif
   130	
   131	#ifdef CONFIG_NSH_USBDEV_TRACE
   132	  /* Initialize any USB tracing options that were requested */
   133	
   134	  usbtrace_enable(TRACE_BITSET);
   135	#endif
   136	
   137	#if defined(CONFIG_NSH_SYMTAB)
   138	  /* Make sure that we are using our symbol table */
   139	
   140	  symdesc.symtab   = CONFIG_NSH_SYMTAB_ARRAYNAME;
   141	  symdesc.nsymbols = CONFIG_NSH_SYMTAB_COUNTNAME;
   142	
   143	  boardctl(BOARDIOC_APP_SYMTAB, (uintptr_t)&symdesc);
   144	#endif
   145	
   146	#if defined(CONFIG_ETC_ROMFS) && !defined(CONFIG_NSH_DISABLESCRIPT)
   147	  pstate = nsh_newconsole(false);
   148	
   149	  /* Execute the system init script */
   150	
   151	  nsh_sysinitscript(&pstate->cn_vtbl);
   152	#endif
   153	
   154	#ifdef CONFIG_NSH_NETINIT
   155	  /* Bring up the network */
   156	
   157	  netinit_bringup();
   158	#endif
   159	
   160	#if defined(CONFIG_ETC_ROMFS) && !defined(CONFIG_NSH_DISABLESCRIPT)
   161	  /* Execute the start-up script */
   162	
   163	  nsh_initscript(&pstate->cn_vtbl);
   164	  nsh_release(&pstate->cn_vtbl);
   165	#endif
   166	
   167	#if defined(CONFIG_NSH_TELNET) && !defined(CONFIG_NSH_DISABLE_TELNETSTART) && \
   168	  !defined(CONFIG_NETINIT_NETLOCAL)
   169	  /* If the Telnet console is selected as a front-end, then start the
   170	   * Telnet daemon UNLESS network initialization is deferred via
   171	   * CONFIG_NETINIT_NETLOCAL.  In that case, the telnet daemon must be
   172	   * started manually with the telnetd command after the network has
   173	   * been initialized
   174	   */
   175	
   176	  nsh_telnetstart(AF_UNSPEC);
   177	#endif
   178	
   179	#if defined(CONFIG_NSH_DROPBEAR) && \
   180	    !defined(CONFIG_NSH_DISABLE_DROPBEARSTART) && \
   181	    !defined(CONFIG_NETINIT_NETLOCAL)
   182	  /* If Dropbear is selected as an SSH front-end, then start the daemon
   183	   * UNLESS network initialization is deferred via CONFIG_NETINIT_NETLOCAL.
   184	   * In that case, Dropbear must be started manually with the dropbear
   185	   * command after the network has been initialized.
   186	   */
   187	
   188	  nsh_dropbearstart();
   189	#endif
   190	}

    52	/****************************************************************************
    53	 * Private Types

    56	struct fb_state_s
    57	{
    58	  int fd;
    59	  struct fb_videoinfo_s vinfo;
    60	  struct fb_planeinfo_s pinfo;
    61	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_OVERLAY
    62	  struct fb_overlayinfo_s oinfo;
    63	#endif
    64	  FAR void *fbmem;
    65	  FAR void *fbmem2;
    66	  FAR void *act_fbmem;
    67	  uint32_t mem2_yoffset;
    68	};

    74	static const char g_default_fbdev[] = CONFIG_EXAMPLES_FB_DEFAULTFB;

    76	/* Violet-Blue-Green-Yellow-Orange-Red */
    77	
    78	static const uint32_t g_rgb24[NRECTCOLORS] =
    79	{
    80	  RGB24_VIOLET, RGB24_BLUE, RGB24_GREEN,
    81	  RGB24_YELLOW, RGB24_ORANGE, RGB24_RED
    82	};
    83	
    84	static const uint16_t g_rgb16[NRECTCOLORS] =
    85	{
    86	  RGB16_VIOLET, RGB16_BLUE, RGB16_GREEN,
    87	  RGB16_YELLOW, RGB16_ORANGE, RGB16_RED
    88	};
    89	
    90	static const uint8_t g_rgb8[NRECTCOLORS] =
    91	{
    92	  RGB8_VIOLET, RGB8_BLUE, RGB8_GREEN,
    93	  RGB8_YELLOW, RGB8_ORANGE, RGB8_RED
    94	};
    95

    97

    99	{

   101	  RGB24_MAGENTA, RGB24_RED, RGB24_BLUE
   102	};

   107	  RGB16_MAGENTA, RGB16_RED, RGB16_BLUE

   123	/****************************************************************************
   124	 * Private Functions
   125	 ****************************************************************************/
   126	
   127	/****************************************************************************
   128	 * sync_area
   129	 ****************************************************************************/
   130	
   131	static void sync_area(FAR struct fb_state_s *state)
   132	{
   133	  if (state->fbmem2 == NULL)
   134	    {
   135	      return;
   136	    }
   137	
   138	  if (state->act_fbmem == state->fbmem)
   139	    {
   140	      memcpy(state->fbmem, state->fbmem2,
   141	             state->vinfo.yres * state->pinfo.stride);
   142	    }
   143	  else
   144	    {
   145	      memcpy(state->fbmem2, state->fbmem,
   146	             state->vinfo.yres * state->pinfo.stride);
   147	    }
   148	}
   149	
   150	/****************************************************************************
   151	 * pan_display
   152	 ****************************************************************************/
   153	
   154	static void pan_display(FAR struct fb_state_s *state)
   155	{
   156	  struct pollfd pfd;
   157	  int ret;
   158	
   159	  pfd.fd = state->fd;
   160	  pfd.events = POLLOUT;
   161	
   162	  ret = poll(&pfd, 1, 0);
   163	
   164	  if (ret > 0)
   165	    {
   166	      if (state->fbmem2 == NULL)
   167	        {
   168	          return;
   169	        }
   170	
   171	      if (state->act_fbmem == state->fbmem)
   172	        {
   173	          state->pinfo.yoffset = 0;
   174	        }
   175	      else
   176	        {
   177	          state->pinfo.yoffset = state->mem2_yoffset;
   178	        }
   179	
   180	      ioctl(state->fd, FBIOPAN_DISPLAY,
   181	            (unsigned long)(uintptr_t)&state->pinfo);
   182	
   183	      state->act_fbmem = state->act_fbmem == state->fbmem ?
   184	                         state->fbmem2 : state->fbmem;
   185	    }
   186	}
   187	
   188	/****************************************************************************
   189	 * fbdev_get_pinfo
   190	 ****************************************************************************/
   191	
   192	static int fbdev_get_pinfo(int fd, FAR struct fb_planeinfo_s *pinfo)
   193	{
   194	  if (ioctl(fd, FBIOGET_PLANEINFO, (unsigned long)((uintptr_t)pinfo)) < 0)
   195	    {
   196	      int errcode = errno;
   197	
   198	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: ioctl(FBIOGET_PLANEINFO) failed: %d\n",
   199	              errcode);
   200	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   201	    }
   202	
   203	  printf("PlaneInfo (plane %d):\n", pinfo->display);
   204	  printf("    fbmem: %p\n", pinfo->fbmem);
   205	  printf("    fblen: %zu\n", pinfo->fblen);
   206	  printf("   stride: %u\n", pinfo->stride);
   207	  printf("  display: %u\n", pinfo->display);
   208	  printf("      bpp: %u\n", pinfo->bpp);
   209	
   210	  /* Only these pixel depths are supported.  viinfo.fmt is ignored, only
   211	   * certain color formats are supported.
   212	   */
   213	
   214	  if (pinfo->bpp != 32 && pinfo->bpp != 24 &&
   215	      pinfo->bpp != 16 && pinfo->bpp != 8 &&
   216	      pinfo->bpp != 1)
   217	    {
   218	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: bpp=%u not supported\n", pinfo->bpp);
   219	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   220	    }
   221	
   222	  return 0;
   223	}
   224	
   225	/****************************************************************************
   226	 * fb_init_mem2
   227	 ****************************************************************************/
   228	
   229	static int fb_init_mem2(FAR struct fb_state_s *state)
   230	{
   231	  struct fb_planeinfo_s pinfo;
   232	  FAR void *mem2;
   233	  size_t framesize;
   234	  off_t offset;
   235	  int ret;
   236	
   237	  /* Get display[1] planeinfo */
   238	
   239	  memset(&pinfo, 0, sizeof(pinfo));
   240	  pinfo.display = state->pinfo.display + 1;
   241	
   242	  if ((ret = fbdev_get_pinfo(state->fd, &pinfo)) < 0)
   243	    {
   244	      return ret;
   245	    }
   246	
   247	  if (pinfo.bpp != state->pinfo.bpp)
   248	    {
   249	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: fbmem2 bpp mismatch\n");
   250	      return -EINVAL;
   251	    }
   252	
   253	  /* Use virtual offset = fblen = stride * yres */
   254	
   255	  framesize = (size_t)state->vinfo.yres * state->pinfo.stride;
   256	  offset = framesize;
   257	  state->mem2_yoffset = state->vinfo.yres;
   258	
   259	  mem2 = mmap(NULL, framesize,
   260	              PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE,
   261	              MAP_SHARED | MAP_FILE,
   262	              state->fd, offset);
   263	  if (mem2 == MAP_FAILED)
   264	    {
   265	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: mmap mem2 failed: %d\n", errno);
   266	      return -errno;
   267	    }
   268	
   269	  state->fbmem2 = mem2;
   270	  fprintf(stderr, "Mapped FB mem2: %p, yoffset: %" PRIu32
   271	          ", gap: %td (%s)\n",
   272	          state->fbmem2, state->mem2_yoffset,
   273	          (char *)mem2 - ((char *)state->fbmem + framesize),
   274	          ((char *)mem2 == (char *)state->fbmem + framesize) ?
   275	          "consecutive" : "non-consecutive");
   276	
   277	  return 0;
   278	}
   279	
   280	/****************************************************************************
   281	 * draw_rect
   282	 ****************************************************************************/
   283	
   284	static void draw_rect32(FAR struct fb_state_s *state,
   285	                        FAR struct fb_area_s *area, uint32_t color)
   286	{
   287	  FAR uint32_t *dest;
   288	  FAR uint8_t *row;
   289	  int x;
   290	  int y;
   291	
   292	  row = (FAR uint8_t *)state->act_fbmem + state->pinfo.stride * area->y;
   293	  for (y = 0; y < area->h; y++)
   294	    {
   295	      dest = ((FAR uint32_t *)row) + area->x;
   296	      for (x = 0; x < area->w; x++)
   297	        {
   298	          *dest++ = color | 0xff000000;
   299	        }
   300	
   301	      row += state->pinfo.stride;
   302	    }
   303	}
   304	
   305	static void draw_rect24(FAR struct fb_state_s *state,
   306	                        FAR struct fb_area_s *area, uint32_t color)
   307	{
   308	  FAR uint8_t *dest;
   309	  FAR uint8_t *row;
   310	  int x;
   311	  int y;
   312	
   313	  row = (FAR uint8_t *)state->act_fbmem + state->pinfo.stride * area->y;
   314	  for (y = 0; y < area->h; y++)
   315	    {
   316	      dest = ((FAR uint8_t *)row) + area->x * 3;
   317	      for (x = 0; x < area->w; x++)
   318	        {
   319	          *dest++ = color & 0xff;
   320	          *dest++ = (color >> 8) & 0xff;
   321	          *dest++ = (color >> 16) & 0xff;
   322	        }
   323	
   324	      row += state->pinfo.stride;
   325	    }
   326	}
   327	
   328	static void draw_rect16(FAR struct fb_state_s *state,
   329	                        FAR struct fb_area_s *area, uint16_t color)
   330	{
   331	  FAR uint16_t *dest;
   332	  FAR uint8_t *row;
   333	  int x;
   334	  int y;
   335	
   336	  row = (FAR uint8_t *)state->act_fbmem + state->pinfo.stride * area->y;
   337	  for (y = 0; y < area->h; y++)
   338	    {
   339	      dest = ((FAR uint16_t *)row) + area->x;
   340	      for (x = 0; x < area->w; x++)
   341	        {
   342	          *dest++ = color;
   343	        }
   344	
   345	      row += state->pinfo.stride;
   346	    }
   347	}
   348	
   349	static void draw_rect8(FAR struct fb_state_s *state,
   350	                       FAR struct fb_area_s *area, uint8_t color)
   351	{
   352	  FAR uint8_t *dest;
   353	  FAR uint8_t *row;
   354	  int x;
   355	  int y;
   356	
   357	  row = (FAR uint8_t *)state->act_fbmem + state->pinfo.stride * area->y;
   358	  for (y = 0; y < area->h; y++)
   359	    {
   360	      dest = row + area->x;
   361	      for (x = 0; x < area->w; x++)
   362	        {
   363	          *dest++ = color;
   364	        }
   365	
   366	      row += state->pinfo.stride;
   367	    }
   368	}
   369	
   370	static void draw_rect1(FAR struct fb_state_s *state,
   371	                       FAR struct fb_area_s *area, uint8_t color)
   372	{
   373	  FAR uint8_t *pixel;
   374	  FAR uint8_t *row;
   375	  uint8_t color8 = color == 0 ? 0 : 0xff;
   376	
   377	  int start_full_x;
   378	  int end_full_x;
   379	  int start_bit_shift;
   380	  int last_bits;
   381	  uint8_t lmask;
   382	  uint8_t rmask;
   383	  int y;
   384	
   385	  /* Calculate the framebuffer address of the first row to draw on */
   386	
   387	  row    = (FAR uint8_t *)state->act_fbmem + state->pinfo.stride * area->y;
   388	
   389	  /* Calculate the position of the first complete (with all bits) byte.
   390	   * Then calculate the last byte with all the bits.
   391	   */
   392	
   393	  start_full_x = ((area->x + 7) >> 3);
   394	  end_full_x   = ((area->x + area->w) >> 3);
   395	
   396	  /* Calculate the number of bits in byte before start that need to remain
   397	   * unchanged. Later calculate the mask.
   398	   */
   399	
   400	  start_bit_shift = 8 + area->x - (start_full_x << 3);
   401	  lmask = 0xff >> start_bit_shift;
   402	
   403	  /* Calculate the number of bits that needs to be changed after last byte
   404	   * with all the bits. Later calculate the mask.
   405	   */
   406	
   407	  last_bits = area->x + area->w - (end_full_x << 3);
   408	  rmask = 0xff << (8 - last_bits);
   409	
   410	  /* Calculate a mask on the first and last bytes of the sequence that may
   411	   * not be completely filled with pixel.
   412	   */
   413	
   414	  /* Now draw each row, one-at-a-time */
   415	
   416	  for (y = 0; y < area->h; y++)
   417	    {
   418	      /* 'pixel' points to the 1st pixel the next row */
   419	
   420	      /* Special case: The row starts and ends within the same byte */
   421	
   422	      if (start_full_x > end_full_x)
   423	        {
   424	          pixel = row + start_full_x - 1;
   425	          *pixel = (*pixel & (~lmask | ~rmask)) | (lmask & rmask & color8);
   426	          continue;
   427	        }
   428	
   429	      if (start_bit_shift != 0)
   430	        {
   431	          pixel = row + start_full_x - 1;
   432	          *pixel = (*pixel & ~lmask) | (lmask & color8);
   433	        }
   434	
   435	      if (end_full_x > start_full_x)
   436	        {
   437	          pixel = row + start_full_x;
   438	          memset(pixel, color8, end_full_x - start_full_x);
   439	        }
   440	
   441	      if (last_bits != 0)
   442	        {
   443	          pixel = row + end_full_x;
   444	          *pixel = (*pixel & ~rmask) | (rmask & color8);
   445	        }
   446	
   447	      row += state->pinfo.stride;
   448	    }
   449	}
   450	
   451	static void draw_rect(FAR struct fb_state_s *state,
   452	                      FAR struct fb_area_s *area, uint32_t color24,
   453	                      uint16_t color16, uint8_t color8, uint8_t mono)
   454	{
   455	  switch (state->pinfo.bpp)
   456	    {
   457	      case 32:
   458	        draw_rect32(state, area, color24);
   459	        break;
   460	
   461	      case 24:
   462	        draw_rect24(state, area, color24);
   463	        break;
   464	
   465	      case 16:
   466	        draw_rect16(state, area, color16);
   467	        break;
   468	
   469	      case 8:
   470	      default:
   471	        draw_rect8(state, area, color8);
   472	        break;
   473	
   474	      case 1:
   475	        draw_rect1(state, area, mono);
   476	        break;
   477	    }
   478	}
   479	
   480	static void present_area(FAR struct fb_state_s *state,
   481	                         FAR const struct fb_area_s *area)
   482	{
   483	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_UPDATE
   484	  struct fb_area_s update = *area;
   485	  int ret;
   486	
   487	  int yoffset = state->act_fbmem == state->fbmem ?
   488	                0 : state->mem2_yoffset;
   489	
   490	  update.y += yoffset;
   491	
   492	  ret = ioctl(state->fd, FBIO_UPDATE,
   493	              (unsigned long)((uintptr_t)&update));
   494	  if (ret < 0)
   495	    {
   496	      int errcode = errno;
   497	
   498	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: ioctl(FBIO_UPDATE) failed: %d\n",
   499	              errcode);
   500	    }
   501	#endif
   502	
   503	  if (state->pinfo.yres_virtual == (state->vinfo.yres * 2))
   504	    {
   505	      pan_display(state);
   506	    }
   507	}
   508	
   509	/****************************************************************************
   510	 * Public Functions
   511	 ****************************************************************************/
   512	
   513	/****************************************************************************
   514	 * fb_main
   515	 ****************************************************************************/
   516	
   517	int main(int argc, FAR char *argv[])
   518	{
   519	  FAR const char *fbdev = g_default_fbdev;
   520	  int pattern = PATTERN_RECTANGLES;
   521	  struct fb_state_s state;
   522	  struct fb_area_s area;
   523	  int nsteps;
   524	  int xstep;
   525	  int ystep;
   526	  int width;
   527	  int height;
   528	  int color;
   529	  int x;
   530	  int y;
   531	  int ret;
   532	  int opt;
   533	
   534	  while ((opt = getopt(argc, argv, "p:")) != ERROR)
   535	    {
   536	      switch (opt)
   537	        {
   538	          case 'p':
   539	            if (strcmp(optarg, "rectangles") == 0)
   540	              {
   541	                pattern = PATTERN_RECTANGLES;
   542	              }
   543	            else if (strcmp(optarg, "smpte") == 0)
   544	              {
   545	                pattern = PATTERN_SMPTE;
   546	              }
   547	            else
   548	              {
   549	                fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Unknown pattern: %s\n", optarg);
   550	                goto usage;
   551	              }
   552	
   553	            break;
   554	
   555	          default:
   556	            goto usage;
   557	        }
   558	    }
   559	
   560	  if (optind < argc)
   561	    {
   562	      fbdev = argv[optind++];
   563	    }
   564	
   565	  if (optind < argc)
   566	    {
   567	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Too many framebuffer paths\n");
   568	      goto usage;
   569	    }
   570	
   571	  /* Open the framebuffer driver */
   572	
   573	  memset(&state, 0, sizeof(state));
   574	  state.fd = open(fbdev, O_RDWR);
   575	  if (state.fd < 0)
   576	    {
   577	      int errcode = errno;
   578	
   579	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: Failed to open %s: %d\n", fbdev, errcode);
   580	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   581	    }
   582	
   583	  /* Get the characteristics of the framebuffer */
   584	
   585	  ret = ioctl(state.fd, FBIOGET_VIDEOINFO,
   586	              (unsigned long)((uintptr_t)&state.vinfo));
   587	  if (ret < 0)
   588	    {
   589	      int errcode = errno;
   590	
   591	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: ioctl(FBIOGET_VIDEOINFO) failed: %d\n",
   592	              errcode);
   593	      close(state.fd);
   594	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   595	    }
   596	
   597	  printf("VideoInfo:\n");
   598	  printf("      fmt: %u\n", state.vinfo.fmt);
   599	  printf("     xres: %u\n", state.vinfo.xres);
   600	  printf("     yres: %u\n", state.vinfo.yres);
   601	  printf("  nplanes: %u\n", state.vinfo.nplanes);
   602	
   603	#ifdef CONFIG_FB_OVERLAY
   604	  printf("noverlays: %u\n", state.vinfo.noverlays);
   605	
   606	  /* Select the first overlay, which should be the composed framebuffer */
   607	
   608	  ret = ioctl(state.fd, FBIO_SELECT_OVERLAY, 0);
   609	  if (ret < 0)
   610	    {
   611	      int errcode = errno;
   612	
   613	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: ioctl(FBIO_SELECT_OVERLAY) failed: %d\n",
   614	              errcode);
   615	      close(state.fd);
   616	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   617	    }
   618	
   619	  /* Get the first overlay information */
   620	
   621	  state.oinfo.overlay = 0;
   622	  ret = ioctl(state.fd, FBIOGET_OVERLAYINFO,
   623	                        (unsigned long)((uintptr_t)&state.oinfo));
   624	  if (ret < 0)
   625	    {
   626	      int errcode = errno;
   627	
   628	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: ioctl(FBIOGET_OVERLAYINFO) failed: %d\n",
   629	              errcode);
   630	      close(state.fd);
   631	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   632	    }
   633	
   634	  printf("OverlayInfo (overlay 0):\n");
   635	  printf("    fbmem: %p\n", state.oinfo.fbmem);
   636	  printf("    fblen: %zu\n", state.oinfo.fblen);
   637	  printf("   stride: %u\n", state.oinfo.stride);
   638	  printf("  overlay: %u\n", state.oinfo.overlay);
   639	  printf("      bpp: %u\n", state.oinfo.bpp);
   640	  printf("    blank: %u\n", state.oinfo.blank);
   641	  printf("chromakey: 0x%08" PRIx32 "\n", state.oinfo.chromakey);
   642	  printf("    color: 0x%08" PRIx32 "\n", state.oinfo.color);
   643	  printf("   transp: 0x%02x\n", state.oinfo.transp.transp);
   644	  printf("     mode: %u\n", state.oinfo.transp.transp_mode);
   645	  printf("     area: (%u,%u) => (%u,%u)\n",
   646	                      state.oinfo.sarea.x, state.oinfo.sarea.y,
   647	                      state.oinfo.sarea.w, state.oinfo.sarea.h);
   648	  printf("     accl: %" PRIu32 "\n", state.oinfo.accl);
   649	
   650	  /* select default framebuffer layer */
   651	
   652	  ret = ioctl(state.fd, FBIO_SELECT_OVERLAY, FB_NO_OVERLAY);
   653	  if (ret < 0)
   654	    {
   655	      int errcode = errno;
   656	
   657	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: ioctl(FBIO_SELECT_OVERLAY) failed: %d\n",
   658	              errcode);
   659	      close(state.fd);
   660	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   661	    }
   662	
   663	#endif
   664	
   665	  if ((ret = fbdev_get_pinfo(state.fd, &state.pinfo)) < 0)
   666	    {
   667	      close(state.fd);
   668	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   669	    }
   670	
   671	  /* mmap() the framebuffer.
   672	   *
   673	   * NOTE: In the FLAT build the frame buffer address returned by the
   674	   * FBIOGET_PLANEINFO IOCTL command will be the same as the framebuffer
   675	   * address.  mmap(), however, is the preferred way to get the framebuffer
   676	   * address because in the KERNEL build, it will perform the necessary
   677	   * address mapping to make the memory accessible to the application.
   678	   */
   679	
   680	  state.fbmem = mmap(NULL, state.pinfo.fblen, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE,
   681	                     MAP_SHARED | MAP_FILE, state.fd, 0);
   682	  if (state.fbmem == MAP_FAILED)
   683	    {
   684	      int errcode = errno;
   685	
   686	      fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: mmap() failed: %d\n",
   687	              errcode);
   688	      close(state.fd);
   689	      return EXIT_FAILURE;
   690	    }
   691	
   692	  printf("Mapped FB mem: %p\n", state.fbmem);
   693	
   694	  /* double buffer mode */
   695	
   696	  if (state.pinfo.yres_virtual == (state.vinfo.yres * 2))
   697	    {
   698	      if ((ret = fb_init_mem2(&state)) < 0)
   699	        {
   700	          munmap(state.fbmem, state.pinfo.fblen);
   701	          close(state.fd);
   702	          return ret;
   703	        }
   704	    }
   705	
   706	  /* Draw the selected pattern */
   707	
   708	  state.act_fbmem = state.fbmem;
   709	  if (pattern == PATTERN_SMPTE)
   710	    {
   711	      for (color = 0; color < NSMPTECOLORS; color++)
   712	        {
   713	          area.x = color * state.vinfo.xres / NSMPTECOLORS;
   714	          area.y = 0;
   715	          area.w = (color + 1) * state.vinfo.xres / NSMPTECOLORS -
   716	                   area.x;
   717	          area.h = state.vinfo.yres;
   718	
   719	          draw_rect(&state, &area, g_smpte_rgb24[color],
   720	                    g_smpte_rgb16[color], g_smpte_rgb8[color],
   721	                    g_smpte_mono[color]);
   722	        }
   723	
   724	      area.x = 0;
   725	      area.y = 0;
   726	      area.w = state.vinfo.xres;
   727	      area.h = state.vinfo.yres;
   728	      present_area(&state, &area);
   729	    }
   730	  else
   731	    {
   732	      nsteps = 2 * (NRECTCOLORS - 1) + 1;
   733	      xstep  = state.vinfo.xres / nsteps;
   734	      ystep  = state.vinfo.yres / nsteps;
   735	      width  = state.vinfo.xres;
   736	      height = state.vinfo.yres;
   737	
   738	      for (x = 0, y = 0, color = 0;
   739	           color < NRECTCOLORS;
   740	           x += xstep, y += ystep, color++)
   741	        {
   742	          area.x = x;
   743	          area.y = y;
   744	          area.w = width;
   745	          area.h = height;
   746	
   747	          printf("%2d: (%3d,%3d) (%3d,%3d)\n",
   748	                 color, area.x, area.y, area.w, area.h);
   749	
   750	          draw_rect(&state, &area, g_rgb24[color],
   751	                    g_rgb16[color], g_rgb8[color], color & 1);
   752	          present_area(&state, &area);
   753	          usleep(500 * 1000);
   754	
   755	          width  -= (2 * xstep);
   756	          height -= (2 * ystep);
   757	
   758	          /* double buffer mode */
   759	
   760	          if (state.pinfo.yres_virtual == (state.vinfo.yres * 2))
   761	            {
   762	              sync_area(&state);
   763	            }
   764	        }
   765	    }
   766	
   767	  printf("Test finished\n");
   768	  ret = EXIT_SUCCESS;
   769	
   770	  munmap(state.fbmem, state.pinfo.fblen);
   771	  munmap(state.fbmem2, state.pinfo.fblen);
   772	  close(state.fd);
   773	  return ret;
   774	
   775	usage:
   776	  fprintf(stderr,
   777	          "USAGE: %s [-p rectangles|smpte] [<fb-driver-path>]\n",
   778	          argv[0]);
   779	  return EXIT_FAILURE;
   780	}
